158: 
159: fn project_error(error: ProjectError) -> MutationError {
160:     match error {
161:         ProjectError::Cancelled => MutationError::Cancelled,
162:         ProjectError::Rejected(OperationalErrorCode::SandboxDenied) => {
163:             MutationError::PermissionDenied
164:         }
165:         ProjectError::Rejected(OperationalErrorCode::UnsupportedPlatform) => {
166:             MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform
167:         }
168:         ProjectError::Rejected(OperationalErrorCode::OutputLimitExceeded) => {
169:             MutationError::LimitExceeded
170:         }
171:         ProjectError::Rejected(
172:             OperationalErrorCode::InvalidProject | OperationalErrorCode::ProjectNotFound,
173:         ) => MutationError::Conflict,
174:         _ => MutationError::Io,
175:     }
176: }
177: 
178: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
179: struct StoreNode(i32, u64);
180: 
181: fn store_node(fd: &impl AsFd) -> Result<StoreNode, MutationError> {
182:     let stat = fstat(fd).map_err(mutation_io)?;
183:     Ok(StoreNode(stat.st_dev, stat.st_ino))
184: }
185: 
186: fn require_private_directory(fd: &impl AsFd) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
187:     let stat = fstat(fd).map_err(mutation_io)?;
188:     if FileType::from_raw_mode(stat.st_mode) != FileType::Directory
189:         || stat.st_uid != rustix::process::geteuid().as_raw()
190:         || stat.st_mode & 0o7777 != 0o700
191:     {
192:         return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
193:     }
194:     Ok(())
195: }
196: 
197: type PrivateStamp = (StoreNode, usize, (i64, i64), (i64, i64));
198: type XattrSnapshot = Vec<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>)>;
199: 
200: fn require_private_file(fd: &impl AsFd, max: usize) -> Result<PrivateStamp, MutationError> {
201:     let stat = fstat(fd).map_err(mutation_io)?;
202:     if FileType::from_raw_mode(stat.st_mode) != FileType::RegularFile
203:         || stat.st_nlink != 1
204:         || stat.st_uid != rustix::process::geteuid().as_raw()
205:         || stat.st_mode & 0o7777 != 0o600
206:     {
207:         return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
208:     }
209:     if stat.st_size < 0 || stat.st_size as u64 > max as u64 {
210:         return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
211:     }
212:     Ok((
213:         StoreNode(stat.st_dev, stat.st_ino),
214:         stat.st_size as usize,
215:         (stat.st_mtime, stat.st_mtime_nsec),
216:         (stat.st_ctime, stat.st_ctime_nsec),
217:     ))
218: }
219: 
220: fn xattrs(fd: &impl AsFd) -> Result<XattrSnapshot, MutationError> {
221:     let mut names = Vec::with_capacity(MAX_XATTR_NAME_BYTES);
222:     flistxattr(fd, spare_capacity(&mut names)).map_err(|error| {
223:         if error == Errno::RANGE {
224:             MutationError::LimitExceeded
225:         } else {
226:             mutation_io(error)
227:         }
228:     })?;
229:     let mut total = names.len();
230:     let mut attributes = Vec::new();
231:     for name in names
232:         .split(|byte| *byte == 0)
233:         .filter(|name| !name.is_empty())
234:     {
235:         let mut value = Vec::with_capacity(MAX_XATTR_VALUE_BYTES);
236:         fgetxattr(fd, name, spare_capacity(&mut value)).map_err(|error| {
237:             if error == Errno::RANGE {
238:                 MutationError::LimitExceeded
239:             } else {
240:                 mutation_io(error)
241:             }
242:         })?;
243:         total = total
244:             .checked_add(value.len())
245:             .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
246:         if total > MAX_XATTR_TOTAL_BYTES {
247:             return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
248:         }
249:         attributes.push((name.to_vec(), value));
250:     }
251:     Ok(attributes)
252: }
253: 
254: struct StateRoot {
255:     slash: OwnedFd,
256:     directory: OwnedFd,
257:     path: PathBuf,
258:     node: StoreNode,
259: }
260: 
261: impl StateRoot {
262:     fn open(path: &Path) -> Result<Self, MutationError> {
263:         let path = checked_path(path).map_err(project_error)?;
264:         if path == Path::new("/") {
265:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
266:         }
267:         let slash = openat(
268:             CWD,
269:             "/",
270:             OFlags::RDONLY | OFlags::DIRECTORY | OFlags::CLOEXEC | OFlags::NOFOLLOW,
271:             Mode::empty(),
272:         )
273:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
274:         let directory = openat(
275:             &slash,
276:             path.strip_prefix("/").map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?,
277:             flags(true),
278:             Mode::empty(),
279:         )
280:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
281:         require_apfs(&directory).map_err(project_error)?;
282:         require_private_directory(&directory)?;
283:         let state = Self {
284:             slash,
285:             node: store_node(&directory)?,
286:             directory,
287:             path,
288:         };
289:         state.check()?;
290:         Ok(state)
291:     }
292: 
293:     fn check(&self) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
294:         let current = openat(
295:             &self.slash,
296:             self.path
297:                 .strip_prefix("/")
298:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?,
299:             flags(true),
300:             Mode::empty(),
301:         )
302:         .map_err(|_| MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
303:         for fd in [&current, &self.directory] {
304:             require_private_directory(fd)?;
305:             if store_node(fd)? != self.node {
306:                 return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
307:             }
308:         }
309:         Ok(())
310:     }
311: 
312:     fn durable(&self) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
313:         self.check()?;
314:         fsync(&self.directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
315:         fcntl_fullfsync(&self.directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
316:         self.check()
317:     }
318: 
319:     fn open_lock(&self, name: &str) -> Result<OwnedFd, MutationError> {
320:         self.check()?;
321:         let lock = openat(
322:             &self.directory,
323:             name,
324:             flags(false) | OFlags::RDWR | OFlags::CREATE,
325:             Mode::RUSR | Mode::WUSR,
326:         )
327:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
328:         require_private_file(&lock, 0)?;
329:         self.check()?;
330:         Ok(lock)
331:     }
332: 
333:     fn lock(&self, name: &str) -> Result<OwnedFd, MutationError> {
334:         let lock = self.open_lock(name)?;
335:         flock(&lock, FlockOperation::NonBlockingLockExclusive).map_err(mutation_io)?;
336:         self.check()?;
337:         Ok(lock)
338:     }
339: 
340:     fn read_optional(&self, name: &str) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, MutationError> {
341:         self.check()?;
342:         let fd = match openat(&self.directory, name, flags(false), Mode::empty()) {
343:             Ok(fd) => fd,
344:             Err(Errno::NOENT) => return Ok(None),
345:             Err(error) => return Err(mutation_io(error)),
346:         };
347:         let before = require_private_file(&fd, MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES)?;
348:         let mut file = File::from(fd);
349:         let mut bytes = Vec::new();
350:         (&mut file)
351:             .take(MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES as u64 + 1)
352:             .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
353:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
354:         if bytes.len() > MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES
355:             || bytes.len() != before.1
356:             || require_private_file(&file, MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES)? != before
357:         {
358:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
359:         }
360:         self.check()?;
361:         Ok(Some(bytes))
362:     }
363: 
364:     fn write_new(
365:         &self,
366:         name: &str,
367:         bytes: &[u8],
368:         phase: JournalPhase,
369:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
370:         if bytes.len() > MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES {
371:             return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
372:         }
373:         self.check()?;
374:         let fd = openat(
375:             &self.directory,
376:             name,
377:             flags(false) | OFlags::WRONLY | OFlags::CREATE | OFlags::EXCL,
378:             Mode::RUSR | Mode::WUSR,
379:         )
380:         .map_err(|error| {
381:             if error == Errno::EXIST {
382:                 MutationError::RecoveryRequired
383:             } else {
384:                 mutation_io(error)
385:             }
386:         })?;
387:         let mut file = File::from(fd);
388:         require_private_file(&file, 0)?;
389:         write_all_with_test_fault(&mut file, bytes, IoFaultPoint::JournalWrite(phase))?;
390:         fsync(&file).map_err(mutation_io)?;
391:         fcntl_fullfsync(&file).map_err(mutation_io)?;
392:         if require_private_file(&file, MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES)?.1 != bytes.len() {
393:             return Err(MutationError::Io);
394:         }
395:         self.durable()
396:     }
397: }
398: 
399: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
400: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
401: struct JournalFile {
402:     path: String,
403:     bytes: String,
404: }

406: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
407: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
408: struct JournalBundle {
409:     directories: Vec<String>,
410:     files: Vec<JournalFile>,
411: }
412: 
413: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
414: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
415: struct JournalNode {
416:     device: i32,
417:     inode: u64,
418: }
419: 
420: impl From<Node> for JournalNode {
421:     fn from(node: Node) -> Self {
422:         Self {
423:             device: node.device,
424:             inode: node.inode,
425:         }
426:     }
427: }
428: 
429: impl From<&SourceBundle> for JournalBundle {
430:     fn from(bundle: &SourceBundle) -> Self {
431:         Self {
432:             directories: bundle.directories().to_vec(),
433:             files: bundle
434:                 .files()
435:                 .iter()
436:                 .map(|file| JournalFile {
437:                     path: file.path().to_owned(),
438:                     bytes: STANDARD.encode(file.bytes()),
439:                 })
440:                 .collect(),
441:         }
442:     }
443: }
444: 
445: impl JournalBundle {
446:     fn decode(&self) -> Result<SourceBundle, MutationError> {
447:         let files = self
448:             .files
449:             .iter()
450:             .map(|file| {
451:                 let bytes = STANDARD
452:                     .decode(&file.bytes)
453:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
454:                 SourceFile::new(file.path.clone(), bytes)
455:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
456:             })
457:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
458:         SourceBundle::with_directories(files, self.directories.clone())
459:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
460:     }
461: }
462: 
463: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
464: #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
465: enum JournalPhase {
466:     Prepared,
467:     Scratch,
468:     Staged,
469:     Applying,
470:     Published,
471:     AbortedCleanup,
472:     Committed,
473:     NoChange,
474:     Aborted,
475:     RecoveryRequired,
476: }
477: 
478: impl JournalPhase {
479:     fn rank(self) -> u8 {
480:         match self {
481:             Self::Prepared => 0,
482:             Self::Scratch => 1,
483:             Self::Staged => 2,
484:             Self::Applying => 3,
485:             Self::Published
486:             | Self::AbortedCleanup
487:             | Self::Committed
488:             | Self::NoChange
489:             | Self::Aborted
490:             | Self::RecoveryRequired => 4,
491:         }
492:     }
493: }
494: 
495: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
496: enum JournalFormat {
497:     V1,
498:     #[default]
499:     V2,
500: }
501: 
502: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
503: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
504: struct JournalMutationFile {
505:     path: String,
506:     temp_path: String,
507:     source_node: JournalNode,
508:     staged_node: Option<JournalNode>,
509: }
510: 
511: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
512: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
513: struct JournalBody {
514:     id: String,
515:     digest: String,
516:     key: String,
517:     operation: String,
518:     workspace_path: String,
519:     workspace_device: i32,
520:     workspace_inode: u64,
521:     files: Vec<JournalMutationFile>,
522:     validation: String,
523:     sequence: u64,
524:     phase: JournalPhase,
525:     before: JournalBundle,
526:     after: JournalBundle,
527:     legacy_v1: bool,
528:     #[serde(skip)]
529:     format: JournalFormat,
530: }
531: 
532: impl JournalBody {
533:     fn same_binding(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
534:         self.id == other.id
535:             && self.digest == other.digest
536:             && self.key == other.key
537:             && self.operation == other.operation
538:             && self.workspace_path == other.workspace_path
539:             && self.workspace_device == other.workspace_device
540:             && self.workspace_inode == other.workspace_inode
541:             && self.files.len() == other.files.len()
542:             && self.files.iter().zip(&other.files).all(|(current, next)| {
543:                 current.path == next.path
544:                     && current.temp_path == next.temp_path
545:                     && current.source_node == next.source_node
546:                     && (current.staged_node == next.staged_node
547:                         || (current.staged_node.is_none() && next.staged_node.is_some()))
548:             })
549:             && self.validation == other.validation
550:             && self.before == other.before
551:             && self.after == other.after
552:     }
553: }
554: 
555: fn require_newer_staging(
556:     final_body: &JournalBody,
557:     staging_body: &JournalBody,
558: ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
559:     if !final_body.same_binding(staging_body)
560:         || staging_body.sequence <= final_body.sequence
561:         || staging_body.phase.rank() < final_body.phase.rank()
562:     {
563:         return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
564:     }
565:     Ok(())
566: }
567:

571:     format: String,
572:     checksum: String,
573:     body: JournalBody,
574: }

578:     format: &'static str,
579:     checksum: &'a str,
580:     body: &'a JournalBody,
581: }

584: struct JournalFormatProbe {

586: }

588: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
589: #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
590: enum LegacyJournalPhaseV1 {
591:     Prepared,
592:     Scratch,
593:     Staged,
594:     Published,
595:     AbortedCleanup,
596:     Committed,
597:     NoChange,
598:     Aborted,
599:     RecoveryRequired,
600: }

602: #[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
603: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
604: struct LegacyJournalBodyV1 {
605:     id: String,
606:     digest: String,
607:     key: String,
608:     operation: String,
609:     workspace_path: String,
610:     workspace_device: i32,
611:     workspace_inode: u64,
612:     temp_path: String,
613:     source_node: JournalNode,
614:     staged_node: Option<JournalNode>,
615:     validation: String,
616:     sequence: u64,
617:     phase: LegacyJournalPhaseV1,
618:     before: JournalBundle,
619:     after: JournalBundle,
620: }
621: 
622: #[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
623: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
624: struct LegacyJournalRecordV1 {
625:     format: String,
626:     checksum: String,
627:     body: LegacyJournalBodyV1,
628: }
629: 
630: fn sha256(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
631:     let mut encoded = String::from("sha256:");
632:     for byte in Sha256::digest(bytes) {
633:         use std::fmt::Write;
634:         // Writing into a String cannot fail; preserve the typed I/O surface anyway.
635:         let _ = write!(&mut encoded, "{byte:02x}");
636:     }
637:     encoded
638: }
639: 
640: struct Sha256Writer(Sha256);
641: 
642: impl Write for Sha256Writer {
643:     fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
644:         self.0.update(bytes);
645:         Ok(bytes.len())
646:     }
647: 
648:     fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
649:         Ok(())
650:     }
651: }
652: 
653: #[derive(Default)]
654: struct CountingWriter {
655:     bytes: usize,
656: }
657: 
658: impl Write for CountingWriter {
659:     fn write(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) -> std::io::Result<usize> {
660:         self.bytes = self
661:             .bytes
662:             .checked_add(bytes.len())
663:             .ok_or_else(|| std::io::Error::other("journal length overflow"))?;
664:         Ok(bytes.len())
665:     }
666: 
667:     fn flush(&mut self) -> std::io::Result<()> {
668:         Ok(())
669:     }
670: }
671: 
672: fn canonical_checksum(value: &impl Serialize) -> Result<String, MutationError> {
673:     let mut writer = Sha256Writer(Sha256::new());
674:     serde_json::to_writer(&mut writer, value).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
675:     let mut encoded = String::from("sha256:");
676:     for byte in writer.0.finalize() {
677:         use std::fmt::Write;
678:         // Writing into a String cannot fail; preserve the typed I/O surface anyway.
679:         let _ = write!(&mut encoded, "{byte:02x}");
680:     }
681:     Ok(encoded)
682: }
683: 
684: fn borrowed_record<'a>(body: &'a JournalBody, checksum: &'a str) -> BorrowedJournalRecordV2<'a> {
685:     BorrowedJournalRecordV2 {
686:         format: "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2",
687:         checksum,
688:         body,
689:     }
690: }
691: 
692: fn encode(body: &JournalBody) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MutationError> {
693:     let checksum = canonical_checksum(body)?;
694:     let record = borrowed_record(body, &checksum);
695:     let bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&record).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
696:     if bytes.len() > MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES {
697:         return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
698:     }
699:     Ok(bytes)
700: }
701: 
702: fn worst_case_record_len(body: &JournalBody) -> Result<usize, MutationError> {
703:     let mut worst_case = body.clone();
704:     worst_case.sequence = u64::MAX;
705:     worst_case.phase = JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired;
706:     for file in &mut worst_case.files {
707:         file.staged_node = Some(JournalNode {
708:             device: i32::MIN,
709:             inode: u64::MAX,
710:         });
711:     }
712:     let checksum = canonical_checksum(&worst_case)?;
713:     let record = borrowed_record(&worst_case, &checksum);
714:     let mut writer = CountingWriter::default();
715:     serde_json::to_writer(&mut writer, &record).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
716:     if writer.bytes > MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES {
717:         return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
718:     }
719:     Ok(writer.bytes)
720: }
721: 
722: fn decode_envelope(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<JournalBody, MutationError> {
723:     if bytes.len() > MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES {
724:         return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
725:     }
726:     let probe: JournalFormatProbe =
727:         serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
728:     let body = match probe.format.as_str() {
729:         "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2" => {
730:             let record: JournalRecordV2 =
731:                 serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
732:             if record.checksum
733:                 != canonical_checksum(&record.body).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
734:             {
735:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
736:             }
737:             record.body
738:         }
739:         "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v1" => {
740:             let record: LegacyJournalRecordV1 =
741:                 serde_json::from_slice(bytes).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
742:             if record.checksum
743:                 != canonical_checksum(&record.body).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
744:                 || record.body.operation != "manifest_patch"
745:             {
746:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
747:             }
748:             let phase = match record.body.phase {
749:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Prepared => JournalPhase::Prepared,
750:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Scratch => JournalPhase::Scratch,
751:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Staged => JournalPhase::Staged,
752:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Published => JournalPhase::Published,
753:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::AbortedCleanup => JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup,
754:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Committed => JournalPhase::Committed,
755:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::NoChange => JournalPhase::NoChange,
756:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Aborted => JournalPhase::Aborted,
757:                 LegacyJournalPhaseV1::RecoveryRequired => JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired,
758:             };
759:             JournalBody {
760:                 id: record.body.id,
761:                 digest: record.body.digest,
762:                 key: record.body.key,
763:                 operation: record.body.operation,
764:                 workspace_path: record.body.workspace_path,
765:                 workspace_device: record.body.workspace_device,
766:                 workspace_inode: record.body.workspace_inode,
767:                 files: vec![JournalMutationFile {
768:                     path: "Cargo.toml".to_owned(),
769:                     temp_path: record.body.temp_path,
770:                     source_node: record.body.source_node,
771:                     staged_node: record.body.staged_node,
772:                 }],
773:                 validation: record.body.validation,
774:                 sequence: record.body.sequence,
775:                 phase,
776:                 before: record.body.before,
777:                 after: record.body.after,
778:                 legacy_v1: true,
779:                 format: JournalFormat::V1,
780:             }
781:         }
782:         _ => return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
783:     };
784:     let id = MutationId::new(body.id.clone()).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
785:     IdempotencyKey::new(body.key.clone()).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
786:     body.digest
787:         .parse::<SourceFingerprint>()
788:         .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
789:     if operation_kind(&body.operation).is_err()
790:         || body.files.len() > 128
791:         || (body.legacy_v1
792:             && (body.operation != "manifest_patch"
793:                 || body.files.len() != 1
794:                 || body.files[0].path != "Cargo.toml"))
795:         || body.files.iter().enumerate().any(|(index, file)| {
796:             file.temp_path.contains('/')
797:                 || file.path.is_empty()
798:                 || file.temp_path
799:                     != if body.legacy_v1 {
800:                         legacy_temp_name(&id)
801:                     } else {
802:                         temp_name(&id, index)
803:                     }
804:         })
805:     {
806:         return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
807:     }
808:     Ok(body)
809: }
810: 
811: fn decode(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<JournalBody, MutationError> {
812:     let body = decode_envelope(bytes)?;
813:     let before = body.before.decode()?;
814:     let after = body.after.decode()?;
815:     let candidate = MutationCandidate {
816:         kind: operation_kind(&body.operation)?,
817:         before,
818:         after,
819:         validation: body.validation.clone(),
820:     };
821:     if mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
822:         != body
823:             .digest
824:             .parse::<SourceFingerprint>()
825:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
826:     {
827:         return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
828:     }
829:     let expected = candidate_files(&candidate).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
830:     if expected.len() != body.files.len()
831:         || expected
832:             .iter()
833:             .zip(&body.files)
834:             .any(|(expected, actual)| expected.path != actual.path)
835:         || body
836:             .files
837:             .windows(2)
838:             .any(|pair| pair[0].path >= pair[1].path || pair[0].temp_path == pair[1].temp_path)
839:     {
840:         return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
841:     }
842:     Ok(body)
843: }
844: 
845: fn journal_name(id: &MutationId) -> String {
846:     format!("journal-{}.json", id.as_str())
847: }
848: 
849: fn legacy_temp_name(id: &MutationId) -> String {
850:     format!(".rust-mcp-mut-{}.swap", &id.as_str()[4..])
851: }
852: 
853: fn temp_name(id: &MutationId, index: usize) -> String {
854:     format!(".rust-mcp-mut-{}-{index:03}.swap", &id.as_str()[4..])
855: }
856: 
857: fn operation_kind(operation: &str) -> Result<MutationKind, MutationError> {
858:     match operation {
859:         "manifest_patch" => Ok(MutationKind::ManifestPatch),
860:         "format_apply" => Ok(MutationKind::FormatApply),
861:         "fix_apply" => Ok(MutationKind::FixApply),
862:         "dependency_add" => Ok(MutationKind::DependencyAdd),
863:         "dependency_remove" => Ok(MutationKind::DependencyRemove),
864:         _ => Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
865:     }
866: }
867: 
868: fn operation_name(kind: MutationKind) -> &'static str {
869:     match kind {
870:         MutationKind::ManifestPatch => "manifest_patch",
871:         MutationKind::FormatApply => "format_apply",
872:         MutationKind::FixApply => "fix_apply",
873:         MutationKind::DependencyAdd => "dependency_add",
874:         MutationKind::DependencyRemove => "dependency_remove",
875:     }
876: }
877: 
878: fn source_file<'a>(bundle: &'a SourceBundle, path: &str) -> Option<&'a SourceFile> {
879:     bundle.files().iter().find(|file| file.path() == path)
880: }
881: 
882: #[derive(Clone, Copy)]
883: struct CandidateFile<'a> {
884:     path: &'a str,
885:     before: &'a [u8],
886:     after: &'a [u8],
887: }
888: 
889: fn candidate_files(candidate: &MutationCandidate) -> Result<Vec<CandidateFile<'_>>, MutationError> {
890:     if candidate.before.directories() != candidate.after.directories()
891:         || candidate.before.files().len() != candidate.after.files().len()
892:     {
893:         return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
894:     }
895:     let mut changed = Vec::new();
896:     for (before, after) in candidate.before.files().iter().zip(candidate.after.files()) {
897:         if before.path() != after.path() {
898:             return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
899:         }
900:         if before.bytes() != after.bytes() {
901:             changed.push(CandidateFile {
902:                 path: before.path(),
903:                 before: before.bytes(),
904:                 after: after.bytes(),
905:             });
906:         }
907:     }
908:     match candidate.kind {
909:         MutationKind::ManifestPatch => {
910:             let before =
911:                 source_file(&candidate.before, "Cargo.toml").ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
912:             let after =
913:                 source_file(&candidate.after, "Cargo.toml").ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
914:             if changed
915:                 .iter()
916:                 .any(|file| !matches!(file.path, "Cargo.toml" | "Cargo.lock"))
917:             {
918:                 return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
919:             }
920:             if after.bytes().len() > MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES {
921:                 return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
922:             }
923:             validate_manifest_patch(before.bytes(), after.bytes())?;
924:             if changed.is_empty() {
925:                 // Preserve the M2-01 receipt contract for a semantic no-op.
926:                 Ok(vec![CandidateFile {
927:                     path: "Cargo.toml",
928:                     before: before.bytes(),
929:                     after: after.bytes(),
930:                 }])
931:             } else {
932:                 Ok(changed)
933:             }
934:         }
935:         MutationKind::DependencyAdd | MutationKind::DependencyRemove => {
936:             let manifests = changed
937:                 .iter()
938:                 .filter(|file| file.path == "Cargo.toml" || file.path.ends_with("/Cargo.toml"))
939:                 .copied()
940:                 .collect::<Vec<_>>();
941:             if manifests.len() > 1
942:                 || changed.iter().any(|file| {
943:                     file.path != "Cargo.lock"
944:                         && file.path != "Cargo.toml"
945:                         && !file.path.ends_with("/Cargo.toml")
946:                 })
947:             {
948:                 return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
949:             }
950:             if let Some(manifest) = manifests.first() {
951:                 if manifest.after.len() > MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES {
952:                     return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
953:                 }
954:                 validate_dependency_delta(
955:                     manifest.before,
956:                     manifest.after,
957:                     if candidate.kind == MutationKind::DependencyAdd {
958:                         DependencyDelta::Add
959:                     } else {
960:                         DependencyDelta::Remove
961:                     },
962:                 )?;
963:             }
964:             Ok(changed)
965:         }
966:         MutationKind::FormatApply | MutationKind::FixApply => {
967:             if changed.len() > 128 || changed.iter().any(|file| !file.path.ends_with(".rs")) {
968:                 return Err(if changed.len() > 128 {
969:                     MutationError::LimitExceeded
970:                 } else {
971:                     MutationError::Invalid
972:                 });
973:             }
974:             Ok(changed)
975:         }
976:     }
977: }
978: 
979: fn validate_candidate(request: &MutationCommit) -> Result<Vec<CandidateFile<'_>>, MutationError> {
980:     if mutation_digest(&request.candidate)? != request.digest {
981:         return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
982:     }
983:     candidate_files(&request.candidate)
984: }
985: 
986: fn mixed_bundle(
987:     before: &SourceBundle,
988:     after: &SourceBundle,
989:     files: &[JournalMutationFile],
990:     published_prefix: usize,
991: ) -> Result<SourceBundle, MutationError> {
992:     let published: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = files
993:         .iter()
994:         .take(published_prefix)
995:         .map(|file| file.path.as_str())
996:         .collect();
997:     let output = before
998:         .files()
999:         .iter()
1000:         .map(|file| {
1001:             let bytes = if published.contains(file.path()) {
1002:                 source_file(after, file.path())
1003:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
1004:                     .bytes()
1005:             } else {
1006:                 file.bytes()
1007:             };
1008:             SourceFile::new(file.path().to_owned(), bytes.to_vec())
1009:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1010:         })
1011:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
1012:     SourceBundle::with_directories(output, before.directories().to_vec())
1013:         .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1014: }
1015: 
1016: fn file_fingerprint(bytes: &[u8]) -> Result<SourceFingerprint, MutationError> {
1017:     sha256(bytes).parse().map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)
1018: }
1019: 
1020: fn mutation_state(phase: JournalPhase) -> MutationState {
1021:     match phase {
1022:         JournalPhase::Committed => MutationState::Committed,
1023:         JournalPhase::NoChange => MutationState::NoChange,
1024:         JournalPhase::Aborted => MutationState::Aborted,
1025:         JournalPhase::Published
1026:         | JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup
1027:         | JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired
1028:         | JournalPhase::Prepared
1029:         | JournalPhase::Scratch
1030:         | JournalPhase::Staged
1031:         | JournalPhase::Applying => MutationState::RecoveryRequired,
1032:     }
1033: }
1034: 
1035: fn make_receipt(body: &JournalBody) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1036:     let state = mutation_state(body.phase);
1037:     let before = body.before.decode()?;
1038:     let after = body.after.decode()?;
1039:     let files = body
1040:         .files
1041:         .iter()
1042:         .map(|file| {
1043:             let before_file =
1044:                 source_file(&before, &file.path).ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1045:             let after_file =
1046:                 source_file(&after, &file.path).ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1047:             let (effect_after, effect_after_bytes) = match state {
1048:                 MutationState::Committed => (
1049:                     Some(file_fingerprint(after_file.bytes())?),
1050:                     Some(after_file.bytes().len() as u64),
1051:                 ),
1052:                 MutationState::NoChange | MutationState::Aborted => (
1053:                     Some(file_fingerprint(before_file.bytes())?),
1054:                     Some(before_file.bytes().len() as u64),
1055:                 ),
1056:                 MutationState::RecoveryRequired => (None, None),
1057:             };
1058:             Ok(MutationFileReceipt {
1059:                 path: file.path.clone(),
1060:                 before: file_fingerprint(before_file.bytes())?,
1061:                 after: file_fingerprint(after_file.bytes())?,
1062:                 before_bytes: before_file.bytes().len() as u64,
1063:                 after_bytes: after_file.bytes().len() as u64,
1064:                 effect_after,
1065:                 effect_after_bytes,
1066:             })
1067:         })
1068:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, MutationError>>()?;
1069:     Ok(MutationReceipt {
1070:         id: MutationId::new(body.id.clone()).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
1071:         digest: body
1072:             .digest
1073:             .parse()
1074:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
1075:         state,
1076:         validation: body.validation.clone(),
1077:         files,
1078:     })
1079: }
1080: 
1081: struct Continue;
1082: impl OperationControl for Continue {
1083:     fn check(&self) -> Result<(), ProjectError> {
1084:         Ok(())
1085:     }
1086: }
1087: 
1088: #[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
1089: enum CommitCheckpoint {
1090:     Prepared,
1091:     FileCloned(usize),
1092:     CloneCreated,
1093:     Scratch,
1094:     WrittenBeforeSync,
1095:     BeforeStagedPersist,
1096:     Staged,
1097:     Applying,
1098:     FileSwapped(usize),
1099:     FileCleaned(usize),
1100:     Swapped,
1101:     Verified,
1102:     Published,
1103:     Cleaned,
1104:     Committed,
1105: }
1106: 
1107: fn workspace_lock_name(node: Node) -> String {
1108:     let mut hash = Sha256::new();
1109:     hash.update(node.device.to_le_bytes());
1110:     hash.update(node.inode.to_le_bytes());
1111:     let shard = hash.finalize()[0] % WORKSPACE_LOCK_SHARDS;
1112:     format!("workspace-lock-{shard:02}.lock")
1113: }
1114: 
1115: fn is_workspace_lock_name(name: &str) -> bool {
1116:     name.strip_prefix("workspace-lock-")
1117:         .and_then(|name| name.strip_suffix(".lock"))
1118:         .filter(|shard| shard.len() == 2 && shard.bytes().all(|byte| byte.is_ascii_digit()))
1119:         .and_then(|shard| shard.parse::<u8>().ok())
1120:         .is_some_and(|shard| shard < WORKSPACE_LOCK_SHARDS)
1121: }
1122: 
1123: pub struct NativeMutationStore {
1124:     state: StateRoot,
1125:     projects: SecureProjects,
1126:     kind: MutationKind,
1127: }
1128: 
1129: struct StoreIndex {
1130:     journal_count: usize,
1131:     bytes: u64,
1132:     bodies: Vec<(JournalBody, bool)>,
1133:     summaries: Vec<MutationRecordSummary>,
1134: }
1135: 
1136: fn ensure_new_record_quota(
1137:     index: &StoreIndex,
1138:     transient_reservation: u64,
1139:     retained_future_record: u64,
1140: ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1141:     let retained_ceiling = MAX_STORE_BYTES
1142:         .checked_sub(RECOVERY_HEADROOM_BYTES)
1143:         .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1144:     if index.journal_count >= MAX_JOURNALS
1145:         || index
1146:             .bytes
1147:             .checked_add(transient_reservation)
1148:             .is_none_or(|total| total > MAX_STORE_BYTES)
1149:         || index
1150:             .bytes
1151:             .checked_add(retained_future_record)
1152:             .is_none_or(|total| total > retained_ceiling)
1153:     {
1154:         return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
1155:     }
1156:     Ok(())
1157: }
1158: 
1159: impl NativeMutationStore {
1160:     pub fn open(state_path: &Path, write_roots: &[PathBuf]) -> Result<Self, MutationError> {
1161:         Self::open_for_kind(state_path, write_roots, MutationKind::ManifestPatch)
1162:     }
1163: 
1164:     pub fn open_for_kind(
1165:         state_path: &Path,
1166:         write_roots: &[PathBuf],
1167:         kind: MutationKind,
1168:     ) -> Result<Self, MutationError> {
1169:         let checked_state = checked_path(state_path).map_err(project_error)?;
1170:         for root in write_roots {
1171:             let root = checked_path(root).map_err(project_error)?;
1172:             if checked_state.starts_with(&root) || root.starts_with(&checked_state) {
1173:                 return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1174:             }
1175:         }
1176:         let projects = SecureProjects::new(write_roots).map_err(project_error)?;
1177:         let state = StateRoot::open(&checked_state)?;
1178:         state.open_lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1179:         state.durable()?;
1180:         Ok(Self {
1181:             state,
1182:             projects,
1183:             kind,
1184:         })
1185:     }
1186: 
1187:     /// Checks the live host grant and the lease's retained physical identity.
1188:     pub fn authorize(&self, lease: &ProjectLease) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1189:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)
1190:     }
1191: 
1192:     /// Lists bounded durable records for the local operator without project I/O.
1193:     pub fn list_records(&self) -> Result<Vec<MutationRecordSummary>, MutationError> {
1194:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1195:         Ok(self.scan_store()?.summaries)
1196:     }
1197: 
1198:     /// Removes one explicitly identified terminal replay record.
1199:     pub fn prune_record(
1200:         &self,
1201:         id: &MutationId,
1202:         digest: &SourceFingerprint,
1203:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1204:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1205:         let name = journal_name(id);
1206:         let staging = format!(".{name}.staging");
1207:         if self.state.read_optional(&staging)?.is_some() {
1208:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1209:         }
1210:         let raw = self
1211:             .state
1212:             .read_optional(&name)?
1213:             .ok_or(MutationError::NotFound)?;
1214:         let body = decode(&raw)?;
1215:         if body.id != id.as_str() {
1216:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1217:         }
1218:         if body.digest != digest.as_str() {
1219:             return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
1220:         }
1221:         if !matches!(
1222:             body.phase,
1223:             JournalPhase::Committed | JournalPhase::NoChange | JournalPhase::Aborted
1224:         ) {
1225:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1226:         }
1227:         unlinkat(&self.state.directory, &name, AtFlags::empty()).map_err(|error| {
1228:             if error == Errno::NOENT {
1229:                 MutationError::NotFound
1230:             } else {
1231:                 mutation_io(error)
1232:             }
1233:         })?;
1234:         self.state.durable()
1235:     }
1236: 
1237:     pub fn commit(
1238:         &self,
1239:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1240:         request: &MutationCommit,
1241:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
1242:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1243:         self.commit_checked(lease, request, control, |_| Ok(()))
1244:     }
1245: 
1246:     /// Replays only an existing journal binding; it never creates a journal.
1247:     pub fn replay(
1248:         &self,
1249:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1250:         id: &MutationId,
1251:         digest: &SourceFingerprint,
1252:         key: &IdempotencyKey,
1253:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
1254:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1255:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1256:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1257:         self.authorize_with(lease, control)?;
1258:         let workspace_lock = workspace_lock_name(lease.node);
1259:         let _workspace = self.state.lock(&workspace_lock)?;
1260:         let observed = match self.load_readonly(lease, id)? {
1261:             Some(body) => body,
1262:             None => {
1263:                 if self.orphan_temp_exists(lease, id)? {
1264:                     return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1265:                 }
1266:                 return Err(MutationError::NotFound);
1267:             }
1268:         };
1269:         self.replay_loaded_locked(lease, id, digest, key, control, observed)
1270:     }
1271: 
1272:     fn commit_checked(
1273:         &self,
1274:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1275:         request: &MutationCommit,
1276:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
1277:         mut checkpoint: impl FnMut(CommitCheckpoint) -> Result<(), MutationError>,
1278:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1279:         if request.candidate.kind != self.kind {
1280:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1281:         }
1282:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1283:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1284:         self.authorize_with(lease, control)?;
1285:         let workspace_lock = workspace_lock_name(lease.node);
1286:         let _workspace = self.state.lock(&workspace_lock)?;
1287:         let planned = validate_candidate(request)?;
1288:         let changed = planned.iter().any(|file| file.before != file.after);
1289: 
1290:         if let Some(observed) = self.load_readonly(lease, &request.id)? {
1291:             return self.replay_loaded_locked(
1292:                 lease,
1293:                 &request.id,
1294:                 &request.digest,
1295:                 &request.key,
1296:                 control,
1297:                 observed,
1298:             );
1299:         }
1300:         let index = self.scan_store()?;
1301:         self.reject_new_commit(&index, lease, request)?;
1302: 
1303:         let before_live = self
1304:             .projects
1305:             .source(lease, control)
1306:             .map_err(project_error)?;
1307:         if before_live != request.candidate.before {
1308:             return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
1309:         }
1310:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1311:         let mut files = Vec::with_capacity(planned.len());
1312:         for (index, file) in planned.iter().enumerate() {
1313:             let (source_bytes, source_node) =
1314:                 self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, file.path)?;
1315:             if source_bytes != file.before {
1316:                 return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
1317:             }
1318:             files.push(JournalMutationFile {
1319:                 path: file.path.to_owned(),
1320:                 temp_path: temp_name(&request.id, index),
1321:                 source_node: source_node.into(),
1322:                 staged_node: None,
1323:             });
1324:         }
1325: 
1326:         let mut body = JournalBody {
1327:             id: request.id.as_str().to_owned(),
1328:             digest: request.digest.as_str().to_owned(),
1329:             key: request.key.as_str().to_owned(),
1330:             operation: operation_name(self.kind).to_owned(),
1331:             workspace_path: lease
1332:                 .path
1333:                 .to_str()
1334:                 .ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?
1335:                 .to_owned(),
1336:             workspace_device: lease.node.device,
1337:             workspace_inode: lease.node.inode,
1338:             files,
1339:             validation: request.candidate.validation.clone(),
1340:             sequence: 0,
1341:             phase: JournalPhase::Prepared,
1342:             before: JournalBundle::from(&request.candidate.before),
1343:             after: JournalBundle::from(&request.candidate.after),
1344:             legacy_v1: false,
1345:             format: JournalFormat::V2,
1346:         };
1347:         let name = journal_name(&request.id);
1348:         let worst_case_bytes = worst_case_record_len(&body)?;
1349:         // Reserve two maximum-size copies because phase persistence briefly
1350:         // holds the durable final record and its complete staging successor.
1351:         let reservation = (worst_case_bytes as u64)
1352:             .checked_mul(2)
1353:             .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1354:         ensure_new_record_quota(&index, reservation, worst_case_bytes as u64)?;
1355:         let encoded = encode(&body)?;
1356:         self.state
1357:             .write_new(&name, &encoded, JournalPhase::Prepared)?;
1358:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Prepared) {
1359:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1360:         }
1361: 
1362:         if !changed {
1363:             body.phase = JournalPhase::NoChange;
1364:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
1365:             return make_receipt(&body);
1366:         }
1367: 
1368:         for (index, plan) in planned.iter().enumerate() {
1369:             let staged_node = match self.clone_temp_before(
1370:                 lease,
1371:                 &body.files[index].path,
1372:                 &body.files[index].temp_path,
1373:                 plan.before,
1374:                 body.files[index].source_node,
1375:             ) {
1376:                 Ok(node) => node,
1377:                 Err(error) => return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error)),
1378:             };
1379:             body.files[index].staged_node = Some(staged_node);
1380:             if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::FileCloned(index)) {
1381:                 return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1382:             }
1383:         }
1384:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::CloneCreated) {
1385:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1386:         }
1387:         body.phase = JournalPhase::Scratch;
1388:         if let Err(error) = self.persist(lease, &mut body) {
1389:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1390:         }
1391:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Scratch) {
1392:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1393:         }
1394:         for (index, plan) in planned.iter().enumerate() {
1395:             let staged_node = body.files[index]
1396:                 .staged_node
1397:                 .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1398:             if let Err(error) = self.rewrite_temp_after(
1399:                 lease,
1400:                 &body.files[index].path,
1401:                 &body.files[index].temp_path,
1402:                 (plan.before, plan.after),
1403:                 body.files[index].source_node,
1404:                 staged_node,
1405:                 || checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::WrittenBeforeSync),
1406:             ) {
1407:                 return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1408:             }
1409:         }
1410:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::BeforeStagedPersist) {
1411:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1412:         }
1413:         body.phase = JournalPhase::Staged;
1414:         if let Err(error) = self.persist(lease, &mut body) {
1415:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1416:         }
1417:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Staged) {
1418:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1419:         }
1420: 
1421:         let exclusions = match self.temp_exclusions(lease, &body, false) {
1422:             Ok(exclusions) => exclusions,
1423:             Err(error) => return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error)),
1424:         };
1425:         let live = match self
1426:             .projects
1427:             .source_excluding(lease, control, &exclusions)
1428:             .map_err(project_error)
1429:         {
1430:             Ok(live) => live,
1431:             Err(error) => return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error)),
1432:         };
1433:         if live != request.candidate.before {
1434:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, MutationError::Conflict));
1435:         }
1436:         if let Err(error) = control.check().map_err(project_error) {
1437:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1438:         }
1439:         body.phase = JournalPhase::Applying;
1440:         if let Err(error) = self.persist(lease, &mut body) {
1441:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1442:         }
1443:         if let Err(error) = checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Applying) {
1444:             return Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error));
1445:         }
1446:         let mut published_files = 0usize;
1447:         let post_effect = (|| {
1448:             for (index, plan) in planned.iter().enumerate() {
1449:                 let file = &body.files[index];
1450:                 let staged_node = file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1451:                 self.swap(lease, &file.path, &file.temp_path)?;
1452:                 published_files += 1;
1453:                 checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::FileSwapped(index))?;
1454:                 self.verify_swap(
1455:                     lease,
1456:                     &file.path,
1457:                     &file.temp_path,
1458:                     plan.before,
1459:                     plan.after,
1460:                     file.source_node,
1461:                     staged_node,
1462:                 )?;
1463:             }
1464:             checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Swapped)?;
1465:             checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Verified)?;
1466:             let exclusions = self.temp_exclusions(lease, &body, true)?;
1467:             let live = self
1468:                 .projects
1469:                 .source_excluding(lease, &Continue, &exclusions)
1470:                 .map_err(project_error)?;
1471:             if live != request.candidate.after {
1472:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1473:             }
1474:             body.phase = JournalPhase::Published;
1475:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
1476:             checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Published)?;
1477:             for (index, plan) in planned.iter().enumerate() {
1478:                 let file = &body.files[index];
1479:                 self.cleanup_temp(
1480:                     lease,
1481:                     &file.path,
1482:                     &file.temp_path,
1483:                     plan.before,
1484:                     plan.after,
1485:                     file.source_node,
1486:                     file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
1487:                 )?;
1488:                 checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::FileCleaned(index))?;
1489:             }
1490:             checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Cleaned)?;
1491:             let mut committed = body.clone();
1492:             committed.phase = JournalPhase::Committed;
1493:             self.persist(lease, &mut committed)?;
1494:             body = committed;
1495:             checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::Committed)?;
1496:             make_receipt(&body)
1497:         })();
1498:         match post_effect {
1499:             Ok(value) => Ok(value),
1500:             Err(error) if published_files == 0 => Err(self.abort_before_effect(lease, body, error)),
1501:             Err(_) => Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
1502:         }
1503:     }
1504: 
1505:     fn replay_loaded_locked(
1506:         &self,
1507:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1508:         id: &MutationId,
1509:         digest: &SourceFingerprint,
1510:         key: &IdempotencyKey,
1511:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
1512:         mut observed: JournalBody,
1513:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1514:         if observed.id != id.as_str() {
1515:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1516:         }
1517:         if observed.digest != digest.as_str() || observed.key != key.as_str() {
1518:             return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
1519:         }
1520:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1521:         if matches!(
1522:             observed.phase,
1523:             JournalPhase::Committed | JournalPhase::NoChange | JournalPhase::Aborted
1524:         ) && self.all_temps_absent(lease, &observed)?
1525:         {
1526:             if observed.format == JournalFormat::V1 {
1527:                 self.persist(lease, &mut observed)?;
1528:             }
1529:             return make_receipt(&observed);
1530:         }
1531:         let body = self
1532:             .load_repair_for(lease, id, Some(&observed))?
1533:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1534:         self.recover_locked(lease, body)
1535:     }
1536: 
1537:     pub fn receipt(
1538:         &self,
1539:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1540:         id: &MutationId,
1541:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1542:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1543:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)?;
1544:         let body = match self.load_readonly(lease, id)? {
1545:             Some(body) => body,
1546:             None => {
1547:                 if self.orphan_temp_exists(lease, id)? {
1548:                     return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1549:                 }
1550:                 return Err(MutationError::NotFound);
1551:             }
1552:         };
1553:         self.authorize_body(lease, &body)?;
1554:         if !self.all_temps_absent(lease, &body)? {
1555:             let mut pending = body;
1556:             pending.phase = JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired;
1557:             return make_receipt(&pending);
1558:         }
1559:         make_receipt(&body)
1560:     }
1561: 
1562:     pub fn recover(
1563:         &self,
1564:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1565:         id: &MutationId,
1566:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
1567:         let _global = self.state.lock(GLOBAL_LOCK)?;
1568:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)?;
1569:         let workspace_lock = workspace_lock_name(lease.node);
1570:         let _workspace = self.state.lock(&workspace_lock)?;
1571:         let body = match self.load_repair(lease, id)? {
1572:             Some(body) => body,
1573:             None => {
1574:                 if self.orphan_temp_exists(lease, id)? {
1575:                     return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1576:                 }
1577:                 return Err(MutationError::NotFound);
1578:             }
1579:         };
1580:         self.authorize_body(lease, &body)?;
1581:         self.recover_locked(lease, body)
1582:     }
1583: 
1584:     fn authorize_with(
1585:         &self,
1586:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1587:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
1588:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1589:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1590:         self.state.check()?;
1591:         if Node::of(&lease.directory).map_err(project_error)? != lease.node
1592:             || Node::of(
1593:                 &self
1594:                     .projects
1595:                     .open_path(&lease.path, true)
1596:                     .map_err(project_error)?,
1597:             )
1598:             .map_err(project_error)?
1599:                 != lease.node
1600:         {
1601:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1602:         }
1603:         control.check().map_err(project_error)?;
1604:         let granted_root = self
1605:             .projects
1606:             .roots
1607:             .iter()
1608:             .find(|root| lease.path == root.path && root.node == lease.node);
1609:         if granted_root.is_none() {
1610:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1611:         }
1612:         Ok(())
1613:     }
1614: 
1615:     fn authorize_body(
1616:         &self,
1617:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1618:         body: &JournalBody,
1619:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1620:         if body.workspace_path != lease.path.to_str().ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?
1621:             || body.workspace_device != lease.node.device
1622:             || body.workspace_inode != lease.node.inode
1623:             || operation_kind(&body.operation)? != self.kind
1624:         {
1625:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1626:         }
1627:         Ok(())
1628:     }
1629: 
1630:     fn scan_store(&self) -> Result<StoreIndex, MutationError> {
1631:         self.state.check()?;
1632:         let mut dir = Dir::read_from(&self.state.directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
1633:         let mut entry_count = 0usize;
1634:         let mut bytes = 0u64;
1635:         let mut records: BTreeMap<String, (Option<JournalBody>, Option<JournalBody>, u64)> =
1636:             BTreeMap::new();
1637:         for entry in &mut dir {
1638:             let entry = entry.map_err(mutation_io)?;
1639:             let name = entry
1640:                 .file_name()
1641:                 .to_str()
1642:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1643:             if matches!(name, "." | "..") {
1644:                 continue;
1645:             }
1646:             entry_count += 1;
1647:             if entry_count > MAX_STORE_ENTRIES {
1648:                 return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
1649:             }
1650:             if name == GLOBAL_LOCK || is_workspace_lock_name(name) {
1651:                 let fd = openat(&self.state.directory, name, flags(false), Mode::empty())
1652:                     .map_err(mutation_io)?;
1653:                 require_private_file(&fd, 0)?;
1654:                 continue;
1655:             }
1656:             let is_final = name.starts_with("journal-") && name.ends_with(".json");
1657:             let is_staging = name.starts_with(".journal-") && name.ends_with(".json.staging");
1658:             if !is_final && !is_staging {
1659:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1660:             }
1661:             let raw = self
1662:                 .state
1663:                 .read_optional(name)?
1664:                 .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1665:             bytes = bytes
1666:                 .checked_add(raw.len() as u64)
1667:                 .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1668:             if bytes > MAX_STORE_BYTES {
1669:                 return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
1670:             }
1671:             let body = decode_envelope(&raw)?;
1672:             let expected = if is_final {
1673:                 format!("journal-{}.json", body.id)
1674:             } else {
1675:                 format!(".journal-{}.json.staging", body.id)
1676:             };
1677:             if name != expected {
1678:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1679:             }
1680:             let slot = records.entry(body.id.clone()).or_default();
1681:             let target = if is_final { &mut slot.0 } else { &mut slot.1 };
1682:             if target.replace(body).is_some() {
1683:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1684:             }
1685:             slot.2 = slot
1686:                 .2
1687:                 .checked_add(raw.len() as u64)
1688:                 .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1689:             if records.len() > MAX_JOURNALS {
1690:                 return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
1691:             }
1692:         }
1693:         let journal_count = records.len();
1694:         let mut bodies = Vec::with_capacity(journal_count * 2);
1695:         let mut summaries = Vec::with_capacity(journal_count);
1696:         for (_, (final_body, staging_body, stored_bytes)) in records {
1697:             if let (Some(final_body), Some(staging_body)) = (&final_body, &staging_body) {
1698:                 require_newer_staging(final_body, staging_body)?;
1699:             }
1700:             let summary_body = staging_body
1701:                 .as_ref()
1702:                 .or(final_body.as_ref())
1703:                 .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1704:             summaries.push(MutationRecordSummary {
1705:                 id: MutationId::new(summary_body.id.clone())
1706:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
1707:                 digest: summary_body
1708:                     .digest
1709:                     .parse()
1710:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
1711:                 state: if staging_body.is_some() {
1712:                     MutationState::RecoveryRequired
1713:                 } else {
1714:                     mutation_state(summary_body.phase)
1715:                 },
1716:                 stored_bytes,
1717:             });
1718:             bodies.extend(final_body.map(|body| (body, false)));
1719:             bodies.extend(staging_body.map(|body| (body, true)));
1720:         }
1721:         self.state.check()?;
1722:         Ok(StoreIndex {
1723:             journal_count,
1724:             bytes,
1725:             bodies,
1726:             summaries,
1727:         })
1728:     }
1729: 
1730:     fn read_pair(
1731:         &self,
1732:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1733:         id: &MutationId,
1734:     ) -> Result<(Option<JournalBody>, Option<JournalBody>), MutationError> {
1735:         let name = journal_name(id);
1736:         let staging_name = format!(".{name}.staging");
1737:         let final_bytes = self.state.read_optional(&name)?;
1738:         let staging_bytes = self.state.read_optional(&staging_name)?;
1739:         let final_body = final_bytes.as_deref().map(decode).transpose()?;
1740:         let staging_body = staging_bytes.as_deref().map(decode).transpose()?;
1741:         if let Some(body) = final_body.as_ref() {
1742:             self.authorize_body(lease, body)?;
1743:         }
1744:         if let Some(body) = staging_body.as_ref() {
1745:             self.authorize_body(lease, body)?;
1746:         }
1747:         if final_body
1748:             .as_ref()
1749:             .is_some_and(|body| body.id != id.as_str())
1750:             || staging_body
1751:                 .as_ref()
1752:                 .is_some_and(|body| body.id != id.as_str())
1753:         {
1754:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1755:         }
1756:         if let (Some(final_body), Some(staging_body)) = (&final_body, &staging_body) {
1757:             require_newer_staging(final_body, staging_body)?;
1758:         }
1759:         Ok((final_body, staging_body))
1760:     }
1761: 
1762:     fn load_readonly(
1763:         &self,
1764:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1765:         id: &MutationId,
1766:     ) -> Result<Option<JournalBody>, MutationError> {
1767:         match self.read_pair(lease, id)? {
1768:             (None, None) => Ok(None),
1769:             (Some(body), None) => Ok(Some(body)),
1770:             (None, Some(mut body)) | (Some(_), Some(mut body)) => {
1771:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired;
1772:                 Ok(Some(body))
1773:             }
1774:         }
1775:     }
1776: 
1777:     fn load_repair(
1778:         &self,
1779:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1780:         id: &MutationId,
1781:     ) -> Result<Option<JournalBody>, MutationError> {
1782:         self.load_repair_for(lease, id, None)
1783:     }
1784: 
1785:     fn load_repair_for(
1786:         &self,
1787:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1788:         id: &MutationId,
1789:         expected: Option<&JournalBody>,
1790:     ) -> Result<Option<JournalBody>, MutationError> {
1791:         let name = journal_name(id);
1792:         let staging_name = format!(".{name}.staging");
1793:         match self.read_pair(lease, id)? {
1794:             (None, None) => Ok(None),
1795:             (Some(body), None) => {
1796:                 if expected.is_some_and(|expected| !body.same_binding(expected)) {
1797:                     return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1798:                 }
1799:                 Ok(Some(body))
1800:             }
1801:             (None, Some(body)) | (Some(_), Some(body)) => {
1802:                 if expected.is_some_and(|expected| !body.same_binding(expected)) {
1803:                     return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1804:                 }
1805:                 renameat(
1806:                     &self.state.directory,
1807:                     &staging_name,
1808:                     &self.state.directory,
1809:                     &name,
1810:                 )
1811:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1812:                 self.state.durable()?;
1813:                 Ok(Some(body))
1814:             }
1815:         }
1816:     }
1817: 
1818:     fn reject_new_commit(
1819:         &self,
1820:         index: &StoreIndex,
1821:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1822:         request: &MutationCommit,
1823:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1824:         for (body, is_staging) in &index.bodies {
1825:             if body.key == request.key.as_str() && body.id != request.id.as_str() {
1826:                 return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
1827:             }
1828:             if body.id != request.id.as_str()
1829:                 && body.workspace_device == lease.node.device
1830:                 && body.workspace_inode == lease.node.inode
1831:                 && (*is_staging
1832:                     || matches!(
1833:                         body.phase,
1834:                         JournalPhase::Prepared
1835:                             | JournalPhase::Scratch
1836:                             | JournalPhase::Staged
1837:                             | JournalPhase::Applying
1838:                             | JournalPhase::Published
1839:                             | JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup
1840:                             | JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired
1841:                     ))
1842:             {
1843:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1844:             }
1845:         }
1846:         Ok(())
1847:     }
1848: 
1849:     fn persist(&self, lease: &ProjectLease, body: &mut JournalBody) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
1850:         let id = MutationId::new(body.id.clone()).map_err(|_| MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1851:         let current = self
1852:             .load_repair_for(lease, &id, Some(body))?
1853:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
1854:         if current.format == JournalFormat::V2
1855:             && current.phase == body.phase
1856:             && current
1857:                 .files
1858:                 .iter()
1859:                 .map(|file| file.staged_node)
1860:                 .eq(body.files.iter().map(|file| file.staged_node))
1861:         {
1862:             *body = current;
1863:             return Ok(());
1864:         }
1865:         let mut next = body.clone();
1866:         next.format = JournalFormat::V2;
1867:         next.sequence = current
1868:             .sequence
1869:             .checked_add(1)
1870:             .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1871:         let encoded = encode(&next)?;
1872:         let name = journal_name(&id);
1873:         let staging = format!(".{name}.staging");
1874:         self.state.write_new(&staging, &encoded, next.phase)?;
1875:         renameat(
1876:             &self.state.directory,
1877:             &staging,
1878:             &self.state.directory,
1879:             &name,
1880:         )
1881:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
1882:         self.state.durable()?;
1883:         *body = next;
1884:         Ok(())
1885:     }
1886: 
1887:     fn authority<'a>(
1888:         &'a self,
1889:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1890:         path: &Path,
1891:     ) -> Result<(&'a Root, PathBuf), MutationError> {
1892:         let root = self
1893:             .projects
1894:             .roots
1895:             .iter()
1896:             .find(|root| root.path == lease.path && root.node == lease.node)
1897:             .ok_or(MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
1898:         self.projects.check_root(root).map_err(project_error)?;
1899:         let relative = path
1900:             .strip_prefix(&root.path)
1901:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
1902:         Ok((root, relative.to_path_buf()))
1903:     }
1904: 
1905:     fn workspace_entry(
1906:         &self,
1907:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1908:         name: &str,
1909:     ) -> Result<Option<OwnedFd>, MutationError> {
1910:         let path = lease.path.join(name);
1911:         match self.projects.open_path(&path, false) {
1912:             Ok(fd) => Ok(Some(fd)),
1913:             Err(ProjectError::Rejected(OperationalErrorCode::ProjectNotFound)) => Ok(None),
1914:             Err(error) => Err(project_error(error)),
1915:         }
1916:     }
1917: 
1918:     fn orphan_temp_exists(
1919:         &self,
1920:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1921:         id: &MutationId,
1922:     ) -> Result<bool, MutationError> {
1923:         if self
1924:             .workspace_entry(lease, &legacy_temp_name(id))?
1925:             .is_some()
1926:         {
1927:             return Ok(true);
1928:         }
1929:         for index in 0..128 {
1930:             if self
1931:                 .workspace_entry(lease, &temp_name(id, index))?
1932:                 .is_some()
1933:             {
1934:                 return Ok(true);
1935:             }
1936:         }
1937:         Ok(false)
1938:     }
1939: 
1940:     fn all_temps_absent(
1941:         &self,
1942:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1943:         body: &JournalBody,
1944:     ) -> Result<bool, MutationError> {
1945:         for file in &body.files {
1946:             if self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?.is_some() {
1947:                 return Ok(false);
1948:             }
1949:         }
1950:         Ok(true)
1951:     }
1952: 
1953:     fn temp_exclusions(
1954:         &self,
1955:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1956:         body: &JournalBody,
1957:         displaced_sources: bool,
1958:     ) -> Result<BTreeMap<PathBuf, Node>, MutationError> {
1959:         let mut excluded = BTreeMap::new();
1960:         for file in &body.files {
1961:             let Some(fd) = self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)? else {
1962:                 if !displaced_sources && body.operation == "manifest_patch" {
1963:                     continue;
1964:                 }
1965:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1966:             };
1967:             let node = Node::of(&fd).map_err(project_error)?;
1968:             let expected = if displaced_sources {
1969:                 file.source_node
1970:             } else {
1971:                 file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
1972:             };
1973:             if JournalNode::from(node) != expected {
1974:                 return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
1975:             }
1976:             excluded.insert(lease.path.join(&file.temp_path), node);
1977:         }
1978:         Ok(excluded)
1979:     }
1980: 
1981:     fn clone_temp_before(
1982:         &self,
1983:         lease: &ProjectLease,
1984:         source_path: &str,
1985:         temp: &str,
1986:         before: &[u8],
1987:         expected_source: JournalNode,
1988:     ) -> Result<JournalNode, MutationError> {
1989:         let cargo_path = lease.path.join(source_path);
1990:         let source = self
1991:             .projects
1992:             .open_path(&cargo_path, false)
1993:             .map_err(project_error)?;
1994:         let source_stat = fstat(&source).map_err(mutation_io)?;
1995:         let source_stamp = FileStamp::from_stat(source_stat).map_err(project_error)?;
1996:         if JournalNode::from(source_stamp.node) != expected_source
1997:             || source_stat.st_uid != rustix::process::geteuid().as_raw()
1998:             || source_stat.st_mode & 0o7000 != 0
1999:             || source_stat.st_mode & 0o600 != 0o600
2000:             || source_stat.st_mode & 0o133 != 0
2001:         {
2002:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
2003:         }
2004:         let source_xattrs = xattrs(&source)?;
2005:         if self.read_workspace_file(lease, source_path)? != before {
2006:             return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
2007:         }
2008:         if self.workspace_entry(lease, temp)?.is_some() {
2009:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2010:         }
2011:         let temp_path = lease.path.join(temp);
2012:         let (root, relative) = self.authority(lease, &temp_path)?;
2013:         fclonefileat(
2014:             &source,
2015:             &root.directory,
2016:             &relative,
2017:             CloneFlags::from_bits_retain(CLONE_SAFE_METADATA),
2018:         )
2019:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
2020:         let staged = self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2021:             lease,
2022:             source_path,
2023:             temp,
2024:             before,
2025:             Some(before),
2026:             expected_source,
2027:             None,
2028:         )?;
2029:         let cloned = self
2030:             .workspace_entry(lease, temp)?
2031:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2032:         fsync(&cloned).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2033:         fcntl_fullfsync(&cloned).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2034:         self.durable_workspace(lease)?;
2035:         // Recheck after durability: the node and exact clone must still be the
2036:         // one validated above before its identity can be journaled.
2037:         let durable = self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2038:             lease,
2039:             source_path,
2040:             temp,
2041:             before,
2042:             Some(before),
2043:             expected_source,
2044:             Some(staged),
2045:         )?;
2046:         if durable != staged
2047:             || FileStamp::from_stat(fstat(&source).map_err(mutation_io)?).map_err(project_error)?
2048:                 != source_stamp
2049:             || source_xattrs != xattrs(&source)?
2050:         {
2051:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2052:         }
2053:         Ok(staged)
2054:     }
2055: 
2056:     // These explicit values are the complete before/after inode binding; a
2057:     // parameter bundle would make it easier to accidentally reuse the wrong
2058:     // file's authority while iterating a multi-file plan.
2059:     #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
2060:     fn rewrite_temp_after(
2061:         &self,
2062:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2063:         source_path: &str,
2064:         temp: &str,
2065:         bytes: (&[u8], &[u8]),
2066:         source_node: JournalNode,
2067:         staged_node: JournalNode,
2068:         before_sync: impl FnOnce() -> Result<(), MutationError>,
2069:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2070:         let (before, after) = bytes;
2071:         self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2072:             lease,
2073:             source_path,
2074:             temp,
2075:             before,
2076:             Some(before),
2077:             source_node,
2078:             Some(staged_node),
2079:         )?;
2080:         let temp_path = lease.path.join(temp);
2081:         let (root, relative) = self.authority(lease, &temp_path)?;
2082:         let writable = openat(
2083:             &root.directory,
2084:             &relative,
2085:             flags(false) | OFlags::WRONLY,
2086:             Mode::empty(),
2087:         )
2088:         .map_err(mutation_io)?;
2089:         if JournalNode::from(Node::of(&writable).map_err(project_error)?) != staged_node {
2090:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2091:         }
2092:         let mut file = File::from(writable);
2093:         file.set_len(0).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
2094:         write_all_with_test_fault(&mut file, after, IoFaultPoint::TempContentWrite)?;
2095:         before_sync()?;
2096:         fsync(&file).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2097:         fcntl_fullfsync(&file).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2098:         self.durable_workspace(lease)?;
2099:         self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2100:             lease,
2101:             source_path,
2102:             temp,
2103:             before,
2104:             Some(after),
2105:             source_node,
2106:             Some(staged_node),
2107:         )?;
2108:         Ok(())
2109:     }
2110: 
2111:     /// Validates a prepublication clone. `expected_temp_bytes == None` is only
2112:     /// used after a durable phase has made the node protocol-owned scratch.
2113:     #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
2114:     fn validate_unpublished_temp(
2115:         &self,
2116:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2117:         source_path: &str,
2118:         temp: &str,
2119:         before: &[u8],
2120:         expected_temp_bytes: Option<&[u8]>,
2121:         source_node: JournalNode,
2122:         staged_node: Option<JournalNode>,
2123:     ) -> Result<JournalNode, MutationError> {
2124:         let source = self
2125:             .workspace_entry(lease, source_path)?
2126:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2127:         let staged = self
2128:             .workspace_entry(lease, temp)?
2129:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2130:         let source_stat = fstat(&source).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2131:         let staged_stat = fstat(&staged).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2132:         // FileStamp is the load-bearing regular-file and st_nlink == 1 check
2133:         // for both descriptors; the comparisons below bind remaining metadata.
2134:         let source_stamp = FileStamp::from_stat(source_stat).map_err(project_error)?;
2135:         let staged_stamp = FileStamp::from_stat(staged_stat).map_err(project_error)?;
2136:         let current_uid = rustix::process::geteuid().as_raw();
