2137:         if JournalNode::from(source_stamp.node) != source_node
2138:             || source_stamp.node == staged_stamp.node
2139:             || staged_node.is_some_and(|expected| JournalNode::from(staged_stamp.node) != expected)
2140:             || source_stat.st_uid != current_uid
2141:             || staged_stat.st_uid != current_uid
2142:             || source_stat.st_uid != staged_stat.st_uid
2143:             || source_stat.st_gid != staged_stat.st_gid
2144:             || source_stat.st_mode != staged_stat.st_mode
2145:             || source_stat.st_flags != staged_stat.st_flags
2146:             || xattrs(&source)? != xattrs(&staged)?
2147:         {
2148:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2149:         }
2150:         let (source_bytes, observed_source) =
2151:             self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, source_path)?;
2152:         let (staged_bytes, observed_staged) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, temp)?;
2153:         if source_bytes != before
2154:             || JournalNode::from(observed_source) != source_node
2155:             || JournalNode::from(observed_staged) != JournalNode::from(staged_stamp.node)
2156:             || expected_temp_bytes.is_some_and(|expected| staged_bytes != expected)
2157:         {
2158:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2159:         }
2160:         Ok(staged_stamp.node.into())
2161:     }
2162: 
2163:     fn swap(
2164:         &self,
2165:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2166:         source_path: &str,
2167:         temp: &str,
2168:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2169:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)?;
2170:         let cargo_path = lease.path.join(source_path);
2171:         let temp_path = lease.path.join(temp);
2172:         let (root, cargo_relative) = self.authority(lease, &cargo_path)?;
2173:         let (temp_root, temp_relative) = self.authority(lease, &temp_path)?;
2174:         if root.node != temp_root.node {
2175:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
2176:         }
2177:         renameat_with(
2178:             &root.directory,
2179:             &temp_relative,
2180:             &root.directory,
2181:             &cargo_relative,
2182:             RenameFlags::from_bits_retain(RENAME_SAFE_SWAP),
2183:         )
2184:         .map_err(mutation_io)
2185:     }
2186: 
2187:     #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
2188:     fn verify_swap(
2189:         &self,
2190:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2191:         source_path: &str,
2192:         temp: &str,
2193:         before: &[u8],
2194:         after: &[u8],
2195:         source_node: JournalNode,
2196:         staged_node: JournalNode,
2197:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2198:         let (active_bytes, active_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, source_path)?;
2199:         let (displaced_bytes, displaced_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, temp)?;
2200:         if active_bytes != after
2201:             || displaced_bytes != before
2202:             || JournalNode::from(active_node) != staged_node
2203:             || JournalNode::from(displaced_node) != source_node
2204:         {
2205:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2206:         }
2207:         let active = self
2208:             .workspace_entry(lease, source_path)?
2209:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2210:         let displaced = self
2211:             .workspace_entry(lease, temp)?
2212:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2213:         fsync(&active).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2214:         fcntl_fullfsync(&active).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2215:         fsync(&displaced).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2216:         fcntl_fullfsync(&displaced).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2217:         test_io_fault(IoFaultPoint::PostSwapDurability)?;
2218:         self.durable_source_parent(lease, source_path)?;
2219:         self.durable_workspace(lease)?;
2220:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)
2221:     }
2222: 
2223:     #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
2224:     fn cleanup_temp(
2225:         &self,
2226:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2227:         source_path: &str,
2228:         temp: &str,
2229:         before: &[u8],
2230:         after: &[u8],
2231:         source_node: JournalNode,
2232:         staged_node: JournalNode,
2233:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2234:         let (active, active_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, source_path)?;
2235:         let (displaced, displaced_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, temp)?;
2236:         if active != after
2237:             || displaced != before
2238:             || JournalNode::from(active_node) != staged_node
2239:             || JournalNode::from(displaced_node) != source_node
2240:         {
2241:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2242:         }
2243:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)?;
2244:         let root = self
2245:             .projects
2246:             .roots
2247:             .iter()
2248:             .find(|root| root.path == lease.path && root.node == lease.node)
2249:             .ok_or(MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
2250:         self.projects.check_root(root).map_err(project_error)?;
2251:         unlinkat(&root.directory, temp, AtFlags::empty()).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2252:         self.durable_workspace(lease)?;
2253:         if self.workspace_entry(lease, temp)?.is_some() {
2254:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2255:         }
2256:         let (active, active_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, source_path)?;
2257:         if active != after || JournalNode::from(active_node) != staged_node {
2258:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2259:         }
2260:         Ok(())
2261:     }
2262: 
2263:     #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
2264:     fn cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2265:         &self,
2266:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2267:         source_path: &str,
2268:         temp: &str,
2269:         before: &[u8],
2270:         expected_temp: Option<&[u8]>,
2271:         source_node: JournalNode,
2272:         staged_node: JournalNode,
2273:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2274:         self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2275:             lease,
2276:             source_path,
2277:             temp,
2278:             before,
2279:             expected_temp,
2280:             source_node,
2281:             Some(staged_node),
2282:         )?;
2283:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)?;
2284:         let root = self
2285:             .projects
2286:             .roots
2287:             .iter()
2288:             .find(|root| root.path == lease.path && root.node == lease.node)
2289:             .ok_or(MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
2290:         self.projects.check_root(root).map_err(project_error)?;
2291:         unlinkat(&root.directory, temp, AtFlags::empty()).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2292:         self.durable_workspace(lease)?;
2293:         if self.workspace_entry(lease, temp)?.is_some() {
2294:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2295:         }
2296:         let (active, active_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, source_path)?;
2297:         if active != before || JournalNode::from(active_node) != source_node {
2298:             return Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired);
2299:         }
2300:         Ok(())
2301:     }
2302: 
2303:     fn durable_workspace(&self, lease: &ProjectLease) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2304:         let directory = self
2305:             .projects
2306:             .open_path(&lease.path, true)
2307:             .map_err(project_error)?;
2308:         fsync(&directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2309:         fcntl_fullfsync(&directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2310:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)
2311:     }
2312: 
2313:     fn durable_source_parent(
2314:         &self,
2315:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2316:         source_path: &str,
2317:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
2318:         let relative = Path::new(source_path);
2319:         let parent = relative.parent().ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
2320:         if parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
2321:             return Ok(());
2322:         }
2323:         let directory = self
2324:             .projects
2325:             .open_path(&lease.path.join(parent), true)
2326:             .map_err(project_error)?;
2327:         fsync(&directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2328:         fcntl_fullfsync(&directory).map_err(mutation_io)?;
2329:         self.authorize_with(lease, &Continue)
2330:     }
2331: 
2332:     fn read_workspace_file(
2333:         &self,
2334:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2335:         name: &str,
2336:     ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, MutationError> {
2337:         self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, name)
2338:             .map(|(bytes, _)| bytes)
2339:     }
2340: 
2341:     fn read_workspace_file_with_node(
2342:         &self,
2343:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2344:         name: &str,
2345:     ) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Node), MutationError> {
2346:         let fd = self
2347:             .workspace_entry(lease, name)?
2348:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2349:         let before =
2350:             FileStamp::from_stat(fstat(&fd).map_err(mutation_io)?).map_err(project_error)?;
2351:         if before.size < 0 || before.size as usize > rust_engineering_domain::SOURCE_MAX_FILE_BYTES
2352:         {
2353:             return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
2354:         }
2355:         let mut file = File::from(fd);
2356:         let mut bytes = Vec::new();
2357:         (&mut file)
2358:             .take(rust_engineering_domain::SOURCE_MAX_FILE_BYTES as u64 + 1)
2359:             .read_to_end(&mut bytes)
2360:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
2361:         let after =
2362:             FileStamp::from_stat(fstat(&file).map_err(mutation_io)?).map_err(project_error)?;
2363:         if before != after || bytes.len() != before.size as usize {
2364:             return Err(MutationError::Conflict);
2365:         }
2366:         Ok((bytes, after.node))
2367:     }
2368: 
2369:     fn abort_before_effect(
2370:         &self,
2371:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2372:         body: JournalBody,
2373:         original: MutationError,
2374:     ) -> MutationError {
2375:         match self.recover_locked(lease, body) {
2376:             Ok(receipt)
2377:                 if matches!(
2378:                     receipt.state,
2379:                     MutationState::NoChange | MutationState::Aborted
2380:                 ) =>
2381:             {
2382:                 original
2383:             }
2384:             _ => MutationError::RecoveryRequired,
2385:         }
2386:     }
2387: 
2388:     fn recover_legacy_v1_locked(
2389:         &self,
2390:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2391:         mut body: JournalBody,
2392:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
2393:         let before = body.before.decode()?;
2394:         let after = body.after.decode()?;
2395:         let file = body.files.first().ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2396:         let path = file.path.clone();
2397:         let temp_path = file.temp_path.clone();
2398:         let source_node = file.source_node;
2399:         let before_bytes = source_file(&before, &path)
2400:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2401:             .bytes();
2402:         let after_bytes = source_file(&after, &path)
2403:             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2404:             .bytes();
2405:         let (active, active_node) = self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &path)?;
2406:         let temp = self
2407:             .workspace_entry(lease, &temp_path)?
2408:             .map(|_| self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &temp_path))
2409:             .transpose()?;
2410:         if temp.is_none()
2411:             && matches!(
2412:                 body.phase,
2413:                 JournalPhase::Committed | JournalPhase::NoChange | JournalPhase::Aborted
2414:             )
2415:         {
2416:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2417:             return make_receipt(&body);
2418:         }
2419:         let active_node = JournalNode::from(active_node);
2420:         let unchanged_phase = if before_bytes == after_bytes {
2421:             JournalPhase::NoChange
2422:         } else {
2423:             JournalPhase::Aborted
2424:         };
2425:         let staged_node = body.files[0].staged_node;
2426:         match (body.phase, staged_node, temp.as_ref()) {
2427:             (JournalPhase::Prepared, None, Some((temp, _)))
2428:                 if active == before_bytes
2429:                     && temp.as_slice() == before_bytes
2430:                     && active_node == source_node =>
2431:             {
2432:                 let Ok(staged_node) = self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2433:                     lease,
2434:                     &path,
2435:                     &temp_path,
2436:                     before_bytes,
2437:                     Some(before_bytes),
2438:                     source_node,
2439:                     None,
2440:                 ) else {
2441:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2442:                 };
2443:                 body.files[0].staged_node = Some(staged_node);
2444:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup;
2445:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2446:                 self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2447:                     lease,
2448:                     &path,
2449:                     &temp_path,
2450:                     before_bytes,
2451:                     None,
2452:                     source_node,
2453:                     staged_node,
2454:                 )?;
2455:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2456:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2457:             }
2458:             (JournalPhase::Scratch, Some(staged_node), Some(_))
2459:                 if active == before_bytes && active_node == source_node =>
2460:             {
2461:                 if self
2462:                     .validate_unpublished_temp(
2463:                         lease,
2464:                         &path,
2465:                         &temp_path,
2466:                         before_bytes,
2467:                         None,
2468:                         source_node,
2469:                         Some(staged_node),
2470:                     )
2471:                     .is_err()
2472:                 {
2473:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2474:                 }
2475:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup;
2476:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2477:                 self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2478:                     lease,
2479:                     &path,
2480:                     &temp_path,
2481:                     before_bytes,
2482:                     None,
2483:                     source_node,
2484:                     staged_node,
2485:                 )?;
2486:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2487:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2488:             }
2489:             (JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup, Some(staged_node), Some(_))
2490:                 if active == before_bytes && active_node == source_node =>
2491:             {
2492:                 self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2493:                     lease,
2494:                     &path,
2495:                     &temp_path,
2496:                     before_bytes,
2497:                     None,
2498:                     source_node,
2499:                     staged_node,
2500:                 )?;
2501:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2502:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2503:             }
2504:             (phase, Some(staged_node), Some((temp, temp_node)))
2505:                 if matches!(
2506:                     phase,
2507:                     JournalPhase::Staged | JournalPhase::Published | JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired
2508:                 ) && active == after_bytes
2509:                     && temp.as_slice() == before_bytes
2510:                     && active_node == staged_node
2511:                     && JournalNode::from(*temp_node) == source_node =>
2512:             {
2513:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::Published;
2514:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2515:                 self.cleanup_temp(
2516:                     lease,
2517:                     &path,
2518:                     &temp_path,
2519:                     before_bytes,
2520:                     after_bytes,
2521:                     source_node,
2522:                     staged_node,
2523:                 )?;
2524:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::Committed;
2525:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2526:             }
2527:             (phase, Some(staged_node), None)
2528:                 if matches!(
2529:                     phase,
2530:                     JournalPhase::Staged
2531:                         | JournalPhase::Published
2532:                         | JournalPhase::Committed
2533:                         | JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired
2534:                 ) && active == after_bytes
2535:                     && active_node == staged_node =>
2536:             {
2537:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::Committed;
2538:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2539:             }
2540:             (JournalPhase::Staged, Some(staged_node), Some((temp, temp_node)))
2541:                 if active == before_bytes
2542:                     && temp.as_slice() == after_bytes
2543:                     && active_node == source_node
2544:                     && JournalNode::from(*temp_node) == staged_node =>
2545:             {
2546:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup;
2547:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2548:                 self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2549:                     lease,
2550:                     &path,
2551:                     &temp_path,
2552:                     before_bytes,
2553:                     Some(after_bytes),
2554:                     source_node,
2555:                     staged_node,
2556:                 )?;
2557:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2558:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2559:             }
2560:             (phase, _, None)
2561:                 if matches!(
2562:                     phase,
2563:                     JournalPhase::Prepared
2564:                         | JournalPhase::Scratch
2565:                         | JournalPhase::Staged
2566:                         | JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup
2567:                 ) && active == before_bytes
2568:                     && active_node == source_node =>
2569:             {
2570:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2571:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2572:             }
2573:             _ => {}
2574:         }
2575:         make_receipt(&body)
2576:     }
2577: 
2578:     fn recover_locked(
2579:         &self,
2580:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2581:         body: JournalBody,
2582:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
2583:         self.recover_locked_checked(lease, body, |_| Ok(()))
2584:     }
2585: 
2586:     fn recover_locked_checked(
2587:         &self,
2588:         lease: &ProjectLease,
2589:         mut body: JournalBody,
2590:         mut checkpoint: impl FnMut(CommitCheckpoint) -> Result<(), MutationError>,
2591:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
2592:         if body.legacy_v1 {
2593:             return self.recover_legacy_v1_locked(lease, body);
2594:         }
2595:         let before = body.before.decode()?;
2596:         let after = body.after.decode()?;
2597:         if matches!(
2598:             body.phase,
2599:             JournalPhase::Committed | JournalPhase::NoChange | JournalPhase::Aborted
2600:         ) {
2601:             if self.all_temps_absent(lease, &body)? {
2602:                 return make_receipt(&body);
2603:             }
2604:             body.phase = JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired;
2605:             return make_receipt(&body);
2606:         }
2607:         let unchanged_phase = if before == after {
2608:             JournalPhase::NoChange
2609:         } else {
2610:             JournalPhase::Aborted
2611:         };
2612:         if matches!(
2613:             body.phase,
2614:             JournalPhase::Prepared
2615:                 | JournalPhase::Scratch
2616:                 | JournalPhase::Staged
2617:                 | JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup
2618:         ) {
2619:             let phase = body.phase;
2620:             for index in 0..body.files.len() {
2621:                 let file = &body.files[index];
2622:                 let before_bytes = source_file(&before, &file.path)
2623:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2624:                     .bytes();
2625:                 let (active, active_node) =
2626:                     self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &file.path)?;
2627:                 if active != before_bytes || JournalNode::from(active_node) != file.source_node {
2628:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2629:                 }
2630:                 let temp = self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?;
2631:                 if phase == JournalPhase::Prepared {
2632:                     if temp.is_some() {
2633:                         let staged_node = match self.validate_unpublished_temp(
2634:                             lease,
2635:                             &file.path,
2636:                             &file.temp_path,
2637:                             before_bytes,
2638:                             Some(before_bytes),
2639:                             file.source_node,
2640:                             None,
2641:                         ) {
2642:                             Ok(node) => node,
2643:                             Err(_) => return make_receipt(&body),
2644:                         };
2645:                         body.files[index].staged_node = Some(staged_node);
2646:                     }
2647:                 } else if temp.is_none()
2648:                     && body.operation != "manifest_patch"
2649:                     && phase != JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup
2650:                 {
2651:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2652:                 } else if temp.is_some() {
2653:                     let expected = if phase == JournalPhase::Staged {
2654:                         Some(
2655:                             source_file(&after, &file.path)
2656:                                 .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2657:                                 .bytes(),
2658:                         )
2659:                     } else {
2660:                         None
2661:                     };
2662:                     if self
2663:                         .validate_unpublished_temp(
2664:                             lease,
2665:                             &file.path,
2666:                             &file.temp_path,
2667:                             before_bytes,
2668:                             expected,
2669:                             file.source_node,
2670:                             file.staged_node,
2671:                         )
2672:                         .is_err()
2673:                     {
2674:                         return make_receipt(&body);
2675:                     }
2676:                 }
2677:             }
2678:             body.phase = JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup;
2679:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2680:             for (index, file) in body.files.iter().enumerate() {
2681:                 if self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?.is_some() {
2682:                     self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2683:                         lease,
2684:                         &file.path,
2685:                         &file.temp_path,
2686:                         source_file(&before, &file.path)
2687:                             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2688:                             .bytes(),
2689:                         None,
2690:                         file.source_node,
2691:                         file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
2692:                     )?;
2693:                     checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::FileCleaned(index))?;
2694:                 }
2695:             }
2696:             body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2697:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2698:             return make_receipt(&body);
2699:         }
2700: 
2701:         if matches!(
2702:             body.phase,
2703:             JournalPhase::Applying | JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired
2704:         ) {
2705:             let mut prefix = 0usize;
2706:             let mut saw_before = false;
2707:             let mut exclusions = BTreeMap::new();
2708:             for file in &body.files {
2709:                 let before_bytes = source_file(&before, &file.path)
2710:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2711:                     .bytes();
2712:                 let after_bytes = source_file(&after, &file.path)
2713:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2714:                     .bytes();
2715:                 let staged_node = file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2716:                 let (active, active_node) =
2717:                     self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &file.path)?;
2718:                 let temp = self
2719:                     .workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?
2720:                     .map(|_| self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &file.temp_path))
2721:                     .transpose()?;
2722:                 if temp.is_none()
2723:                     && body.operation == "manifest_patch"
2724:                     && active == before_bytes
2725:                     && JournalNode::from(active_node) == file.source_node
2726:                 {
2727:                     saw_before = true;
2728:                     continue;
2729:                 }
2730:                 let Some((temp, temp_node)) = temp else {
2731:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2732:                 };
2733:                 let published = active == after_bytes
2734:                     && JournalNode::from(active_node) == staged_node
2735:                     && temp == before_bytes
2736:                     && JournalNode::from(temp_node) == file.source_node;
2737:                 let pending = active == before_bytes
2738:                     && JournalNode::from(active_node) == file.source_node
2739:                     && temp == after_bytes
2740:                     && JournalNode::from(temp_node) == staged_node;
2741:                 if published && !saw_before {
2742:                     prefix += 1;
2743:                     exclusions.insert(lease.path.join(&file.temp_path), temp_node);
2744:                 } else if pending {
2745:                     saw_before = true;
2746:                     exclusions.insert(lease.path.join(&file.temp_path), temp_node);
2747:                 } else {
2748:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2749:                 }
2750:             }
2751:             let expected = mixed_bundle(&before, &after, &body.files, prefix)?;
2752:             let live = self
2753:                 .projects
2754:                 .source_excluding(lease, &Continue, &exclusions)
2755:                 .map_err(project_error)?;
2756:             if live != expected {
2757:                 return make_receipt(&body);
2758:             }
2759:             if prefix == 0 {
2760:                 body.phase = JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup;
2761:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2762:                 for (index, file) in body.files.iter().enumerate() {
2763:                     if self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?.is_some() {
2764:                         self.cleanup_unpublished_temp(
2765:                             lease,
2766:                             &file.path,
2767:                             &file.temp_path,
2768:                             source_file(&before, &file.path)
2769:                                 .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2770:                                 .bytes(),
2771:                             Some(
2772:                                 source_file(&after, &file.path)
2773:                                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2774:                                     .bytes(),
2775:                             ),
2776:                             file.source_node,
2777:                             file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
2778:                         )?;
2779:                         checkpoint(CommitCheckpoint::FileCleaned(index))?;
2780:                     }
2781:                 }
2782:                 body.phase = unchanged_phase;
2783:                 self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2784:                 return make_receipt(&body);
2785:             }
2786:             for file in body.files.iter().skip(prefix) {
2787:                 let before_bytes = source_file(&before, &file.path)
2788:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2789:                     .bytes();
2790:                 let after_bytes = source_file(&after, &file.path)
2791:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2792:                     .bytes();
2793:                 let staged_node = file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2794:                 self.swap(lease, &file.path, &file.temp_path)?;
2795:                 self.verify_swap(
2796:                     lease,
2797:                     &file.path,
2798:                     &file.temp_path,
2799:                     before_bytes,
2800:                     after_bytes,
2801:                     file.source_node,
2802:                     staged_node,
2803:                 )?;
2804:             }
2805:             let exclusions = self.temp_exclusions(lease, &body, true)?;
2806:             let live = self
2807:                 .projects
2808:                 .source_excluding(lease, &Continue, &exclusions)
2809:                 .map_err(project_error)?;
2810:             if live != after {
2811:                 return make_receipt(&body);
2812:             }
2813:             body.phase = JournalPhase::Published;
2814:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2815:         }
2816: 
2817:         if body.phase == JournalPhase::Published {
2818:             let mut exclusions = BTreeMap::new();
2819:             for file in &body.files {
2820:                 let after_bytes = source_file(&after, &file.path)
2821:                     .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2822:                     .bytes();
2823:                 let staged_node = file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?;
2824:                 let (active, active_node) =
2825:                     self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &file.path)?;
2826:                 if active != after_bytes || JournalNode::from(active_node) != staged_node {
2827:                     return make_receipt(&body);
2828:                 }
2829:                 if self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?.is_some() {
2830:                     let (temp, temp_node) =
2831:                         self.read_workspace_file_with_node(lease, &file.temp_path)?;
2832:                     let before_bytes = source_file(&before, &file.path)
2833:                         .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2834:                         .bytes();
2835:                     if temp != before_bytes || JournalNode::from(temp_node) != file.source_node {
2836:                         return make_receipt(&body);
2837:                     }
2838:                     exclusions.insert(lease.path.join(&file.temp_path), temp_node);
2839:                 }
2840:             }
2841:             let live = self
2842:                 .projects
2843:                 .source_excluding(lease, &Continue, &exclusions)
2844:                 .map_err(project_error)?;
2845:             if live != after {
2846:                 return make_receipt(&body);
2847:             }
2848:             for file in &body.files {
2849:                 if self.workspace_entry(lease, &file.temp_path)?.is_some() {
2850:                     self.cleanup_temp(
2851:                         lease,
2852:                         &file.path,
2853:                         &file.temp_path,
2854:                         source_file(&before, &file.path)
2855:                             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2856:                             .bytes(),
2857:                         source_file(&after, &file.path)
2858:                             .ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?
2859:                             .bytes(),
2860:                         file.source_node,
2861:                         file.staged_node.ok_or(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)?,
2862:                     )?;
2863:                 }
2864:             }
2865:             body.phase = JournalPhase::Committed;
2866:             self.persist(lease, &mut body)?;
2867:         }
2868:         make_receipt(&body)
2869:     }
2870: }
2871:

2874: mod tests;

FILE crates/project-adapter/src/mutation_port.rs
1: use crate::{ProjectLease, mutation_store::NativeMutationStore};
2: use rust_engineering_application::{MutationPublisher, OperationControl};
3: use rust_engineering_domain::{
4:     IdempotencyKey, MutationCommit, MutationError, MutationId, MutationReceipt, SourceFingerprint,
5: };
6: 
7: pub fn mutation_bytes_digest(
8:     bytes: &[u8],
9: ) -> Result<rust_engineering_domain::SourceFingerprint, MutationError> {
10:     use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
11:     use std::fmt::Write;
12:     let mut result = String::from("sha256:");
13:     for byte in Sha256::digest(bytes) {
14:         write!(result, "{byte:02x}").map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
15:     }
16:     result.parse().map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)
17: }
18: 
19: impl MutationPublisher<ProjectLease> for NativeMutationStore {
20:     fn authorize(&self, lease: &ProjectLease) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
21:         self.authorize(lease)
22:     }
23:     fn commit(
24:         &self,
25:         lease: &ProjectLease,
26:         request: &MutationCommit,
27:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
28:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
29:         self.commit(lease, request, control)
30:     }
31:     fn replay(
32:         &self,
33:         lease: &ProjectLease,
34:         id: &MutationId,
35:         digest: &SourceFingerprint,
36:         key: &IdempotencyKey,
37:         control: &dyn OperationControl,
38:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
39:         self.replay(lease, id, digest, key, control)
40:     }
41:     fn receipt(
42:         &self,
43:         lease: &ProjectLease,
44:         id: &MutationId,
45:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
46:         self.receipt(lease, id)
47:     }
48:     fn recover(
49:         &self,
50:         lease: &ProjectLease,
51:         id: &MutationId,
52:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
53:         self.recover(lease, id)
54:     }
55: }

FILE crates/project-adapter/src/mutation_store.rs
1: //! Bounded journaled publication for host-authorized local mutations.
2: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
3: use rust_engineering_application::OperationControl;
4: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
5: use rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind;
6: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
7: use rust_engineering_domain::{
8:     IdempotencyKey, MutationCommit, MutationId, MutationReceipt, MutationRecordSummary,
9: };
10: use rust_engineering_domain::{MutationCandidate, MutationError, SourceFingerprint};
11: use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
12: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
13: use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
14: 
15: const MAX_VALIDATION_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
16: 
17: fn field(hash: &mut Sha256, bytes: &[u8]) {
18:     hash.update((bytes.len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
19:     hash.update(bytes);
20: }
21: 
22: fn bundle(hash: &mut Sha256, value: &rust_engineering_domain::SourceBundle) {
23:     hash.update((value.directories().len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
24:     for directory in value.directories() {
25:         field(hash, directory.as_bytes());
26:     }
27:     hash.update((value.files().len() as u64).to_le_bytes());
28:     for file in value.files() {
29:         field(hash, file.path().as_bytes());
30:         field(hash, file.bytes());
31:     }
32: }
33: 
34: /// Exact domain-separated digest used by preview and commit.
35: pub fn mutation_digest(candidate: &MutationCandidate) -> Result<SourceFingerprint, MutationError> {
36:     if candidate.validation.len() > MAX_VALIDATION_BYTES {
37:         return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
38:     }
39:     let mut hash = Sha256::new();
40:     hash.update(b"rust-engineering-mcp/mutation-candidate/v1\0");
41:     let kind = match candidate.kind {
42:         rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind::ManifestPatch => b"manifest_patch".as_slice(),
43:         rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind::FormatApply => b"format_apply".as_slice(),
44:         rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind::FixApply => b"fix_apply".as_slice(),
45:         rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind::DependencyAdd => b"dependency_add".as_slice(),
46:         rust_engineering_domain::MutationKind::DependencyRemove => b"dependency_remove".as_slice(),
47:     };
48:     field(&mut hash, kind);
49:     bundle(&mut hash, &candidate.before);
50:     bundle(&mut hash, &candidate.after);
51:     field(&mut hash, candidate.validation.as_bytes());
52:     let mut encoded = String::from("sha256:");
53:     for byte in hash.finalize() {
54:         use std::fmt::Write;
55:         write!(&mut encoded, "{byte:02x}").map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
56:     }
57:     encoded.parse().map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)
58: }
59: 
60: #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
61: pub use crate::filesystem::NativeMutationStore;
62: 
63: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
64: pub struct NativeMutationStore;
65: 
66: #[cfg(not(target_os = "macos"))]
67: impl NativeMutationStore {
68:     pub fn open(_state_path: &Path, _write_roots: &[PathBuf]) -> Result<Self, MutationError> {
69:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
70:     }
71:     pub fn open_for_kind(
72:         _state_path: &Path,
73:         _write_roots: &[PathBuf],
74:         _kind: MutationKind,
75:     ) -> Result<Self, MutationError> {
76:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
77:     }
78:     pub fn commit(
79:         &self,
80:         _lease: &crate::ProjectLease,
81:         _request: &MutationCommit,
82:         _control: &dyn OperationControl,
83:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
84:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
85:     }
86:     pub fn replay(
87:         &self,
88:         _lease: &crate::ProjectLease,
89:         _id: &MutationId,
90:         _digest: &SourceFingerprint,
91:         _key: &IdempotencyKey,
92:         _control: &dyn OperationControl,
93:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
94:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
95:     }
96:     pub fn authorize(&self, _lease: &crate::ProjectLease) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
97:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
98:     }
99:     pub fn receipt(
100:         &self,
101:         _lease: &crate::ProjectLease,
102:         _id: &MutationId,
103:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
104:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
105:     }
106:     pub fn recover(
107:         &self,
108:         _lease: &crate::ProjectLease,
109:         _id: &MutationId,
110:     ) -> Result<MutationReceipt, MutationError> {
111:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
112:     }
113:     pub fn list_records(&self) -> Result<Vec<MutationRecordSummary>, MutationError> {
114:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
115:     }
116:     pub fn prune_record(
117:         &self,
118:         _id: &MutationId,
119:         _digest: &SourceFingerprint,
120:     ) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
121:         Err(MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform)
122:     }
123: }

19:     owned: bool,
20: }
21: impl Fixture {

23:         let mut random = [0_u8; 16];
24:         getrandom::fill(&mut random).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
25:         let base = PathBuf::from("/private/tmp").join(format!(
26:             "rust-mcp-mut-unit-{label}-{:032x}",

34:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
35:         std::fs::write(

37:             b"[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\n",
38:         )
39:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
40:         std::fs::write(project.join("src/lib.rs"), b"pub fn value() {}\n")
41:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;

43:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
44:         Ok(Self {
45:             base,
46:             project,
47:             state,
48:             owned: true,
49:         })

51: 
52:     fn request(
53:         &self,
54:         suffix: u128,
55:     ) -> Result<(SecureProjects, ProjectLease, MutationCommit), String> {
56:         let backend = SecureProjects::new(std::slice::from_ref(&self.project))
57:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
58:         let opened = backend
59:             .open(self.project.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?, &Continue)
60:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
61:         let before = backend
62:             .source(&opened.lease, &Continue)
63:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
64:         let files = before
65:                 .files()
66:                 .iter()
67:                 .map(|file| {
68:                     SourceFile::new(
69:                         file.path().to_owned(),
70:                         if file.path() == "Cargo.toml" {
71:                             b"[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\n\n[lints.rust]\nunsafe_code = \"forbid\"\n".to_vec()
72:                         } else {
73:                             file.bytes().to_vec()
74:                         },
75:                     )
76:                 })
77:                 .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
78:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
79:         let after = SourceBundle::with_directories(files, before.directories().to_vec())
80:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
81:         let candidate = MutationCandidate {
82:             kind: MutationKind::ManifestPatch,
83:             before,
84:             after,
85:             validation: "unit-checkpoint".to_owned(),
86:         };
87:         let request = MutationCommit {
88:             id: MutationId::new(format!("mut_{suffix:032x}"))
89:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
90:             digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
91:             key: IdempotencyKey::new(format!("checkpoint-{suffix}"))
92:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
93:             candidate,
94:         };
95:         Ok((backend, opened.lease, request))
96:     }
97: 
98:     fn format_request(
99:         &self,
100:         suffix: u128,
101:     ) -> Result<(SecureProjects, ProjectLease, MutationCommit), String> {
102:         let backend = SecureProjects::new(std::slice::from_ref(&self.project))
103:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
104:         let opened = backend
105:             .open(self.project.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?, &Continue)
106:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
107:         let before = backend
108:             .source(&opened.lease, &Continue)
109:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
110:         let files = before
111:             .files()
112:             .iter()
113:             .map(|file| {
114:                 let bytes = match file.path() {
115:                     "src/lib.rs" => b"pub fn value() {\n}\n".to_vec(),
116:                     "src/other.rs" => b"pub fn other() -> u8 {\n    7\n}\n".to_vec(),
117:                     _ => file.bytes().to_vec(),
118:                 };
119:                 SourceFile::new(file.path().to_owned(), bytes)
120:             })
121:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
122:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
123:         let after = SourceBundle::with_directories(files, before.directories().to_vec())
124:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
125:         let candidate = MutationCandidate {
126:             kind: MutationKind::FormatApply,
127:             before,
128:             after,
129:             validation: "unit-format-checkpoint".to_owned(),
130:         };
131:         let request = MutationCommit {
132:             id: MutationId::new(format!("mut_{suffix:032x}"))
133:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
134:             digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
135:             key: IdempotencyKey::new(format!("format-checkpoint-{suffix}"))
136:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
137:             candidate,
138:         };
139:         Ok((backend, opened.lease, request))
140:     }
141: }
142: impl Drop for Fixture {
143:     fn drop(&mut self) {
144:         if self.owned {
145:             let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.base);
146:         }
147:     }
148: }
149: 
150: fn snapshot_tree(root: &Path) -> Result<BTreeMap<PathBuf, Option<Vec<u8>>>, String> {
151:     fn visit(
152:         root: &Path,
153:         relative: &Path,
154:         snapshot: &mut BTreeMap<PathBuf, Option<Vec<u8>>>,
155:     ) -> Result<(), String> {
156:         let mut entries = std::fs::read_dir(root.join(relative))
157:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
158:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
159:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
160:         entries.sort_by_key(std::fs::DirEntry::file_name);
161:         for entry in entries {
162:             let child = relative.join(entry.file_name());
163:             let kind = entry.file_type().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
164:             if kind.is_dir() {
165:                 snapshot.insert(child.clone(), None);
166:                 visit(root, &child, snapshot)?;
167:             } else if kind.is_file() {
168:                 snapshot.insert(
169:                     child.clone(),
170:                     Some(std::fs::read(root.join(&child)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?),
171:                 );
172:             } else {
173:                 return Err(format!("unsupported snapshot entry: {}", child.display()));
174:             }
175:         }
176:         Ok(())
177:     }
178: 
179:     let mut snapshot = BTreeMap::new();
180:     visit(root, Path::new(""), &mut snapshot)?;
181:     Ok(snapshot)
182: }
183: 
184: fn write_reviewed_bundle(root: &Path, bundle: &SourceBundle) -> Result<(), String> {
185:     for directory in bundle.directories() {
186:         std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join(directory)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
187:     }
188:     for file in bundle.files() {
189:         let path = root.join(file.path());
190:         if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
191:             std::fs::create_dir_all(parent).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
192:         }
193:         std::fs::write(path, file.bytes()).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
194:     }
195:     Ok(())
196: }
197: 
198: fn manifest_lock_request(
199:     fixture: &Fixture,
200:     suffix: u128,
201: ) -> Result<(SecureProjects, ProjectLease, MutationCommit), String> {
202:     std::fs::write(
203:         fixture.project.join("Cargo.lock"),
204:         b"# before lock\nversion = 4\n",
205:     )
206:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
207:     let backend = SecureProjects::new(std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
208:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
209:     let opened = backend
210:         .open(fixture.project.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?, &Continue)
211:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
212:     let before = backend
213:         .source(&opened.lease, &Continue)
214:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
215:     let files = before
216:         .files()
217:         .iter()
218:         .map(|file| {
219:             let bytes = match file.path() {
220:                 "Cargo.lock" => b"# after lock\nversion = 4\n".to_vec(),
221:                 "Cargo.toml" => b"[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\n\n[features]\ndefault = [\"dep:serde\"]\n".to_vec(),
222:                 _ => file.bytes().to_vec(),
223:             };
224:             SourceFile::new(file.path().to_owned(), bytes)
225:         })
226:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
227:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
228:     let after = SourceBundle::with_directories(files, before.directories().to_vec())
229:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
230:     let candidate = MutationCandidate {
231:         kind: MutationKind::ManifestPatch,
232:         before,
233:         after,
234:         validation: "unit-manifest-lock-checkpoint".to_owned(),
235:     };
236:     let request = MutationCommit {
237:         id: MutationId::new(format!("mut_{suffix:032x}")).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
238:         digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
239:         key: IdempotencyKey::new(format!("manifest-lock-checkpoint-{suffix}"))
240:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
241:         candidate,
242:     };
243:     Ok((backend, opened.lease, request))
244: }
245: 
246: fn encode_legacy_v1(body: &JournalBody) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
247:     let file = body.files.first().ok_or("file")?;
248:     let phase = match body.phase {
249:         JournalPhase::Prepared => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Prepared,
250:         JournalPhase::Scratch => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Scratch,
251:         JournalPhase::Staged => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Staged,
252:         JournalPhase::Published => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Published,
253:         JournalPhase::AbortedCleanup => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::AbortedCleanup,
254:         JournalPhase::Committed => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Committed,
255:         JournalPhase::NoChange => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::NoChange,
256:         JournalPhase::Aborted => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::Aborted,
257:         JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired => LegacyJournalPhaseV1::RecoveryRequired,
258:         JournalPhase::Applying => return Err("v1 has no applying phase".to_owned()),
259:     };
260:     let legacy = LegacyJournalBodyV1 {
261:         id: body.id.clone(),
262:         digest: body.digest.clone(),
263:         key: body.key.clone(),
264:         operation: body.operation.clone(),
265:         workspace_path: body.workspace_path.clone(),
266:         workspace_device: body.workspace_device,
267:         workspace_inode: body.workspace_inode,
268:         temp_path: legacy_temp_name(
269:             &MutationId::new(body.id.clone()).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
270:         ),
271:         source_node: file.source_node,
272:         staged_node: file.staged_node,
273:         validation: body.validation.clone(),
274:         sequence: body.sequence,
275:         phase,
276:         before: body.before.clone(),
277:         after: body.after.clone(),
278:     };
279:     let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&legacy).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
280:     serde_json::to_vec(&LegacyJournalRecordV1 {
281:         format: "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v1".to_owned(),
282:         checksum: sha256(&canonical),
283:         body: legacy,
284:     })
285:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
286: }
287: 
288: fn encode_v2_buffered_reference(mut body: JournalBody) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
289:     body.format = JournalFormat::V2;
290:     let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&body).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
291:     serde_json::to_vec(&JournalRecordV2 {
292:         format: "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2".to_owned(),
293:         checksum: sha256(&canonical),
294:         body,
295:     })
296:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())
297: }

299: #[test]
300: fn streamed_checksums_preserve_buffered_v1_and_v2_bytes() -> Result<(), String> {
301:     let fixture = Fixture::new("streamed-checksum-compatibility")?;
302:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(147)?;
303:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
304:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
305:     let interrupted = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
306:         let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
307:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Prepared {
308:                 std::panic::resume_unwind(Box::new("capture prepared journal"));
309:             }
310:             Ok(())
311:         });
312:     }));
313:     assert!(interrupted.is_err());
314:     let raw = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(journal_name(&request.id)))
315:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
316:     let mut body = decode(&raw).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
317:     body.sequence = u64::MAX;
318:     body.phase = JournalPhase::RecoveryRequired;
319:     body.files[0].staged_node = Some(JournalNode {
320:         device: i32::MIN,
321:         inode: u64::MAX,
322:     });
323:     let canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&body).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
324:     assert_eq!(
325:         canonical_checksum(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
326:         sha256(&canonical)
327:     );
328:     assert_eq!(
329:         encode(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
330:         encode_v2_buffered_reference(body.clone())?
331:     );
332: 
333:     body.sequence = 7;
334:     body.phase = JournalPhase::Staged;
335:     let legacy = encode_legacy_v1(&body)?;
336:     let parsed: LegacyJournalRecordV1 =
337:         serde_json::from_slice(&legacy).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
338:     let legacy_canonical = serde_json::to_vec(&parsed.body).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
339:     assert_eq!(
340:         canonical_checksum(&parsed.body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
341:         sha256(&legacy_canonical)
342:     );
343:     assert_eq!(
344:         decode(&legacy)
345:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
346:             .format,
347:         JournalFormat::V1
348:     );
349:     Ok(())
350: }

352: #[test]
353: fn legacy_v1_receipt_is_read_only_and_explicit_recovery_migrates_to_v2() -> Result<(), String> {
354:     let fixture = Fixture::new("legacy-v1")?;
355:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(144)?;
356:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
357:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
358:     let interrupted = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
359:         let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
360:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Staged {
361:                 std::panic::resume_unwind(Box::new("abrupt legacy fixture"));
362:             }
363:             Ok(())
364:         });
365:     }));
366:     assert!(interrupted.is_err());
367:     let name = journal_name(&request.id);
368:     let raw = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
369:     let body = decode(&raw).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
370:     let indexed = fixture.project.join(&body.files[0].temp_path);
371:     let legacy = fixture.project.join(legacy_temp_name(&request.id));
372:     std::fs::rename(indexed, &legacy).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
373:     let legacy_record = encode_legacy_v1(&body)?;
374:     std::fs::write(fixture.state.join(&name), &legacy_record).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
375:     std::fs::set_permissions(
376:         fixture.state.join(&name),
377:         std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600),
378:     )
379:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
380: 
381:     assert_eq!(
382:         store
383:             .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
384:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
385:             .state,
386:         MutationState::RecoveryRequired
387:     );
388:     assert_eq!(
389:         store.list_records().map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?[0].state,
390:         MutationState::RecoveryRequired
391:     );
392:     assert_eq!(
393:         std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
394:         legacy_record
395:     );
396:     assert_eq!(
397:         store
398:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
399:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
400:             .state,
401:         MutationState::Aborted
402:     );
403:     assert!(!legacy.exists());
404:     let migrated = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
405:     let format: JournalFormatProbe =
406:         serde_json::from_slice(&migrated).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
407:     assert_eq!(format.format, "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2");
408:     Ok(())
409: }

411: #[test]
412: fn terminal_legacy_v1_replay_migrates_only_after_exact_binding() -> Result<(), String> {
413:     let fixture = Fixture::new("legacy-v1-terminal-replay")?;
414:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(149)?;
415:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
416:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
417:     let committed = store
418:         .commit(&lease, &request, &Continue)
419:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
420:     let name = journal_name(&request.id);
421:     let raw = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
422:     let body = decode(&raw).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
423:     let legacy = encode_legacy_v1(&body)?;
424:     std::fs::write(fixture.state.join(&name), &legacy).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
425: 
426:     let wrong_key =
427:         IdempotencyKey::new("wrong-legacy-key".to_owned()).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
428:     assert_eq!(
429:         store.replay(&lease, &request.id, &request.digest, &wrong_key, &Continue,),
430:         Err(MutationError::Conflict)
431:     );
432:     assert_eq!(
433:         std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
434:         legacy
435:     );
436:     assert_eq!(
437:         store.replay(
438:             &lease,
439:             &request.id,
440:             &request.digest,
441:             &request.key,
442:             &Continue,
443:         ),
444:         Ok(committed)
445:     );
446:     let migrated = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
447:     let format: JournalFormatProbe =
448:         serde_json::from_slice(&migrated).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
449:     assert_eq!(format.format, "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2");
450:     Ok(())
451: }

453: #[test]
454: fn unknown_journal_format_never_cleans_or_changes_source() -> Result<(), String> {
455:     let fixture = Fixture::new("unknown-v3")?;
456:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(145)?;
457:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
458:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
459:     let interrupted = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
460:         let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
461:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Staged {
462:                 std::panic::resume_unwind(Box::new("abrupt unknown-format fixture"));
463:             }
464:             Ok(())
465:         });
466:     }));
467:     assert!(interrupted.is_err());
468:     let name = journal_name(&request.id);
469:     let raw = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&name)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
470:     let text = std::str::from_utf8(&raw).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
471:     let unknown = text.replace(
472:         "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v2",
473:         "rust-engineering-mcp-mutation-journal-v3",
474:     );
475:     std::fs::write(fixture.state.join(&name), unknown.as_bytes())
476:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
477:     let temp = fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
478:     let before = source_file(&request.candidate.before, "Cargo.toml")
479:         .ok_or("before")?
480:         .bytes()
481:         .to_vec();
482:     let state_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?;
483:     let project_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?;
484:     assert_eq!(
485:         store.receipt(&lease, &request.id),
486:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
487:     );
488:     assert_eq!(
489:         store.recover(&lease, &request.id),
490:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
491:     );
492:     assert_eq!(
493:         store.replay(
494:             &lease,
495:             &request.id,
496:             &request.digest,
497:             &request.key,
498:             &Continue,
499:         ),
500:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
501:     );
502:     assert!(temp.exists());
503:     assert_eq!(
504:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
505:         before
506:     );
507:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?, state_snapshot);
508:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?, project_snapshot);
509:     Ok(())
510: }

512: #[test]
513: fn terminal_replay_requires_the_exact_binding_and_never_reapplies_source() -> Result<(), String> {
514:     let fixture = Fixture::new("terminal-durable-replay")?;
515:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(146)?;
516:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
517:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
518:     let committed = store
519:         .commit(&lease, &request, &Continue)
520:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
521:     assert_eq!(committed.state, MutationState::Committed);
522: 
523:     let external = b"[package]\nname = \"fixture\"\nversion = \"0.1.0\"\nedition = \"2024\"\n# later user edit\n";
524:     std::fs::write(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml"), external)
525:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
526:     let state_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?;
527:     let project_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?;
528:     assert_eq!(
529:         store.replay(
530:             &lease,
531:             &request.id,
532:             &request.digest,
533:             &request.key,
534:             &Continue,
535:         ),
536:         Ok(committed)
537:     );
538: 
539:     let wrong_digest: SourceFingerprint = format!("sha256:{}", "0".repeat(64))
540:         .parse()
541:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
542:     assert_ne!(wrong_digest, request.digest);
543:     assert_eq!(
544:         store.replay(&lease, &request.id, &wrong_digest, &request.key, &Continue,),
545:         Err(MutationError::Conflict)
546:     );
547:     let wrong_key =
548:         IdempotencyKey::new("wrong-replay-key".to_owned()).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
549:     assert_eq!(
550:         store.replay(&lease, &request.id, &request.digest, &wrong_key, &Continue,),
551:         Err(MutationError::Conflict)
552:     );
553: 
554:     let other = Fixture::new("terminal-durable-replay-other")?;
555:     let (_other_backend, other_lease, _) = other.request(147)?;
556:     assert_eq!(
557:         store.replay(
558:             &other_lease,
559:             &request.id,
560:             &request.digest,
561:             &request.key,
562:             &Continue,
563:         ),
564:         Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied)
565:     );
566:     let wrong_kind = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
567:         &fixture.state,
568:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
569:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
570:     )
571:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
572:     assert_eq!(
573:         wrong_kind.replay(
574:             &lease,
575:             &request.id,
576:             &request.digest,
577:             &request.key,
578:             &Continue,
579:         ),
580:         Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied)
581:     );
582:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?, state_snapshot);
583:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?, project_snapshot);
584:     Ok(())
585: }

587: #[test]
588: fn pending_replay_uses_existing_recovery_and_pruned_replay_is_not_found() -> Result<(), String> {
589:     struct Cancel;
590:     impl OperationControl for Cancel {
591:         fn check(&self) -> Result<(), ProjectError> {
592:             Err(ProjectError::Cancelled)
593:         }
594:     }
595: 
596:     let fixture = Fixture::new("pending-durable-replay")?;
597:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(148)?;
598:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
599:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
600:     assert_eq!(
601:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
602:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Published {
603:                 Err(MutationError::Io)
604:             } else {
605:                 Ok(())
606:             }
607:         }),
608:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
609:     );
610:     let pending_state = snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?;
611:     let pending_project = snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?;
612:     assert_eq!(
613:         store.replay(&lease, &request.id, &request.digest, &request.key, &Cancel,),
614:         Err(MutationError::Cancelled)
615:     );
616:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.state)?, pending_state);
617:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&fixture.project)?, pending_project);
618: 
619:     let source_after = source_file(&request.candidate.after, "Cargo.toml")
620:         .ok_or("after")?
621:         .bytes()
622:         .to_vec();
623:     let recovered = store
624:         .replay(
625:             &lease,
626:             &request.id,
627:             &request.digest,
628:             &request.key,
629:             &Continue,
630:         )
631:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
632:     assert_eq!(recovered.state, MutationState::Committed);
633:     assert_eq!(
634:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
635:         source_after
636:     );
637:     assert!(!fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0)).exists());
638: 
639:     store
640:         .prune_record(&request.id, &request.digest)
641:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
642:     assert_eq!(
643:         store.replay(
644:             &lease,
645:             &request.id,
646:             &request.digest,
647:             &request.key,
648:             &Continue,
649:         ),
650:         Err(MutationError::NotFound)
651:     );
652:     assert_eq!(
653:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
654:         source_after
655:     );
656:     let orphan = fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
657:     std::fs::write(&orphan, b"unbound reserved leaf").map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
658:     assert_eq!(
659:         store.replay(
660:             &lease,
661:             &request.id,
662:             &request.digest,
663:             &request.key,
664:             &Continue,
665:         ),
666:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
667:     );
668:     assert_eq!(
669:         std::fs::read(&orphan).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
670:         b"unbound reserved leaf"
671:     );
672:     assert_eq!(
673:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
674:         source_after
675:     );
676:     Ok(())
677: }

679: #[test]
680: fn abrupt_checkpoint_helper() -> Result<(), String> {
681:     let Some(project) = std::env::var_os(CRASH_PROJECT) else {
682:         return Ok(());
683:     };
684:     let project = PathBuf::from(project);
685:     let state = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os(CRASH_STATE).ok_or("state")?);
686:     let ready = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os(CRASH_READY).ok_or("ready")?);
687:     let requested = std::env::var(CRASH_PHASE).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
688:     let fixture = Fixture {
689:         base: project.parent().ok_or("base")?.to_path_buf(),
690:         project,
691:         state,
692:         owned: false,
693:     };
694:     let format = std::env::var(CRASH_KIND).is_ok_and(|kind| kind == "format");
695:     let (_backend, lease, request) = if format {
696:         fixture.format_request(CRASH_SUFFIX)?
697:     } else {
698:         fixture.request(CRASH_SUFFIX)?
699:     };
700:     let kind = if format {
701:         MutationKind::FormatApply
702:     } else {
703:         MutationKind::ManifestPatch
704:     };
705:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
706:         &fixture.state,
707:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
708:         kind,
709:     )
710:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
711:     let target = match requested.as_str() {
712:         "clone_created" | "clone_unknown" => CommitCheckpoint::CloneCreated,
713:         "file_cloned_0" => CommitCheckpoint::FileCloned(0),
714:         "scratch" => CommitCheckpoint::Scratch,
715:         "applying" => CommitCheckpoint::Applying,
716:         "swapped" => CommitCheckpoint::Swapped,
717:         "file_swapped_0" => CommitCheckpoint::FileSwapped(0),
718:         "file_swapped_1" => CommitCheckpoint::FileSwapped(1),
719:         "file_cleaned_0" => CommitCheckpoint::FileCleaned(0),
720:         "published" => CommitCheckpoint::Published,
721:         _ => return Err("unknown crash phase".to_owned()),
722:     };
723:     let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
724:         if phase == target {
725:             std::fs::write(&ready, b"ready").map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
726:             loop {
727:                 std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(1));
728:             }
729:         }
730:         Ok(())
731:     });
732:     Err("checkpoint helper unexpectedly completed".to_owned())
733: }

735: #[test]
736: fn killed_process_recovers_each_durable_boundary() -> Result<(), String> {
737:     use std::process::Command;
738: 
739:     for phase in ["clone_created", "clone_unknown", "scratch", "swapped"] {
740:         let fixture = Fixture::new(&format!("process-crash-{phase}"))?;
741:         let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(CRASH_SUFFIX)?;
742:         let ready = fixture.base.join("crash-ready");
743:         let mut child = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?)
744:             .arg("--exact")
745:             .arg("filesystem::macos::mutation::tests::abrupt_checkpoint_helper")
746:             .arg("--nocapture")
747:             .env(CRASH_PROJECT, &fixture.project)
748:             .env(CRASH_STATE, &fixture.state)
749:             .env(CRASH_READY, &ready)
750:             .env(CRASH_PHASE, phase)
751:             .spawn()
752:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
753:         let mut observed = false;
754:         for _ in 0..500 {
755:             if ready.exists() {
756:                 observed = true;
757:                 break;
758:             }
759:             std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));
760:         }
761:         if !observed {
762:             let _ = child.kill();
763:             let _ = child.wait();
764:             return Err(format!("crash helper {phase} did not reach its checkpoint"));
765:         }
766:         child.kill().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
767:         let status = child.wait().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
768:         assert!(!status.success(), "killed helper unexpectedly succeeded");
769: 
770:         let temp = fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
771:         if phase == "clone_unknown" {
772:             std::fs::write(&temp, b"unknown unjournaled clone bytes")
773:                 .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
774:         }
775: 
776:         let restarted =
777:             NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
778:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
779:         let receipt = restarted
780:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
781:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
782:         let expected = match phase {
783:             "clone_unknown" => MutationState::RecoveryRequired,
784:             "swapped" => MutationState::Committed,
785:             _ => MutationState::Aborted,
786:         };
787:         assert_eq!(receipt.state, expected);
788:         assert_eq!(temp.exists(), phase == "clone_unknown");
789:         if phase == "clone_unknown" {
790:             assert_eq!(
791:                 std::fs::read(&temp).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
792:                 b"unknown unjournaled clone bytes"
793:             );
794:         }
795:         let expected_bytes = if expected == MutationState::Committed {
796:             source_file(&request.candidate.after, "Cargo.toml")
797:         } else {
798:             source_file(&request.candidate.before, "Cargo.toml")
799:         }
800:         .ok_or("cargo")?
801:         .bytes();
802:         assert_eq!(
803:             std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
804:             expected_bytes
805:         );
806:     }
807:     Ok(())
808: }

810: #[test]
811: fn killed_process_rolls_forward_known_format_prefix() -> Result<(), String> {
812:     use std::process::Command;
813: 
814:     for phase in [
815:         "file_cloned_0",
816:         "applying",
817:         "file_swapped_0",
818:         "file_swapped_1",
819:         "published",
820:         "file_cleaned_0",
821:     ] {
822:         let fixture = Fixture::new(&format!("format-prefix-crash-{phase}"))?;
823:         let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.format_request(CRASH_SUFFIX)?;
824:         let ready = fixture.base.join("format-crash-ready");
825:         let mut child = Command::new(std::env::current_exe().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?)
826:             .arg("--exact")
827:             .arg("filesystem::macos::mutation::tests::abrupt_checkpoint_helper")
828:             .arg("--nocapture")
829:             .env(CRASH_PROJECT, &fixture.project)
830:             .env(CRASH_STATE, &fixture.state)
831:             .env(CRASH_READY, &ready)
832:             .env(CRASH_PHASE, phase)
833:             .env(CRASH_KIND, "format")
834:             .spawn()
835:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
836:         let mut observed = false;
837:         for _ in 0..500 {
838:             if ready.exists() {
839:                 observed = true;
840:                 break;
841:             }
842:             std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));
843:         }
844:         if !observed {
845:             let _ = child.kill();
846:             let _ = child.wait();
847:             return Err(format!("format crash helper did not reach {phase}"));
848:         }
849:         child.kill().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
850:         let status = child.wait().map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
851:         assert!(!status.success(), "killed helper unexpectedly succeeded");
852: 
853:         let first_temp = fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
854:         let second_temp = fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 1));
855:         if phase == "file_cloned_0" {
856:             assert!(first_temp.exists());
857:             assert!(!second_temp.exists());
858:         } else if phase == "file_cleaned_0" {
859:             assert!(!first_temp.exists());
860:             assert!(second_temp.exists());
861:         }
862: 
863:         let restarted = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
864:             &fixture.state,
865:             std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
866:             MutationKind::FormatApply,
867:         )
868:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
869:         let receipt = restarted
870:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
871:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
872:         let expected = if matches!(phase, "file_cloned_0" | "applying") {
873:             MutationState::Aborted
874:         } else {
875:             MutationState::Committed
876:         };
877:         assert_eq!(receipt.state, expected);
878:         for file in &receipt.files {
879:             let expected_file = if expected == MutationState::Committed {
880:                 source_file(&request.candidate.after, &file.path)
881:             } else {
882:                 source_file(&request.candidate.before, &file.path)
883:             }
884:             .ok_or("expected")?;
885:             assert_eq!(
886:                 std::fs::read(fixture.project.join(&file.path))
887:                     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
888:                 expected_file.bytes()
889:             );
890:         }
891:         assert!(
892:             std::fs::read_dir(&fixture.project)
893:                 .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
894:                 .all(|entry| entry
895:                     .ok()
896:                     .and_then(|entry| entry.file_name().into_string().ok())
897:                     .is_none_or(|name| !name.starts_with(".rust-mcp-mut-")))
898:         );
899:     }
900:     Ok(())
901: }

938:         .candidate
939:         .after
940:         .files()
941:         .iter()
942:         .position(|file| file.path() == "src/lib.rs")

945:         source_file(&request.candidate.before, "src/other.rs").ok_or("second before")?;
946:     assert_eq!(
947:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("src/lib.rs")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
948:         first.bytes()
949:     );
950:     assert_eq!(
951:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("src/other.rs")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
952:         second_before.bytes()
953:     );
954:     Ok(())
955: }
