957: #[test]
958: fn manifest_and_lock_first_swap_crash_rolls_forward_only_known_bytes() -> Result<(), String> {
959:     let fixture = Fixture::new("manifest-lock-prefix")?;
960:     let (_backend, lease, request) = manifest_lock_request(&fixture, 240)?;
961:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
962:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
963:     let interrupted = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
964:         let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
965:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::FileSwapped(0) {
966:                 std::panic::resume_unwind(Box::new("manifest lock prefix crash"));
967:             }
968:             Ok(())
969:         });
970:     }));
971:     assert!(interrupted.is_err());
972:     assert_eq!(
973:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.lock")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
974:         source_file(&request.candidate.after, "Cargo.lock")
975:             .ok_or("after lock")?
976:             .bytes()
977:     );
978:     assert_eq!(
979:         std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
980:         source_file(&request.candidate.before, "Cargo.toml")
981:             .ok_or("before manifest")?
982:             .bytes()
983:     );
984:     drop(store);
985:     let restarted =
986:         NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
987:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
988:     assert_eq!(
989:         restarted
990:             .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
991:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
992:             .state,
993:         MutationState::RecoveryRequired
994:     );
995:     assert_eq!(
996:         restarted
997:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
998:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
999:             .state,
1000:         MutationState::Committed
1001:     );
1002:     for path in ["Cargo.lock", "Cargo.toml"] {
1003:         assert_eq!(
1004:             std::fs::read(fixture.project.join(path)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1005:             source_file(&request.candidate.after, path)
1006:                 .ok_or("after file")?
1007:                 .bytes()
1008:         );
1009:     }
1010:     Ok(())
1011: }

1056: fn lost_temp_and_out_of_order_generation_remain_recovery_required() -> Result<(), String> {
1057:     let lost = Fixture::new("format-lost-temp")?;
1058:     let (_backend, lease, request) = lost.format_request(142)?;
1059:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1060:         &lost.state,
1061:         std::slice::from_ref(&lost.project),
1062:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1063:     )
1064:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1065:     let first_temp = lost.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
1066:     assert_eq!(
1067:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1068:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Staged {
1069:                 std::fs::remove_file(&first_temp).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1070:                 return Err(MutationError::Io);
1071:             }
1072:             Ok(())
1073:         }),
1074:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1075:     );
1076:     assert_eq!(
1077:         store
1078:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
1079:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1080:             .state,
1081:         MutationState::RecoveryRequired
1082:     );
1083:     assert!(lost.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 1)).exists());
1084: 
1085:     let applying = Fixture::new("format-lost-applying-temp")?;
1086:     let (_backend, lease, request) = applying.format_request(144)?;
1087:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1088:         &applying.state,
1089:         std::slice::from_ref(&applying.project),
1090:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1091:     )
1092:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1093:     let first_temp = applying.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0));
1094:     assert_eq!(
1095:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1096:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Applying {
1097:                 std::fs::remove_file(&first_temp).map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1098:                 return Err(MutationError::Io);
1099:             }
1100:             Ok(())
1101:         }),
1102:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1103:     );
1104:     let pending = store
1105:         .recover(&lease, &request.id)
1106:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1107:     assert_eq!(pending.state, MutationState::RecoveryRequired);
1108:     assert!(
1109:         pending
1110:             .files
1111:             .iter()
1112:             .all(|file| file.effect_after.is_none() && file.effect_after_bytes.is_none())
1113:     );
1114:     assert!(applying.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 1)).exists());
1115: 
1116:     let ordered = Fixture::new("format-out-of-order")?;
1117:     let (_backend, lease, request) = ordered.format_request(143)?;
1118:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1119:         &ordered.state,
1120:         std::slice::from_ref(&ordered.project),
1121:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1122:     )
1123:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1124:     assert_eq!(
1125:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1126:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::FileSwapped(0) {
1127:                 store.swap(&lease, "src/lib.rs", &temp_name(&request.id, 0))?;
1128:                 store.swap(&lease, "src/other.rs", &temp_name(&request.id, 1))?;
1129:                 return Err(MutationError::Io);
1130:             }
1131:             Ok(())
1132:         }),
1133:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1134:     );
1135:     assert_eq!(
1136:         store
1137:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
1138:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1139:             .state,
1140:         MutationState::RecoveryRequired
1141:     );
1142:     assert_eq!(
1143:         std::fs::read(ordered.project.join("src/lib.rs")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1144:         source_file(&request.candidate.before, "src/lib.rs")
1145:             .ok_or("first")?
1146:             .bytes()
1147:     );
1148:     assert_eq!(
1149:         std::fs::read(ordered.project.join("src/other.rs")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1150:         source_file(&request.candidate.after, "src/other.rs")
1151:             .ok_or("second")?
1152:             .bytes()
1153:     );
1154:     Ok(())
1155: }

1157: #[test]
1158: fn aborted_cleanup_resumes_after_a_durable_missing_temp_prefix() -> Result<(), String> {
1159:     let fixture = Fixture::new("format-aborted-cleanup-prefix")?;
1160:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.format_request(219)?;
1161:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1162:         &fixture.state,
1163:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
1164:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1165:     )
1166:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1167:     let interrupted = std::panic::catch_unwind(std::panic::AssertUnwindSafe(|| {
1168:         let _ = store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1169:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Staged {
1170:                 std::panic::resume_unwind(Box::new("simulated abrupt stop before recovery"));
1171:             }
1172:             Ok(())
1173:         });
1174:     }));
1175:     assert!(interrupted.is_err());
1176:     let body = store
1177:         .load_repair(&lease, &request.id)
1178:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1179:         .ok_or("journal")?;
1180:     assert_eq!(body.phase, JournalPhase::Staged);
1181:     assert_eq!(
1182:         store.recover_locked_checked(&lease, body, |checkpoint| {
1183:             if checkpoint == CommitCheckpoint::FileCleaned(0) {
1184:                 Err(MutationError::Io)
1185:             } else {
1186:                 Ok(())
1187:             }
1188:         }),
1189:         Err(MutationError::Io)
1190:     );
1191:     assert!(!fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0)).exists());
1192:     assert!(fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 1)).exists());
1193:     drop(store);
1194: 
1195:     let restarted = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1196:         &fixture.state,
1197:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
1198:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1199:     )
1200:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1201:     assert_eq!(
1202:         restarted
1203:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
1204:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1205:             .state,
1206:         MutationState::Aborted
1207:     );
1208:     for index in 0..2 {
1209:         assert!(!fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, index)).exists());
1210:     }
1211:     for path in ["src/lib.rs", "src/other.rs"] {
1212:         assert_eq!(
1213:             std::fs::read(fixture.project.join(path)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1214:             source_file(&request.candidate.before, path)
1215:                 .ok_or("before")?
1216:                 .bytes()
1217:         );
1218:     }
1219:     Ok(())
1220: }

1310: #[test]
1311: fn short_journal_writes_fail_closed_at_every_commit_phase() -> Result<(), String> {
1312:     for (index, phase) in [
1313:         JournalPhase::Prepared,
1314:         JournalPhase::Scratch,
1315:         JournalPhase::Staged,
1316:         JournalPhase::Applying,
1317:         JournalPhase::Published,
1318:         JournalPhase::Committed,
1319:     ]
1320:     .into_iter()
1321:     .enumerate()
1322:     {
1323:         let fixture = Fixture::new(&format!("short-journal-{phase:?}"))?;
1324:         let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(300 + index as u128)?;
1325:         let store =
1326:             NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
1327:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1328:         let _fault = inject_test_io_fault(
1329:             IoFaultPoint::JournalWrite(phase),
1330:             TestIoFaultMode::ShortWriteThenNoSpace,
1331:         );
1332:         let result = store.commit(&lease, &request, &Continue);
1333:         assert_eq!(
1334:             result,
1335:             Err(if phase == JournalPhase::Prepared {
1336:                 MutationError::Io
1337:             } else {
1338:                 MutationError::RecoveryRequired
1339:             }),
1340:             "phase {phase:?}"
1341:         );
1342: 
1343:         let partial = if phase == JournalPhase::Prepared {
1344:             fixture.state.join(journal_name(&request.id))
1345:         } else {
1346:             fixture
1347:                 .state
1348:                 .join(format!(".{}.staging", journal_name(&request.id)))
1349:         };
1350:         let partial_bytes = std::fs::read(&partial).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
1351:         assert!(!partial_bytes.is_empty(), "phase {phase:?}");
1352:         assert_eq!(decode(&partial_bytes), Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired));
1353:         assert_eq!(
1354:             store.receipt(&lease, &request.id),
1355:             Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
1356:             "phase {phase:?}"
1357:         );
1358:         assert_eq!(
1359:             store.recover(&lease, &request.id),
1360:             Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
1361:             "phase {phase:?}"
1362:         );
1363:         assert_eq!(
1364:             store.replay(
1365:                 &lease,
1366:                 &request.id,
1367:                 &request.digest,
1368:                 &request.key,
1369:                 &Continue,
1370:             ),
1371:             Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired),
1372:             "phase {phase:?}"
1373:         );
1374:         assert_eq!(
1375:             std::fs::read(&partial).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1376:             partial_bytes,
1377:             "phase {phase:?}"
1378:         );
1379: 
1380:         let expected = if matches!(phase, JournalPhase::Published | JournalPhase::Committed) {
1381:             &request.candidate.after
1382:         } else {
1383:             &request.candidate.before
1384:         };
1385:         assert_eq!(
1386:             std::fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1387:             source_file(expected, "Cargo.toml")
1388:                 .ok_or("Cargo.toml")?
1389:                 .bytes(),
1390:             "phase {phase:?}"
1391:         );
1392:     }
1393:     Ok(())
1394: }

1509: fn corrupt_store_is_quarantined_while_a_new_physical_workspace_and_store_continue()
1510: -> Result<(), String> {
1511:     let original = Fixture::new("remediation-original")?;
1512:     let other = Fixture::new("remediation-other")?;
1513:     let roots = [original.project.clone(), other.project.clone()];
1514:     let store =
1515:         NativeMutationStore::open(&original.state, &roots).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1516: 
1517:     let (_other_backend, other_lease, intact_request) = other.request(309)?;
1518:     let intact = store
1519:         .commit(&other_lease, &intact_request, &Continue)
1520:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1521:     assert_eq!(intact.state, MutationState::Committed);
1522:     let (_other_backend, other_lease, blocked_request) = other.request(310)?;
1523: 
1524:     let (_original_backend, original_lease, damaged_request) = original.request(311)?;
1525:     let _fault = inject_test_io_fault(
1526:         IoFaultPoint::JournalWrite(JournalPhase::Scratch),
1527:         TestIoFaultMode::ShortWriteThenNoSpace,
1528:     );
1529:     assert_eq!(
1530:         store.commit(&original_lease, &damaged_request, &Continue),
1531:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1532:     );
1533:     let reserved_temp = original.project.join(temp_name(&damaged_request.id, 0));
1534:     assert!(reserved_temp.exists());
1535: 
1536:     let original_project_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&original.project)?;
1537:     let other_project_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&other.project)?;
1538:     let original_state_snapshot = snapshot_tree(&original.state)?;
1539:     assert_eq!(store.list_records(), Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired));
1540:     assert_eq!(
1541:         store.prune_record(&damaged_request.id, &damaged_request.digest),
1542:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1543:     );
1544:     assert_eq!(
1545:         store.commit(&other_lease, &blocked_request, &Continue),
1546:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1547:     );
1548:     assert_eq!(
1549:         store.receipt(&original_lease, &damaged_request.id),
1550:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1551:     );
1552:     assert_eq!(
1553:         store.recover(&original_lease, &damaged_request.id),
1554:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1555:     );
1556:     assert_eq!(
1557:         store
1558:             .receipt(&other_lease, &intact_request.id)
1559:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1560:             .state,
1561:         MutationState::Committed
1562:     );
1563:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&original.project)?, original_project_snapshot);
1564:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&other.project)?, other_project_snapshot);
1565:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&original.state)?, original_state_snapshot);
1566: 
1567:     let replacement = Fixture::new("remediation-replacement")?;
1568:     write_reviewed_bundle(&replacement.project, &damaged_request.candidate.before)?;
1569:     assert_ne!(
1570:         std::fs::metadata(&replacement.project)
1571:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?

1574:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
1575:             .ino()
1576:     );
1577:     assert!(
1578:         !snapshot_tree(&replacement.project)?
1579:             .keys()
1580:             .any(|path| path.to_string_lossy().starts_with(".rust-mcp-mut-"))
1581:     );
1582:     let replacement_store = NativeMutationStore::open(
1583:         &replacement.state,
1584:         std::slice::from_ref(&replacement.project),
1585:     )
1586:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1587:     assert_eq!(
1588:         replacement_store.authorize(&original_lease),
1589:         Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied)
1590:     );
1591:     let (_replacement_backend, replacement_lease, replacement_request) =
1592:         replacement.request(312)?;
1593:     let replacement_receipt = replacement_store
1594:         .commit(&replacement_lease, &replacement_request, &Continue)
1595:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1596:     assert_eq!(replacement_receipt.state, MutationState::Committed);
1597:     assert_eq!(
1598:         replacement_receipt.files[0].effect_after,
1599:         Some(replacement_receipt.files[0].after.clone())
1600:     );
1601: 
1602:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&original.project)?, original_project_snapshot);
1603:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&other.project)?, other_project_snapshot);
1604:     assert_eq!(snapshot_tree(&original.state)?, original_state_snapshot);
1605:     assert!(reserved_temp.exists());

1609: #[test]
1610: fn newer_staged_record_left_by_persist_failure_is_recovered() -> Result<(), String> {
1611:     let fixture = Fixture::new("staged-persist-failure")?;
1612:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(130)?;
1613:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
1614:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1615:     let final_name = journal_name(&request.id);
1616:     let staging_name = format!(".{final_name}.staging");
1617:     assert_eq!(
1618:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1619:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::BeforeStagedPersist {
1620:                 let raw = std::fs::read(fixture.state.join(&final_name))
1621:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1622:                 let mut staged = decode(&raw)?;
1623:                 staged.sequence = staged
1624:                     .sequence
1625:                     .checked_add(1)
1626:                     .ok_or(MutationError::LimitExceeded)?;
1627:                 staged.phase = JournalPhase::Staged;
1628:                 std::fs::write(fixture.state.join(&staging_name), encode(&staged)?)
1629:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1630:                 std::fs::set_permissions(
1631:                     fixture.state.join(&staging_name),
1632:                     std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600),
1633:                 )
1634:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1635:                 return Err(MutationError::Io);
1636:             }
1637:             Ok(())
1638:         }),
1639:         Err(MutationError::Io)
1640:     );
1641:     assert!(!fixture.state.join(staging_name).exists());
1642:     assert!(!fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, 0)).exists());
1643:     assert_eq!(
1644:         store
1645:             .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
1646:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1647:             .state,
1648:         MutationState::Aborted
1649:     );
1650:     Ok(())
1651: }

1653: #[test]
1654: fn exact_query_and_recovery_ignore_unrelated_store_damage() -> Result<(), String> {
1655:     let fixture = Fixture::new("reachable-recovery")?;
1656:     let (backend, lease, request) = fixture.request(240)?;
1657:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open(&fixture.state, std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
1658:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1659:     assert_eq!(
1660:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &Continue, |phase| {
1661:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Published {
1662:                 Err(MutationError::Io)
1663:             } else {
1664:                 Ok(())
1665:             }
1666:         }),
1667:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1668:     );
1669:     let unrelated = fixture.state.join("unrelated-damage");
1670:     let file = std::fs::File::create(&unrelated).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
1671:     file.set_len(MAX_STORE_BYTES + 1)
1672:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
1673:     std::fs::set_permissions(&unrelated, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600))
1674:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
1675:     let pending = store
1676:         .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
1677:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1678:     assert_eq!(pending.state, MutationState::RecoveryRequired);
1679:     assert_eq!(pending.files[0].effect_after, None);
1680:     assert_eq!(pending.files[0].effect_after_bytes, None);
1681:     assert_eq!(
1682:         store
1683:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
1684:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1685:             .state,
1686:         MutationState::Committed
1687:     );
1688:     assert_eq!(
1689:         store
1690:             .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
1691:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1692:             .state,
1693:         MutationState::Committed
1694:     );
1695:     let current = backend
1696:         .source(&lease, &Continue)
1697:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1698:     let next = {
1699:         let candidate = MutationCandidate {
1700:             kind: MutationKind::ManifestPatch,
1701:             before: current.clone(),
1702:             after: current,
1703:             validation: "unit-checkpoint".to_owned(),
1704:         };
1705:         MutationCommit {
1706:             id: MutationId::new("mut_00000000000000000000000000000241".to_owned())
1707:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
1708:             digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
1709:             key: IdempotencyKey::new("checkpoint-241".to_owned())
1710:                 .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
1711:             candidate,
1712:         }
1713:     };
1714:     assert!(matches!(
1715:         store.commit(&lease, &next, &Continue),
1716:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired | MutationError::LimitExceeded)
1717:     ));
1718:     Ok(())
1719: }

1726:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;

1740:         fixture.state.join(&staging_name),

1764:         MutationState::Committed
1765:     );
1766:     assert!(!fixture.state.join(staging_name).exists());

1771:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;

1774:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;

1793: #[test]
1794: fn cancellation_during_final_logical_recapture_aborts_and_cleans_temps() -> Result<(), String> {
1795:     use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
1796: 
1797:     struct CancelWhenSet<'a>(&'a AtomicBool);
1798:     impl OperationControl for CancelWhenSet<'_> {
1799:         fn check(&self) -> Result<(), ProjectError> {
1800:             if self.0.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {
1801:                 Err(ProjectError::Cancelled)
1802:             } else {
1803:                 Ok(())
1804:             }
1805:         }
1806:     }
1807: 
1808:     let fixture = Fixture::new("format-cancel-final-capture")?;
1809:     let (_backend, lease, request) = fixture.format_request(220)?;
1810:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
1811:         &fixture.state,
1812:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
1813:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
1814:     )
1815:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
1816:     let cancelled = AtomicBool::new(false);
1817:     let control = CancelWhenSet(&cancelled);
1818:     assert_eq!(
1819:         store.commit_checked(&lease, &request, &control, |phase| {
1820:             if phase == CommitCheckpoint::Staged {
1821:                 cancelled.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);
1822:             }
1823:             Ok(())
1824:         }),
1825:         Err(MutationError::Cancelled)
1826:     );
1827:     assert_eq!(
1828:         store
1829:             .receipt(&lease, &request.id)
1830:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
1831:             .state,
1832:         MutationState::Aborted
1833:     );
1834:     for (index, _) in request.candidate.after.files().iter().enumerate().take(2) {
1835:         assert!(!fixture.project.join(temp_name(&request.id, index)).exists());
1836:     }
1837:     for path in ["src/lib.rs", "src/other.rs"] {
1838:         assert_eq!(
1839:             std::fs::read(fixture.project.join(path)).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?,
1840:             source_file(&request.candidate.before, path)
1841:                 .ok_or("before")?
1842:                 .bytes()
1843:         );
1844:     }
1845:     Ok(())
1846: }

1983:     );

1985:         operator.prune_record(&request.id, &request.digest),
1986:         Err(MutationError::RecoveryRequired)
1987:     );

1993:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;

2337: fn another_workspace_cannot_promote_a_staging_journal() -> Result<(), String> {
2338:     let first = Fixture::new("staging-authority-a")?;
2339:     let second = Fixture::new("staging-authority-b")?;
2340:     let (_first_backend, first_lease, _first_request) = first.request(230)?;
2341:     let (_second_backend, second_lease, second_request) = second.request(231)?;
2342:     let roots = [first.project.clone(), second.project.clone()];
2343:     let store =
2344:         NativeMutationStore::open(&first.state, &roots).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2345:     store
2346:         .commit(&second_lease, &second_request, &Continue)
2347:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2348:     let final_name = journal_name(&second_request.id);
2349:     let staging_name = format!(".{final_name}.staging");
2350:     std::fs::rename(
2351:         first.state.join(&final_name),
2352:         first.state.join(&staging_name),
2353:     )
2354:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2355: 
2356:     let snapshot = || -> Result<Vec<(String, Vec<u8>)>, String> {
2357:         let mut files = std::fs::read_dir(&first.state)
2358:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
2359:             .map(|entry| {
2360:                 let entry = entry.map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2361:                 let name = entry
2362:                     .file_name()
2363:                     .into_string()
2364:                     .map_err(|_| "utf8".to_owned())?;
2365:                 let bytes = std::fs::read(entry.path()).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2366:                 Ok((name, bytes))
2367:             })
2368:             .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, String>>()?;
2369:         files.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(&right.0));
2370:         Ok(files)
2371:     };
2372:     let before = snapshot()?;
2373:     assert_eq!(
2374:         store.receipt(&first_lease, &second_request.id),
2375:         Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied)
2376:     );
2377:     assert_eq!(snapshot()?, before);
2378:     assert!(first.state.join(staging_name).exists());
2379:     assert!(!first.state.join(final_name).exists());
2380:     Ok(())
2381: }

2428: fn canonical_bundle_order_handles_module_file_and_sibling_module_directory() -> Result<(), String> {
2429:     let fixture = Fixture::new("format-module-order")?;
2430:     std::fs::create_dir(fixture.project.join("src/parser")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2431:     std::fs::write(fixture.project.join("src/parser.rs"), b"pub mod ast;\n")
2432:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2433:     std::fs::write(
2434:         fixture.project.join("src/parser/ast.rs"),
2435:         b"pub fn ast( )->u8{1}\n",
2436:     )
2437:     .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2438:     let backend = SecureProjects::new(std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
2439:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2440:     let opened = backend
2441:         .open(fixture.project.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?, &Continue)
2442:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2443:     let before = backend
2444:         .source(&opened.lease, &Continue)
2445:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2446:     let module = before
2447:         .files()
2448:         .iter()
2449:         .position(|file| file.path() == "src/parser.rs")
2450:         .ok_or("module")?;
2451:     let child = before
2452:         .files()
2453:         .iter()
2454:         .position(|file| file.path() == "src/parser/ast.rs")
2455:         .ok_or("child")?;
2456:     assert!(module < child);
2457:     let after_files = before
2458:         .files()
2459:         .iter()
2460:         .map(|file| {
2461:             let bytes = match file.path() {
2462:                 "src/parser.rs" => b"pub mod ast;\n\n".to_vec(),
2463:                 "src/parser/ast.rs" => b"pub fn ast() -> u8 {\n    1\n}\n".to_vec(),
2464:                 _ => file.bytes().to_vec(),
2465:             };
2466:             SourceFile::new(file.path().to_owned(), bytes)
2467:         })
2468:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
2469:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2470:     let after = SourceBundle::with_directories(after_files, before.directories().to_vec())
2471:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2472:     let candidate = MutationCandidate {
2473:         kind: MutationKind::FormatApply,
2474:         before,
2475:         after,
2476:         validation: "module-order".to_owned(),
2477:     };
2478:     let request = MutationCommit {
2479:         id: MutationId::new("mut_00000000000000000000000000000225".to_owned())
2480:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2481:         digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2482:         key: IdempotencyKey::new("module-order".to_owned())
2483:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2484:         candidate,
2485:     };
2486:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
2487:         &fixture.state,
2488:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
2489:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
2490:     )
2491:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2492:     let receipt = store
2493:         .commit(&opened.lease, &request, &Continue)
2494:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2495:     assert_eq!(receipt.state, MutationState::Committed);
2496:     assert_eq!(receipt.files.len(), 2);
2497:     assert_eq!(receipt.files[0].path, "src/parser.rs");
2498:     assert_eq!(receipt.files[1].path, "src/parser/ast.rs");
2499:     Ok(())
2500: }

2503: fn protected_nested_swap_rejects_a_symlinked_parent_that_plain_swap_follows() -> Result<(), String>

2559: #[test]
2560: fn maximum_format_journal_cost_is_bounded_per_global_phase() -> Result<(), String> {
2561:     use std::time::Instant;
2562: 
2563:     const FILES: usize = 128;
2564:     const BYTES_PER_FILE: usize = (16 * 1024 * 1024) / FILES;
2565:     let mut before_files = Vec::with_capacity(FILES);
2566:     let mut after_files = Vec::with_capacity(FILES);
2567:     for index in 0..FILES {
2568:         let path = format!("f{index:03}.rs");
2569:         let before = vec![b'a'; BYTES_PER_FILE];
2570:         let mut after = before.clone();
2571:         after[0] = b'b';
2572:         before_files
2573:             .push(SourceFile::new(path.clone(), before).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?);
2574:         after_files.push(SourceFile::new(path, after).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?);
2575:     }
2576:     let before = SourceBundle::new(before_files).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2577:     let after = SourceBundle::new(after_files).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2578:     let candidate = MutationCandidate {
2579:         kind: MutationKind::FormatApply,
2580:         before: before.clone(),
2581:         after: after.clone(),
2582:         validation: "maximum-format-journal".to_owned(),
2583:     };
2584:     let id = MutationId::new("mut_ffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff".to_owned())
2585:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2586:     let digest = mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2587:     let files = before
2588:         .files()
2589:         .iter()
2590:         .enumerate()
2591:         .map(|(index, file)| JournalMutationFile {
2592:             path: file.path().to_owned(),
2593:             temp_path: temp_name(&id, index),
2594:             source_node: JournalNode {
2595:                 device: 1,
2596:                 inode: index as u64 + 1,
2597:             },
2598:             staged_node: Some(JournalNode {
2599:                 device: 1,
2600:                 inode: index as u64 + FILES as u64 + 1,
2601:             }),
2602:         })
2603:         .collect();
2604:     let mut body = JournalBody {
2605:         id: id.as_str().to_owned(),
2606:         digest: digest.as_str().to_owned(),
2607:         key: "maximum-format-journal".to_owned(),
2608:         operation: "format_apply".to_owned(),
2609:         workspace_path: "/private/tmp/rust-mcp-format-performance".to_owned(),
2610:         workspace_device: 1,
2611:         workspace_inode: 1,
2612:         files,
2613:         validation: candidate.validation,
2614:         sequence: 0,
2615:         phase: JournalPhase::Prepared,
2616:         before: JournalBundle::from(&before),
2617:         after: JournalBundle::from(&after),
2618:         legacy_v1: false,
2619:         format: JournalFormat::V2,
2620:     };
2621:     let worst_case_len = worst_case_record_len(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2622:     assert!(worst_case_len <= MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES);
2623:     let started = Instant::now();
2624:     let mut maximum_encoded = Vec::new();
2625:     let mut total_encoded = 0_u64;
2626:     for phase in [
2627:         JournalPhase::Prepared,
2628:         JournalPhase::Scratch,
2629:         JournalPhase::Staged,
2630:         JournalPhase::Applying,
2631:         JournalPhase::Published,
2632:         JournalPhase::Committed,
2633:     ] {
2634:         body.phase = phase;
2635:         body.sequence += 1;
2636:         let encoded = encode(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2637:         assert!(encoded.len() <= worst_case_len);
2638:         total_encoded += encoded.len() as u64;
2639:         if encoded.len() > maximum_encoded.len() {
2640:             maximum_encoded = encoded;
2641:         }
2642:     }
2643:     let encode_millis = started.elapsed().as_millis();
2644:     let decode_started = Instant::now();
2645:     let decoded = decode(&maximum_encoded).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2646:     let decode_millis = decode_started.elapsed().as_millis();
2647:     let empty_index = StoreIndex {
2648:         journal_count: 0,
2649:         bytes: 0,
2650:         bodies: Vec::new(),
2651:         summaries: Vec::new(),
2652:     };
2653:     ensure_new_record_quota(
2654:         &empty_index,
2655:         (worst_case_len as u64)
2656:             .checked_mul(2)
2657:             .ok_or("reservation overflow")?,
2658:         worst_case_len as u64,
2659:     )
2660:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2661:     assert_eq!(decoded.files.len(), FILES);
2662:     assert_eq!(
2663:         decoded
2664:             .before
2665:             .decode()
2666:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2667:         before
2668:     );
2669:     assert_eq!(
2670:         decoded
2671:             .after
2672:             .decode()
2673:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2674:         after
2675:     );
2676:     eprintln!(
2677:         "M2_02_JOURNAL_COST encoded_max={} worst_case={} encoded_six_phases={} encode_six_ms={} decode_one_ms={}",
2678:         maximum_encoded.len(),
2679:         worst_case_len,
2680:         total_encoded,
2681:         encode_millis,
2682:         decode_millis
2683:     );
2684:     Ok(())
2685: }

2687: #[test]
2688: fn heterogeneous_records_leave_one_maximum_recovery_copy_before_new_admission() {
2689:     let mebibyte = 1024_u64 * 1024;
2690:     assert_eq!(RETAINED_METADATA_GROWTH_BYTES, mebibyte);
2691:     assert_eq!(RECOVERY_STAGING_HEADROOM_BYTES, 48 * mebibyte);
2692:     let retained_records = [48, 47, 46, 39, 27].map(|value| value * mebibyte);
2693:     assert!(
2694:         retained_records
2695:             .iter()
2696:             .all(|bytes| *bytes <= MAX_JOURNAL_BYTES as u64)
2697:     );
2698:     let retained_total = retained_records.iter().sum::<u64>();
2699:     assert_eq!(retained_total, MAX_STORE_BYTES - RECOVERY_HEADROOM_BYTES);
2700: 
2701:     let index = StoreIndex {
2702:         journal_count: retained_records.len(),
2703:         bytes: retained_total,
2704:         bodies: Vec::new(),
2705:         summaries: Vec::new(),
2706:     };
2707:     let small_prepared_record = 1024_u64;
2708:     let small_worst_case = 2048_u64;
2709:     let old_transient_reservation = small_worst_case * 2;
2710:     assert!(retained_total + old_transient_reservation <= MAX_STORE_BYTES);
2711:     assert_eq!(
2712:         ensure_new_record_quota(&index, old_transient_reservation, small_worst_case),
2713:         Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded)
2714:     );
2715: 
2716:     let boundary = StoreIndex {
2717:         journal_count: index.journal_count,
2718:         bytes: retained_total - small_worst_case,
2719:         bodies: Vec::new(),
2720:         summaries: Vec::new(),
2721:     };
2722:     assert_eq!(
2723:         ensure_new_record_quota(&boundary, old_transient_reservation, small_worst_case),
2724:         Ok(())
2725:     );
2726:     assert_eq!(
2727:         boundary.bytes + small_worst_case + RECOVERY_HEADROOM_BYTES,
2728:         MAX_STORE_BYTES
2729:     );
2730:     assert!(small_prepared_record < small_worst_case);
2731: }
2732: 
2733: #[test]
2734: fn metadata_growth_reservation_covers_maximum_v2_and_legacy_conversion() -> Result<(), String> {
2735:     const CHANGED_FILES: usize = 128;
2736:     const PER_RECORD_GROWTH: usize = 8 * 1024;
2737:     let id = MutationId::new("mut_ffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff".to_owned())
2738:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2739:     let mut bundle_files = Vec::with_capacity(CHANGED_FILES);
2740:     let mut files = Vec::with_capacity(CHANGED_FILES);
2741:     for index in 0..CHANGED_FILES {
2742:         let path = format!("p{index:03}/{}", "x".repeat(95));
2743:         assert_eq!(path.len(), rust_engineering_domain::SOURCE_MAX_PATH_BYTES);
2744:         bundle_files.push(JournalFile {
2745:             path: path.clone(),
2746:             bytes: STANDARD.encode(b"x"),
2747:         });
2748:         files.push(JournalMutationFile {
2749:             path,
2750:             temp_path: temp_name(&id, index),
2751:             source_node: JournalNode {
2752:                 device: i32::MIN,
2753:                 inode: u64::MAX,
2754:             },
2755:             staged_node: None,
2756:         });
2757:     }
2758:     let bundle = JournalBundle {
2759:         directories: Vec::new(),
2760:         files: bundle_files,
2761:     };
2762:     let body = JournalBody {
2763:         id: id.as_str().to_owned(),
2764:         digest: format!("sha256:{}", "f".repeat(64)),
2765:         key: "k".repeat(64),
2766:         operation: "format_apply".to_owned(),
2767:         workspace_path: format!("/private/tmp/{}", "w".repeat(95)),
2768:         workspace_device: i32::MIN,
2769:         workspace_inode: u64::MAX,
2770:         files,
2771:         validation: "v".repeat(128),
2772:         sequence: 0,
2773:         phase: JournalPhase::Prepared,
2774:         before: bundle.clone(),
2775:         after: bundle,
2776:         legacy_v1: false,
2777:         format: JournalFormat::V2,
2778:     };
2779:     let current_v2 = encode(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?.len();
2780:     let worst_v2 = worst_case_record_len(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2781:     let v2_growth = worst_v2.checked_sub(current_v2).ok_or("v2 shrank")?;
2782:     assert!(v2_growth <= PER_RECORD_GROWTH, "v2 growth {v2_growth}");
2783: 
2784:     let mut legacy_body = body;
2785:     legacy_body.files.truncate(1);
2786:     legacy_body.operation = "manifest_patch".to_owned();
2787:     legacy_body.format = JournalFormat::V1;
2788:     legacy_body.legacy_v1 = true;
2789:     let legacy_current = encode_legacy_v1(&legacy_body)?.len();
2790:     let decoded_legacy =
2791:         decode_envelope(&encode_legacy_v1(&legacy_body)?).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2792:     let converted_worst =
2793:         worst_case_record_len(&decoded_legacy).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2794:     let legacy_growth = converted_worst.saturating_sub(legacy_current);
2795:     assert!(
2796:         legacy_growth <= PER_RECORD_GROWTH,
2797:         "legacy conversion growth {legacy_growth}"
2798:     );
2799:     eprintln!(
2800:         "M2_METADATA_GROWTH v2_bytes={v2_growth} legacy_v1_to_v2_bytes={legacy_growth} per_record_reservation={PER_RECORD_GROWTH}"
2801:     );
2802:     assert_eq!(
2803:         (PER_RECORD_GROWTH * MAX_JOURNALS) as u64,
2804:         RETAINED_METADATA_GROWTH_BYTES
2805:     );
2806:     Ok(())
2807: }
2808: 
2809: fn maximum_format_request(
2810:     label: &str,
2811:     suffix: u128,
2812: ) -> Result<(Fixture, SecureProjects, ProjectLease, MutationCommit), String> {
2813:     use rust_engineering_domain::SOURCE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES;
2814: 
2815:     const FILES: usize = 128;
2816:     let fixture = Fixture::new(label)?;
2817:     std::fs::remove_dir_all(fixture.project.join("src")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2818:     std::fs::create_dir(fixture.project.join("src")).map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2819:     let manifest_bytes = std::fs::metadata(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml"))
2820:         .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?
2821:         .len() as usize;
2822:     let rust_budget = SOURCE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES
2823:         .checked_sub(manifest_bytes)
2824:         .ok_or("manifest budget")?;
2825:     let base_size = rust_budget / FILES;
2826:     let remainder = rust_budget % FILES;
2827:     for index in 0..FILES {
2828:         let size = base_size + usize::from(index < remainder);
2829:         let name = if index == 0 {
2830:             "lib.rs".to_owned()
2831:         } else {
2832:             format!("f{index:03}.rs")
2833:         };
2834:         std::fs::write(fixture.project.join("src").join(name), vec![b'a'; size])
2835:             .map_err(|error| error.to_string())?;
2836:     }
2837:     let backend = SecureProjects::new(std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project))
2838:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2839:     let opened = backend
2840:         .open(fixture.project.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?, &Continue)
2841:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2842:     let before = backend
2843:         .source(&opened.lease, &Continue)
2844:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2845:     assert_eq!(
2846:         before
2847:             .files()
2848:             .iter()
2849:             .map(|file| file.bytes().len())
2850:             .sum::<usize>(),
2851:         SOURCE_MAX_TOTAL_BYTES
2852:     );
2853:     let after_files = before
2854:         .files()
2855:         .iter()
2856:         .map(|file| {
2857:             let mut bytes = file.bytes().to_vec();
2858:             if file.path().ends_with(".rs") {
2859:                 bytes[0] = b'b';
2860:             }
2861:             SourceFile::new(file.path().to_owned(), bytes)
2862:         })
2863:         .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()
2864:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2865:     let after = SourceBundle::with_directories(after_files, before.directories().to_vec())
2866:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2867:     let candidate = MutationCandidate {
2868:         kind: MutationKind::FormatApply,
2869:         before,
2870:         after,
2871:         validation: "real-format-ceiling".to_owned(),
2872:     };
2873:     let request = MutationCommit {
2874:         id: MutationId::new(format!("mut_{suffix:032x}")).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2875:         digest: mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2876:         key: IdempotencyKey::new("real-format-ceiling".to_owned())
2877:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2878:         candidate,
2879:     };
2880:     Ok((fixture, backend, opened.lease, request))
2881: }
2882: 
2883: #[test]
2884: #[ignore = "explicit release-profile APFS commit-only memory measurement"]
2885: fn measure_real_format_commit_only() -> Result<(), String> {
2886:     use std::time::Instant;
2887: 
2888:     let (fixture, _backend, lease, request) = maximum_format_request(
2889:         "format-real-commit-only",
2890:         0xdddddddddddddddddddddddddddddddd,
2891:     )?;
2892:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
2893:         &fixture.state,
2894:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
2895:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
2896:     )
2897:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2898:     let started = Instant::now();
2899:     let committed = store
2900:         .commit(&lease, &request, &Continue)
2901:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2902:     let commit_millis = started.elapsed().as_millis();
2903:     assert_eq!(committed.state, MutationState::Committed);
2904:     assert_eq!(committed.files.len(), 128);
2905:     eprintln!("M2_07_COMMIT_ONLY commit_ms={commit_millis}");
2906:     Ok(())
2907: }

2909: #[test]
2910: #[ignore = "explicit release-profile APFS ceiling measurement"]
2911: fn measure_real_format_commit_replay_recovery_and_index_ceiling() -> Result<(), String> {
2912:     use std::time::Instant;
2913: 
2914:     const FILES: usize = 128;
2915:     let (fixture, _backend, lease, request) =
2916:         maximum_format_request("format-real-ceiling", 0xeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeeee)?;
2917:     let store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
2918:         &fixture.state,
2919:         std::slice::from_ref(&fixture.project),
2920:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
2921:     )
2922:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2923:     let commit_started = Instant::now();
2924:     let committed = store
2925:         .commit(&lease, &request, &Continue)
2926:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2927:     let commit_millis = commit_started.elapsed().as_millis();
2928:     assert_eq!(committed.state, MutationState::Committed);
2929:     assert_eq!(committed.files.len(), FILES);
2930:     let replay_started = Instant::now();
2931:     assert_eq!(
2932:         store
2933:             .commit(&lease, &request, &Continue)
2934:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
2935:             .state,
2936:         MutationState::Committed
2937:     );
2938:     let replay_millis = replay_started.elapsed().as_millis();
2939:     let recovery_started = Instant::now();
2940:     assert_eq!(
2941:         store
2942:             .recover(&lease, &request.id)
2943:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
2944:             .state,
2945:         MutationState::Committed
2946:     );
2947:     let recovery_millis = recovery_started.elapsed().as_millis();
2948:     let stored_bytes = store.list_records().map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?[0].stored_bytes;
2949: 
2950:     let index_fixture = Fixture::new("format-index-ceiling")?;
2951:     let (index_backend, index_lease, new_request) = index_fixture.format_request(999)?;
2952:     let index_store = NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
2953:         &index_fixture.state,
2954:         std::slice::from_ref(&index_fixture.project),
2955:         MutationKind::FormatApply,
2956:     )
2957:     .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2958:     let index_before = index_backend
2959:         .source(&index_lease, &Continue)
2960:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2961:     let no_change = MutationCandidate {
2962:         kind: MutationKind::FormatApply,
2963:         before: index_before.clone(),
2964:         after: index_before,
2965:         validation: "index-ceiling".to_owned(),
2966:     };
2967:     let digest = mutation_digest(&no_change).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2968:     for index in 0..MAX_JOURNALS {
2969:         let id =
2970:             MutationId::new(format!("mut_{index:032x}")).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2971:         let body = JournalBody {
2972:             id: id.as_str().to_owned(),
2973:             digest: digest.as_str().to_owned(),
2974:             key: format!("index-ceiling-{index}"),
2975:             operation: "format_apply".to_owned(),
2976:             workspace_path: index_lease.path.to_str().ok_or("utf8")?.to_owned(),
2977:             workspace_device: index_lease.node.device,
2978:             workspace_inode: index_lease.node.inode,
2979:             files: Vec::new(),
2980:             validation: no_change.validation.clone(),
2981:             sequence: 0,
2982:             phase: JournalPhase::NoChange,
2983:             before: JournalBundle::from(&no_change.before),
2984:             after: JournalBundle::from(&no_change.after),
2985:             legacy_v1: false,
2986:             format: JournalFormat::V2,
2987:         };
2988:         index_store
2989:             .state
2990:             .write_new(
2991:                 &journal_name(&id),
2992:                 &encode(&body).map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?,
2993:                 body.phase,
2994:             )
2995:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
2996:     }
2997:     let list_started = Instant::now();
2998:     assert_eq!(
2999:         index_store
3000:             .list_records()
3001:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
3002:             .len(),
3003:         MAX_JOURNALS
3004:     );
3005:     let list_millis = list_started.elapsed().as_millis();
3006:     let exact_id = MutationId::new(format!("mut_{:032x}", MAX_JOURNALS / 2))
3007:         .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?;
3008:     let exact_started = Instant::now();
3009:     assert_eq!(
3010:         index_store
3011:             .receipt(&index_lease, &exact_id)
3012:             .map_err(|error| format!("{error:?}"))?
3013:             .state,
3014:         MutationState::NoChange
3015:     );
3016:     let exact_receipt_millis = exact_started.elapsed().as_millis();
3017:     let refusal_started = Instant::now();
3018:     assert_eq!(
3019:         index_store.commit(&index_lease, &new_request, &Continue),
3020:         Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded)
3021:     );
3022:     let quota_refusal_millis = refusal_started.elapsed().as_millis();
3023:     eprintln!(
3024:         "M2_02_REAL_COST commit_ms={commit_millis} replay_ms={replay_millis} recovery_ms={recovery_millis} stored_bytes={stored_bytes} index_list_ms={list_millis} exact_receipt_ms={exact_receipt_millis} quota_refusal_ms={quota_refusal_millis}"
3025:     );
3026:     Ok(())
3027: }

126: pub(super) trait MutationInput:
127:     serde::de::DeserializeOwned + JsonSchema + Send + 'static
128: {
129:     const NAME: &'static str;
130:     const DESCRIPTION: &'static str;
131:     const KIND: MutationKind;
132:     fn into_request(self) -> Result<Input, ErrorData>;
133: }
134: impl MutationInput for Input {
135:     const NAME: &'static str = NAME;
136:     const KIND: MutationKind = MutationKind::ManifestPatch;
137:     const DESCRIPTION: &'static str = "Preview, commit or inspect a journaled semantic root Cargo.toml edit. Host --allow-manifest-write is required. Preview validates the exact candidate in the approved offline Cargo runtime and returns its diff/digest. Commit requires that plan, current project authority and an idempotency key. Reopen the project after commit to read its receipt. Local coordinated writes do not exclude external editors or provide multi-file atomicity. Supports closed feature, built-in profile setting, workspace dependency and package/workspace rust/clippy lint set/remove. Dependency resolution requires host-approved offline vendor data and preserves whether Cargo.lock exists.";
138:     fn into_request(self) -> Result<Input, ErrorData> {
139:         Ok(self)
140:     }
141: }
142: impl MutationInput for FormatInput {
143:     const NAME: &'static str = FORMAT_NAME;
144:     const KIND: MutationKind = MutationKind::FormatApply;
145:     const DESCRIPTION: &'static str = "Preview, commit or inspect a journaled rustfmt operation on existing Rust source files. Host --allow-fmt-write is required. Preview runs rustfmt in the approved offline sandbox, verifies fmt.check against the complete exported candidate, and returns its exact diff/digest without source writes. Commit requires that unexpired plan, current project authority and an idempotency key. Reopen after commit for receipt or recovery. Local coordinated publication does not exclude external editors or provide multi-file atomicity.";
146:     fn into_request(self) -> Result<Input, ErrorData> {
147:         Ok(Input {
148:             project_ref: self.project_ref,
149:             action: match self.action {
150:                 FormatAction::Preview {
151:                     expected_project_fingerprint,
152:                 } => Action::FormatPreview {
153:                     expected_project_fingerprint,
154:                 },
155:                 FormatAction::Commit {
156:                     plan_id,
157:                     plan_digest,
158:                     idempotency_key,
159:                 } => Action::Commit {
160:                     plan_id,
161:                     plan_digest,
162:                     idempotency_key,
163:                 },
164:                 FormatAction::Receipt {
165:                     operation_id,
166:                     recover,
167:                 } => Action::Receipt {
168:                     operation_id,
169:                     recover,
170:                 },
171:             },
172:         })
173:     }
174: }

Project build scripts and proc macros may influence permitted source changes; review the exact diff. No edition migration, broken-code or arbitrary flags. Commit requires the unexpired plan, current project authority and idempotency key. Reopen for receipt/recovery. Local coordinated publication does not exclude external editors or provide multi-file atomicity.";

301: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
302: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
303: struct ResolutionView {
304:     resolution_execution_fingerprint: String,
305:     dataset_fingerprint: String,
306:     resolved_lock_fingerprint: String,
307:     lock_policy: LockPolicy,
308:     lock_disposition: LockDisposition,
309:     manifest_path: String,
310: }
311: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
312: #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
313: enum LockPolicy {
314:     PreservePresence,
315: }
316: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
317: #[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
318: enum LockDisposition {
319:     UpdatedExisting,
320:     TransientUnpublished,
321: }
322: 
323: fn read_validation_frame(encoded: &mut &str) -> Result<String, MutationError> {
324:     let (length, rest) = encoded.split_once(':').ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
325:     if length.len() > 5 || !length.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
326:         return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
327:     }
328:     let length = length
329:         .parse::<usize>()
330:         .map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
331:     if length > 1024 {
332:         return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
333:     }
334:     let field = rest.get(..length).ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?.to_owned();
335:     *encoded = rest.get(length..).ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
336:     Ok(field)
337: }
338: 
339: fn validation_view(mut encoded: &str) -> Result<ValidationView, MutationError> {
340:     let mut fields = Vec::new();
341:     for _ in 0..9 {
342:         let (size, rest) = encoded.split_once(':').ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
343:         if size.len() > 5 || !size.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
344:             return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
345:         }
346:         let size = size.parse::<usize>().map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
347:         if size > 1024 {
348:             return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
349:         }
350:         fields.push(rest.get(..size).ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?.to_owned());
351:         encoded = rest.get(size..).ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
352:     }
353:     let [
354:         version,
355:         mode,
356:         platform,
357:         image_id,
358:         configuration_fingerprint,
359:         execution_fingerprint,
360:         rust_version,
361:         cargo_version,
362:         candidate_source_fingerprint,
363:     ]: [String; 9] = fields.try_into().map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
364:     let mut resolution = None;
365:     let (method, mutation_execution_fingerprint) = match version.as_str() {
366:         "m2-manifest-lints-v1" | "m2-manifest-semantic-v1" if encoded.is_empty() => {
367:             (ValidationMethod::CargoMetadataFrozenNoDeps, None)
368:         }
369:         "m2-fmt-apply-v1" | "m2-fix-apply-v1" => {
370:             let (size, value) = encoded.split_once(':').ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
371:             if size != "71" || value.len() != 71 {
372:                 return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
373:             }
374:             value
375:                 .parse::<SourceFingerprint>()
376:                 .map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
377:             (
378:                 if version == "m2-fix-apply-v1" {
379:                     ValidationMethod::CargoFixThenCheck
380:                 } else {
381:                     ValidationMethod::RustfmtThenFmtCheck
382:                 },
383:                 Some(value.to_owned()),
384:             )
385:         }
386:         "m2-manifest-resolved-v1" | "m2-dependency-add-v1" | "m2-dependency-remove-v1" => {
387:             let resolution_execution_fingerprint = read_validation_frame(&mut encoded)?;
388:             let dataset_fingerprint = read_validation_frame(&mut encoded)?;
389:             let resolved_lock_fingerprint = read_validation_frame(&mut encoded)?;
390:             for fingerprint in [
391:                 &resolution_execution_fingerprint,
392:                 &dataset_fingerprint,
393:                 &resolved_lock_fingerprint,
394:             ] {
395:                 fingerprint
396:                     .parse::<SourceFingerprint>()
397:                     .map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
398:             }
399:             let lock_disposition = match read_validation_frame(&mut encoded)?.as_str() {
400:                 "updated_existing" => LockDisposition::UpdatedExisting,
401:                 "transient_unpublished" => LockDisposition::TransientUnpublished,
402:                 _ => return Err(MutationError::Invalid),
403:             };
404:             let manifest_path = read_validation_frame(&mut encoded)?;
405:             if !encoded.is_empty()
406:                 || rust_engineering_domain::validate_source_path(&manifest_path).is_err()
407:                 || !(manifest_path == "Cargo.toml" || manifest_path.ends_with("/Cargo.toml"))
408:             {
409:                 return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
410:             }
411:             resolution = Some(ResolutionView {
412:                 resolution_execution_fingerprint,
413:                 dataset_fingerprint,
414:                 resolved_lock_fingerprint,
415:                 lock_policy: LockPolicy::PreservePresence,
416:                 lock_disposition,
417:                 manifest_path,
418:             });
419:             (ValidationMethod::CargoMetadataOfflineThenFrozen, None)
420:         }
421:         _ => return Err(MutationError::Invalid),
422:     };
423:     if mode != "local_coordinated" {
424:         return Err(MutationError::Invalid);
425:     }
426:     for fingerprint in [
427:         &image_id,
428:         &configuration_fingerprint,
429:         &execution_fingerprint,
430:         &candidate_source_fingerprint,
431:     ] {
432:         fingerprint
433:             .parse::<SourceFingerprint>()
434:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
435:     }
436:     Ok(ValidationView {
437:         resolution,
438:         method,
439:         mutation_execution_fingerprint,
440:         semantics: SnapshotSemantics::LatestKnown,
441:         platform,
442:         image_id,
443:         configuration_fingerprint,
444:         execution_fingerprint,
445:         rust_version,
446:         cargo_version,
447:         candidate_source_fingerprint,
448:     })
449: }

451: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
452: #[serde(tag = "kind", rename_all = "snake_case", deny_unknown_fields)]
453: enum Data {

456:         plan_digest: String,

458:         files: Vec<Change>,
459:         diff: String,
460:         validation: ValidationView,
461:     },
462:     Receipt {
463:         operation_id: String,
464:         plan_digest: String,

466:         validation: ValidationView,
467:         files: Vec<ReceiptChange>,
468:     },
469: }

479: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
480: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
481: struct Output {
482:     status: Status,
483:     error_code: Option<Reason>,
484:     error_message: Option<&'static str>,
485:     summary: &'static str,
486:     duration_ms: u64,
487:     data: Option<Data>,
488:     diagnostics: [(); 0],
489:     truncation: Truncation,
490:     evidence: MutationEvidence,
491:     concurrency_contract: Concurrency,
492:     guarantees_not_provided: [ExcludedGuarantee; 4],
493: }
494: impl ToolOutput for Output {
495:     fn status(&self) -> ToolStatus {
496:         match self.status {
497:             Status::Passed => ToolStatus::Passed,
498:             Status::Failed => ToolStatus::Failed,
499:             Status::Blocked => ToolStatus::Blocked,
500:             Status::Unavailable => ToolStatus::Unavailable,
501:             Status::Cancelled => ToolStatus::Cancelled,
502:         }
503:     }
504: }
505: impl Output {
506:     fn new(
507:         status: Status,
508:         reason: Option<Reason>,
509:         data: Option<Data>,
510:         summary: &'static str,
511:         duration_ms: u64,
512:     ) -> Self {
513:         let evidence = match &data {
514:             Some(Data::Preview { plan_digest, .. } | Data::Receipt { plan_digest, .. }) => {
515:                 MutationEvidence::MutationSnapshot {
516:                     plan_digest: plan_digest.clone(),
517:                     semantics: SnapshotSemantics::LatestKnown,
518:                     freshness: Freshness::Unknown,
519:                 }
520:             }
521:             None => MutationEvidence::Local,
522:         };
523:         Self {
524:             status,
525:             error_code: reason,
526:             error_message: reason.map(|_| summary),
527:             summary,
528:             duration_ms,
529:             data,
530:             diagnostics: [],
531:             truncation: Truncation::default(),
532:             evidence,
533:             concurrency_contract: Concurrency::LocalCoordinated,
534:             guarantees_not_provided: [
535:                 ExcludedGuarantee::OsExclusionOfExternalWriters,
536:                 ExcludedGuarantee::MultiFileAtomicity,
537:                 ExcludedGuarantee::MaliciousHostProtection,
538:                 ExcludedGuarantee::DemonstratedPowerLossSurvival,
539:             ],
540:         }
541:     }
542:     fn failure(reason: Reason, duration: u64) -> Self {
543:         let status = match reason {
544:             Reason::UnsupportedPlatform
545:             | Reason::ToolchainUnavailable
546:             | Reason::OfflineDataMissing => Status::Unavailable,
547:             Reason::CandidateInvalid | Reason::CommandTimeout => Status::Failed,
548:             Reason::Cancelled => Status::Cancelled,
549:             _ => Status::Blocked,
550:         };
551:         Self::new(
552:             status,
553:             Some(reason),
554:             None,
555:             match reason {
556:                 Reason::InvalidOperation => {
557:                     "Operation is outside this tool's supported mutation contract"
558:                 }
559:                 Reason::PermissionDenied => {
560:                     "A current project reference and exact host write grant for this operation are required"
561:                 }
562:                 Reason::Conflict => {
563:                     "Source or approval changed; reopen the project and request a new preview"
564:                 }
565:                 Reason::LockBusy => "Another operation is active; retry when it finishes",
566:                 Reason::PlanExpired => "Preview expired; request a new preview and review its diff",
567:                 Reason::NotFound => {
568:                     "Plan or operation was not found; a restarted session requires receipt lookup for persisted operations"
569:                 }
570:                 Reason::LimitExceeded => {
571:                     "Mutation exceeds a bounded plan, output or journal budget"
572:                 }
573:                 Reason::UnsupportedPlatform => {
574:                     "This writer requires the qualified macOS ARM64 APFS adapter"
575:                 }
576:                 Reason::Io => {
577:                     "Mutation I/O failed; consult the original operation receipt before retrying a commit"
578:                 }
579:                 Reason::RecoveryRequired => {
580:                     "Preserve journal and mutation temporaries; reopen and request receipt with recover=true"
581:                 }
582:                 Reason::ToolchainUnavailable => {
583:                     "Configure the approved offline Cargo runtime before preview"
584:                 }
585:                 Reason::CandidateInvalid => {
586:                     "The isolated tool or postcondition check rejected the candidate; source was not changed"
587:                 }
588:                 Reason::Cancelled => "Operation cancelled before a successful receipt was returned",
589:                 Reason::OfflineDataMissing => {
590:                     "Configure or update a host-approved Cargo vendor dataset for offline dependency resolution"
591:                 }
592:                 Reason::OfflineDataInvalid => {
593:                     "The configured Cargo vendor data could not be verified against the host fingerprint"
594:                 }
595:                 Reason::CommandTimeout => {
596:                     "The isolated operation exceeded its time budget; no candidate was approved"
597:                 }
598:             },
599:             duration,
600:         )
601:     }
602: }
603: 
604: struct Provider {
605:     config: Option<WriteConfig>,
606:     kind: MutationKind,
607:     store: Option<NativeMutationStore>,
608: }
609: impl Provider {
610:     fn store(&mut self) -> Result<(), MutationError> {
611:         let config = self
612:             .config
613:             .as_ref()
614:             .ok_or(MutationError::PermissionDenied)?;
615:         if self.store.is_none() {
616:             let path = prepare_mutation_state(&config.state_parent)?;
617:             self.store = Some(NativeMutationStore::open_for_kind(
618:                 &path,
619:                 &config.roots,
620:                 self.kind,
621:             )?);
622:         }
623:         Ok(())
624:     }
625: }
