627: #[derive(Default)]
628: pub(super) struct SharedPlans {
629:     plans: MutationPlans,
630:     clock: MonotonicClock,
631: }
632: 
633: #[derive(Clone)]
634: struct AuditRecord {
635:     admitted: bool,
636:     cleanup_uncertain: bool,
637:     output: Output,
638:     allocation: Option<MutationAllocationStats>,
639: }
640: 
641: struct CallAuditState {
642:     tool: &'static str,
643:     phase: audit::Phase,
644:     dispatcher: tracing::Dispatch,
645:     waiter_dropped: AtomicBool,
646:     emitted: AtomicBool,
647:     worker_record: Mutex<Option<AuditRecord>>,
648: }
649: 
650: impl CallAuditState {
651:     fn new(tool: &'static str, phase: audit::Phase) -> Self {
652:         Self {
653:             tool,
654:             phase,
655:             dispatcher: tracing::dispatcher::get_default(Clone::clone),
656:             waiter_dropped: AtomicBool::new(false),
657:             emitted: AtomicBool::new(false),
658:             worker_record: Mutex::new(None),
659:         }
660:     }
661: 
662:     fn worker_completed(&self, record: AuditRecord) {
663:         let record_for_fallback = Self::response_lost(record.clone());
664:         match self.worker_record.lock() {
665:             Ok(mut slot) => *slot = Some(record),
666:             Err(poisoned) => *poisoned.into_inner() = Some(record),
667:         }
668:         if self.waiter_dropped.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
669:             self.emit_once(&record_for_fallback);
670:         }
671:     }
672: 
673:     fn waiter_completed(&self, record: AuditRecord) {
674:         self.emit_once(&record);
675:     }
676: 
677:     fn worker_cleanup_uncertain(&self) -> bool {
678:         match self.worker_record.lock() {
679:             Ok(slot) => slot.as_ref().is_some_and(|record| record.cleanup_uncertain),
680:             Err(poisoned) => poisoned
681:                 .into_inner()
682:                 .as_ref()
683:                 .is_some_and(|record| record.cleanup_uncertain),
684:         }
685:     }
686: 
687:     fn waiter_dropped(&self) {
688:         self.waiter_dropped.store(true, Ordering::Release);
689:         let record = match self.worker_record.lock() {
690:             Ok(slot) => slot.clone(),
691:             Err(poisoned) => poisoned.into_inner().clone(),
692:         };
693:         if let Some(record) = record {
694:             self.emit_once(&Self::response_lost(record));
695:         }
696:     }
697: 
698:     fn response_lost(mut record: AuditRecord) -> AuditRecord {
699:         if matches!(record.output.data, Some(Data::Preview { .. })) {
700:             record.output = Output::failure(Reason::Cancelled, record.output.duration_ms);
701:         }
702:         record
703:     }
704: 
705:     fn emit_once(&self, record: &AuditRecord) {
706:         if self
707:             .emitted
708:             .compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::AcqRel, Ordering::Acquire)
709:             .is_err()
710:         {
711:             return;
712:         }
713:         tracing::dispatcher::with_default(&self.dispatcher, || {
714:             audit::emit(
715:                 self.tool,
716:                 self.phase,
717:                 record.admitted,
718:                 record.cleanup_uncertain,
719:                 &record.output,
720:                 record.allocation,
721:             );
722:         });
723:     }
724: }
725: 
726: struct CallAuditWaiter(Arc<CallAuditState>);
727: 
728: impl Drop for CallAuditWaiter {
729:     fn drop(&mut self) {
730:         self.0.waiter_dropped();
731:     }
732: }
733: 
734: pub(super) type ManifestMutationTool = MutationTool<Input>;
735: pub(super) type FormatMutationTool = MutationTool<FormatInput>;
736: pub(super) type FixMutationTool = MutationTool<FixInput>;
737: pub(super) type DependencyAddTool = MutationTool<DependencyAddInput>;
738: pub(super) type DependencyRemoveTool = MutationTool<DependencyRemoveInput>;
739: 
740: pub(super) struct MutationTool<I> {
741:     pub definition: Tool,
742:     contract: Contract<I, Output>,
743:     registry: Arc<Mutex<Registry>>,
744:     provider: Arc<Mutex<Provider>>,
745:     workers: Workers,
746:     inspector: Arc<RustProjectInspector>,
747:     ready: Arc<AtomicBool>,
748:     plans: Arc<Mutex<SharedPlans>>,
749: }
750: impl<I: MutationInput> MutationTool<I> {
751:     pub fn new(
752:         registry: Arc<Mutex<Registry>>,
753:         workers: Workers,
754:         inspector: Arc<RustProjectInspector>,
755:         ready: Arc<AtomicBool>,
756:         config: Option<WriteConfig>,
757:         plans: Arc<Mutex<SharedPlans>>,
758:     ) -> Result<Self, ErrorData> {
759:         let contract = Contract::<I, Output>::new()?;
760:         let definition = Tool::new(I::NAME, I::DESCRIPTION, (*contract.input_schema).clone())
761:             .with_raw_output_schema(Arc::clone(&contract.output_schema))
762:             .with_annotations(
763:                 ToolAnnotations::new()
764:                     .read_only(false)
765:                     .destructive(true)
766:                     .idempotent(false)
767:                     .open_world(false),
768:             );
769:         Ok(Self {
770:             definition,
771:             contract,
772:             registry,
773:             workers,
774:             inspector,
775:             ready,
776:             plans,
777:             provider: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Provider {
778:                 config,
779:                 kind: I::KIND,
780:                 store: None,
781:             })),
782:         })
783:     }
784: 
785:     pub async fn call(
786:         &self,
787:         request: CallToolRequestParams,
788:         context: RequestContext<RoleServer>,
789:     ) -> Result<CallToolResult, ErrorData> {
790:         let input = self.contract.decode(request.arguments)?.into_request()?;
791:         let started = Instant::now();
792:         let phase = audit::phase(&input.action);
793:         if !self.ready.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
794:             let output = Output::failure(Reason::PermissionDenied, 0);
795:             audit::emit(
796:                 I::NAME,
797:                 phase,
798:                 false,
799:                 false,
800:                 &output,
801:                 allocation_stats(&self.plans),
802:             );
803:             return self.contract.encode(output);
804:         }
805:         let registry = Arc::clone(&self.registry);
806:         let provider = Arc::clone(&self.provider);
807:         let inspector = Arc::clone(&self.inspector);
808:         let plans = Arc::clone(&self.plans);
809:         let worker_plans = Arc::clone(&plans);
810:         let audit_state = Arc::new(CallAuditState::new(I::NAME, phase));
811:         let _audit_waiter = CallAuditWaiter(Arc::clone(&audit_state));
812:         let worker_audit = Arc::clone(&audit_state);
813:         let retention = PreviewRetention::default();
814:         let preview_token = retention.token();
815:         let preview_contract = Contract::<I, Output>::new()?;
816:         let result = self
817:             .workers
818:             .run_joined(context.ct, started + DEADLINE, move |control| {
819:                 let cleanup_uncertain = AtomicBool::new(false);
820:                 let result = (|| {
821:                     let mut provider = provider.try_lock().map_err(lock_reason)?;
822:                     provider.store().map_err(reason)?;
823:                     let vendor_config = provider
824:                         .config
825:                         .as_ref()
826:                         .and_then(|config| config.vendor.clone());
827:                     let Provider { store, .. } = &mut *provider;
828:                     let store = store.as_ref().ok_or(Reason::PermissionDenied)?;
829:                     match input.action {
830:                         action @ (Action::Preview { .. }
831:                         | Action::FormatPreview { .. }
832:                         | Action::SemanticPreview { .. }) => {
833:                             let (workspace, candidate) = match action {
834:                                 action @ (Action::Preview { .. }
835:                                 | Action::SemanticPreview { .. }) => {
836:                                     let (expected_project_fingerprint, target_manifest, edit) =
837:                                         match action {
838:                                             Action::Preview {
839:                                                 expected_project_fingerprint,
840:                                                 edit,
841:                                             } => (
842:                                                 expected_project_fingerprint,
843:                                                 "Cargo.toml".to_owned(),
844:                                                 edit.into_domain().map_err(reason)?,
845:                                             ),
846:                                             Action::SemanticPreview {
847:                                                 expected_project_fingerprint,
848:                                                 target_manifest,
849:                                                 edit,
850:                                             } => (
851:                                                 expected_project_fingerprint,
852:                                                 target_manifest,
853:                                                 edit,
854:                                             ),
855:                                             _ => return Err(Reason::InvalidOperation),
856:                                         };
857:                                     let prepared = registry
858:                                         .try_lock()
859:                                         .map_err(lock_reason)?
860:                                         .prepare_semantic(
861:                                             &input.project_ref,
862:                                             &expected_project_fingerprint,
863:                                             &target_manifest,
864:                                             I::KIND,
865:                                             &edit,
866:                                             store,
867:                                             control,
868:                                         )
869:                                         .map_err(|error| {
870:                                             observed_semantic_reason(error, &cleanup_uncertain)
871:                                         })?;
872:                                     let dataset = if matches!(
873:                                         edit,
874:                                         ManifestEdit::LintSet { .. }
875:                                             | ManifestEdit::LintRemove { .. }
876:                                             | ManifestEdit::ProfileSet { .. }
877:                                             | ManifestEdit::ProfileRemove { .. }
878:                                     ) {
879:                                         None
880:                                     } else {
881:                                         vendor_config
882:                                             .as_ref()
883:                                             .map(|config| {
884:                                                 rust_engineering_project::capture_with_expected(
885:                                                     &config.directory,
886:                                                     &config.fingerprint,
887:                                                     control,
888:                                                 )
889:                                             })
890:                                             .transpose()
891:                                             .map_err(|error| match error {
892:                                                 ProjectError::Cancelled => Reason::Cancelled,
893:                                                 ProjectError::Internal => Reason::Io,
894:                                                 _ => Reason::OfflineDataInvalid,
895:                                             })?
896:                                     };
897:                                     prepared
898:                                         .validate(
899:                                             &TomlManifestEditor,
900:                                             inspector.as_ref(),
901:                                             inspector.as_ref(),
902:                                             dataset.as_ref(),
903:                                             control,
904:                                         )
905:                                         .map_err(|error| {
906:                                             observed_semantic_reason(error, &cleanup_uncertain)
907:                                         })?
908:                                 }
909:                                 Action::FormatPreview {
910:                                     expected_project_fingerprint,
911:                                 } => {
912:                                     let prepared = registry
913:                                         .try_lock()
914:                                         .map_err(lock_reason)?
915:                                         .prepare_format(
916:                                             &input.project_ref,
917:                                             &expected_project_fingerprint,
918:                                             store,
919:                                             control,
920:                                         )
921:                                         .map_err(|error| {
922:                                             observed_preparation_reason(error, &cleanup_uncertain)
923:                                         })?;
924:                                     let command = match I::KIND {
925:                                         MutationKind::FormatApply => {
926:                                             rust_engineering_domain::RustMutationCommand::Format
927:                                         }
928:                                         MutationKind::FixApply => {
929:                                             rust_engineering_domain::RustMutationCommand::Fix
930:                                         }
931:                                         _ => return Err(Reason::InvalidOperation),
932:                                     };
933:                                     prepared
934:                                         .validate_command(command, inspector.as_ref(), control)
935:                                         .map_err(|error| {
936:                                             observed_preparation_reason(error, &cleanup_uncertain)
937:                                         })?
938:                                 }
939:                                 _ => return Err(Reason::InvalidOperation),
940:                             };
941:                             registry
942:                                 .try_lock()
943:                                 .map_err(lock_reason)?
944:                                 .finish_manifest_preview(&input.project_ref, &candidate, control)
945:                                 .map_err(|error| {
946:                                     observed_preparation_reason(error, &cleanup_uncertain)
947:                                 })?;
948:                             let (files, diff) = preview_diff(&candidate).map_err(reason)?;
949:                             let digest = mutation_digest(&candidate).map_err(reason)?;
950:                             let reference = OsReferences.generate().map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?;
951:                             let suffix =
952:                                 reference.as_str().strip_prefix("prj_").ok_or(Reason::Io)?;
953:                             let id = MutationId::new(format!("mut_{suffix}")).map_err(reason)?;
954:                             let data = Data::Preview {
955:                                 plan_id: id.as_str().into(),
956:                                 plan_digest: digest.to_string(),
957:                                 expires_in_seconds: MutationPlans::TTL_SECONDS,
958:                                 files,
959:                                 diff,
960:                                 validation: validation_view(&candidate.validation)
961:                                     .map_err(reason)?,
962:                             };
963:                             // Bound the complete MCP encoding, including duplicated text and JSON,
964:                             // before retaining a plan whose exact diff the peer cannot receive.
965:                             validate_preview_size(&preview_contract, &data)?;
966:                             let mut shared = worker_plans.try_lock().map_err(lock_reason)?;
967:                             let SharedPlans { plans, clock } = &mut *shared;
968:                             plans
969:                                 .remember_revocable(
970:                                     id,
971:                                     digest,
972:                                     workspace,
973:                                     candidate,
974:                                     clock,
975:                                     preview_token,
976:                                 )
977:                                 .map_err(reason)?;
978:                             Ok(data)
979:                         }
980:                         Action::Commit {
981:                             plan_id,
982:                             plan_digest,
983:                             idempotency_key,
984:                         } => {
985:                             let id = MutationId::new(plan_id).map_err(reason)?;
986:                             let key = IdempotencyKey::new(idempotency_key).map_err(reason)?;
987:                             let shared = worker_plans.try_lock().map_err(lock_reason)?;
988:                             let resolved =
989:                                 shared
990:                                     .plans
991:                                     .resolve(&id, &plan_digest, key.clone(), &shared.clock);
992:                             drop(shared);
993:                             let receipt = match resolved {
994:                                 Ok(plan) => {
995:                                     if plan.request.candidate.kind != I::KIND {
996:                                         return Err(Reason::PermissionDenied);
997:                                     }
998:                                     let receipt = registry
999:                                         .try_lock()
1000:                                         .map_err(lock_reason)?
1001:                                         .commit_mutation(
1002:                                             &input.project_ref,
1003:                                             &plan.workspace_root,
1004:                                             &plan.request,
1005:                                             store,
1006:                                             control,
1007:                                         )
1008:                                         .map_err(reason)?;
1009:                                     plan.retire_if_terminal(&receipt);
1010:                                     receipt
1011:                                 }
1012:                                 Err(
1013:                                     missing @ (MutationError::NotFound | MutationError::Expired),
1014:                                 ) => registry
1015:                                     .try_lock()
1016:                                     .map_err(lock_reason)?
1017:                                     .replay_mutation(
1018:                                         &input.project_ref,
1019:                                         &id,
1020:                                         &plan_digest,
1021:                                         &key,
1022:                                         store,
1023:                                         control,
1024:                                     )
1025:                                     .map_err(|error| {
1026:                                         reason(if error == MutationError::NotFound {
1027:                                             missing
1028:                                         } else {
1029:                                             error
1030:                                         })
1031:                                     })?,
1032:                                 Err(error) => return Err(reason(error)),
1033:                             };
1034:                             receipt_data(receipt).map_err(reason)
1035:                         }
1036:                         Action::Receipt {
1037:                             operation_id,
1038:                             recover,
1039:                         } => {
1040:                             let id = MutationId::new(operation_id).map_err(reason)?;
1041:                             let receipt = registry
1042:                                 .try_lock()
1043:                                 .map_err(lock_reason)?
1044:                                 .mutation_receipt(&input.project_ref, &id, recover, store, control)
1045:                                 .map_err(reason)?;
1046:                             receipt_data(receipt).map_err(reason)
1047:                         }
1048:                     }
1049:                 })();
1050:                 let duration = elapsed_millis(started);
1051:                 let output = joined_output(
1052:                     result.clone(),
1053:                     rust_engineering_application::OperationControl::check(control).is_err(),
1054:                     duration,
1055:                 );
1056:                 let admitted = !matches!(output.error_code, Some(Reason::PermissionDenied));
1057:                 worker_audit.worker_completed(AuditRecord {
1058:                     admitted,
1059:                     cleanup_uncertain: cleanup_uncertain.load(Ordering::Acquire),
1060:                     output,
1061:                     allocation: allocation_stats(&worker_plans),
1062:                 });
1063:                 result
1064:             })
1065:             .await;
1066:         let duration = elapsed_millis(started);
1067:         let entered_worker = result.is_ok();
1068:         let output = match result {
1069:             Ok(joined) => joined_output(joined.result, joined.interrupted.is_some(), duration),
1070:             Err(error) => Output::failure(
1071:                 match error {
1072:                     WorkerError::Busy => Reason::LockBusy,
1073:                     WorkerError::Cancelled => Reason::Cancelled,
1074:                     WorkerError::TimedOut => Reason::CommandTimeout,
1075:                     WorkerError::Internal => Reason::Io,
1076:                 },
1077:                 duration,
1078:             ),
1079:         };
1080:         let retain_preview = matches!(&output.data, Some(Data::Preview { .. }));
1081:         let encoded = self.contract.encode(output.clone())?;
1082:         if serde_json::to_vec(&encoded)
1083:             .map_err(|_| ErrorData::internal_error("Mutation encoding failed", None))?
1084:             .len()
1085:             > MAX_RESULT
1086:         {
1087:             let output = Output::failure(Reason::LimitExceeded, duration);
1088:             audit_state.waiter_completed(AuditRecord {
1089:                 admitted: entered_worker,
1090:                 cleanup_uncertain: audit_state.worker_cleanup_uncertain(),
1091:                 output: output.clone(),
1092:                 allocation: allocation_stats(&plans),
1093:             });
1094:             return self.contract.encode(output);
1095:         }
1096:         if retain_preview {
1097:             retention.retain();
1098:         }
1099:         let admitted =
1100:             entered_worker && !matches!(output.error_code, Some(Reason::PermissionDenied));
1101:         audit_state.waiter_completed(AuditRecord {
1102:             admitted,
1103:             cleanup_uncertain: audit_state.worker_cleanup_uncertain(),
1104:             output,
1105:             allocation: allocation_stats(&plans),
1106:         });
1107:         Ok(encoded)
1108:     }
1109: }
1110: 
1111: fn elapsed_millis(started: Instant) -> u64 {
1112:     started.elapsed().as_millis().try_into().unwrap_or(u64::MAX)
1113: }
1114: 
1115: fn allocation_stats(plans: &Arc<Mutex<SharedPlans>>) -> Option<MutationAllocationStats> {
1116:     plans
1117:         .try_lock()
1118:         .ok()
1119:         .map(|shared| shared.plans.allocation_stats())
1120: }
1121: 
1122: fn joined_output(result: Result<Data, Reason>, interrupted: bool, duration: u64) -> Output {
1123:     // Native commit finishes its journal after its irreversible point. Do not
1124:     // relabel a durable receipt as cancelled after losing the response race.
1125:     match result {
1126:         Ok(
1127:             data @ Data::Receipt {
1128:                 state: ReceiptState::RecoveryRequired,
1129:                 ..
1130:             },
1131:         ) => Output::new(
1132:             Status::Blocked,
1133:             Some(Reason::RecoveryRequired),
1134:             Some(data),
1135:             "Publication needs recovery; preserve the journal and avoid concurrent edits",
1136:             duration,
1137:         ),
1138:         Ok(
1139:             data @ Data::Receipt {
1140:                 state: ReceiptState::Aborted,
1141:                 ..
1142:             },
1143:         ) => Output::new(
1144:             Status::Blocked,
1145:             Some(Reason::Conflict),
1146:             Some(data),
1147:             "Operation aborted without applying the candidate; request a new preview",
1148:             duration,
1149:         ),
1150:         Ok(data @ Data::Receipt { .. }) => Output::new(
1151:             Status::Passed,
1152:             None,
1153:             Some(data),
1154:             "Durable mutation receipt",
1155:             duration,
1156:         ),
1157:         Ok(data) if !interrupted => preview_output(data, duration),
1158:         Ok(_) => Output::failure(Reason::Cancelled, duration),
1159:         Err(error) => Output::failure(error, duration),
1160:     }
1161: }
1162: 
1163: fn is_cleanup_uncertain(error: &InspectionError) -> bool {
1164:     matches!(
1165:         error,
1166:         InspectionError::Execution(ExecutionError::CleanupUncertain)
1167:     )
1168: }
1169: 
1170: fn observed_preparation_reason(
1171:     error: MutationPreparationError,
1172:     cleanup_uncertain: &AtomicBool,
1173: ) -> Reason {
1174:     if matches!(&error, MutationPreparationError::Inspection(error) if is_cleanup_uncertain(error))
1175:     {
1176:         cleanup_uncertain.store(true, Ordering::Release);
1177:     }
1178:     preparation_reason(error)
1179: }
1180: 
1181: fn observed_semantic_reason(
1182:     error: rust_engineering_application::SemanticPreparationError,
1183:     cleanup_uncertain: &AtomicBool,
1184: ) -> Reason {
1185:     use rust_engineering_application::{ResolutionError, SemanticPreparationError as E};
1186:     if matches!(
1187:         &error,
1188:         E::Inspection(error) | E::Resolution(ResolutionError::Inspection(error))
1189:             if is_cleanup_uncertain(error)
1190:     ) {
1191:         cleanup_uncertain.store(true, Ordering::Release);
1192:     }
1193:     semantic_reason(error)
1194: }
1195: 
1196: fn reason(error: MutationError) -> Reason {
1197:     match error {
1198:         MutationError::Invalid => Reason::InvalidOperation,
1199:         MutationError::PermissionDenied => Reason::PermissionDenied,
1200:         MutationError::Conflict => Reason::Conflict,
1201:         MutationError::Busy => Reason::LockBusy,
1202:         MutationError::Expired => Reason::PlanExpired,
1203:         MutationError::NotFound => Reason::NotFound,
1204:         MutationError::LimitExceeded => Reason::LimitExceeded,
1205:         MutationError::UnsupportedPlatform => Reason::UnsupportedPlatform,
1206:         MutationError::Cancelled => Reason::Cancelled,
1207:         MutationError::Io => Reason::Io,
1208:         MutationError::RecoveryRequired => Reason::RecoveryRequired,
1209:     }
1210: }
1211: fn preparation_reason(error: MutationPreparationError) -> Reason {
1212:     match error {
1213:         MutationPreparationError::Mutation(error) => reason(error),
1214:         MutationPreparationError::Edit(_) => Reason::InvalidOperation,
1215:         MutationPreparationError::Project(ProjectError::Cancelled) => Reason::Cancelled,
1216:         MutationPreparationError::Project(ProjectError::Internal) => Reason::Io,
1217:         MutationPreparationError::Project(ProjectError::Rejected(
1218:             rust_engineering_domain::OperationalErrorCode::InvalidProject,
1219:         )) => Reason::Conflict,
1220:         MutationPreparationError::Project(_) => Reason::PermissionDenied,
1221:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(InspectionError::Execution(
1222:             ExecutionError::Denied
1223:             | ExecutionError::Unavailable
1224:             | ExecutionError::InvalidConfiguration,
1225:         )) => Reason::ToolchainUnavailable,
1226:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(
1227:             InspectionError::Project(ProjectError::Cancelled)
1228:             | InspectionError::Execution(ExecutionError::Cancelled),
1229:         ) => Reason::Cancelled,
1230:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(InspectionError::Project(ProjectError::Rejected(
1231:             rust_engineering_domain::OperationalErrorCode::InvalidProject,
1232:         ))) => Reason::CandidateInvalid,
1233:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(InspectionError::Project(ProjectError::Rejected(
1234:             rust_engineering_domain::OperationalErrorCode::CommandTimeout,
1235:         ))) => Reason::CommandTimeout,
1236:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(InspectionError::OutputLimit) => Reason::LimitExceeded,
1237:         MutationPreparationError::Inspection(_) => Reason::Io,
1238:     }
1239: }
1240: 
1241: fn semantic_reason(error: rust_engineering_application::SemanticPreparationError) -> Reason {
1242:     use rust_engineering_application::{ResolutionError, SemanticPreparationError as E};
1243:     match error {
1244:         E::Mutation(error) => reason(error),
1245:         E::Edit(rust_engineering_domain::ManifestEditError::Conflict) => Reason::Conflict,
1246:         E::Edit(rust_engineering_domain::ManifestEditError::LimitExceeded) => Reason::LimitExceeded,
1247:         E::Edit(_) => Reason::InvalidOperation,
1248:         E::Project(error) => preparation_reason(MutationPreparationError::Project(error)),
1249:         E::Inspection(error) | E::Resolution(ResolutionError::Inspection(error)) => {
1250:             preparation_reason(MutationPreparationError::Inspection(error))
1251:         }
1252:         E::Resolution(ResolutionError::MissingOfflineData) => Reason::OfflineDataMissing,
1253:         E::Resolution(ResolutionError::InvalidOfflineData) => Reason::OfflineDataInvalid,
1254:         E::Resolution(ResolutionError::Failed) => Reason::CandidateInvalid,
1255:     }
1256: }
1257: #[derive(Clone, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
1258: #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
1259: struct ReceiptChange {
1260:     path: String,
1261:     before_sha256: String,
1262:     intended_after_sha256: String,
1263:     before_bytes: u64,
1264:     intended_after_bytes: u64,
1265:     /// Effect recorded at the terminal journal phase, not current live state.
1266:     effect_after_sha256: Option<String>,
1267:     effect_after_bytes: Option<u64>,
1268: }
1269: 
1270: fn lock_reason<T>(error: TryLockError<T>) -> Reason {
1271:     match error {
1272:         TryLockError::WouldBlock => Reason::LockBusy,
1273:         TryLockError::Poisoned(_) => Reason::Io,
1274:     }
1275: }
1276: fn validate_preview_size<I: serde::de::DeserializeOwned + JsonSchema>(
1277:     contract: &Contract<I, Output>,
1278:     data: &Data,
1279: ) -> Result<(), Reason> {
1280:     let complete = contract
1281:         .encode(preview_output(data.clone(), u64::MAX))
1282:         .map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?;
1283:     if serde_json::to_vec(&complete).map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?.len() > MAX_RESULT {
1284:         return Err(Reason::LimitExceeded);
1285:     }
1286:     Ok(())
1287: }
1288: fn preview_output(data: Data, duration: u64) -> Output {
1289:     Output::new(
1290:         Status::Passed,
1291:         None,
1292:         Some(data),
1293:         "Review this exact diff before commit; source has not been changed",
1294:         duration,
1295:     )
1296: }
1297: 
1298: fn receipt_data(receipt: MutationReceipt) -> Result<Data, MutationError> {
1299:     Ok(Data::Receipt {
1300:         operation_id: receipt.id.as_str().into(),
1301:         validation: validation_view(&receipt.validation)?,
1302:         plan_digest: receipt.digest.to_string(),
1303:         state: match receipt.state {
1304:             MutationState::Committed => ReceiptState::Committed,
1305:             MutationState::NoChange => ReceiptState::NoChange,
1306:             MutationState::Aborted => ReceiptState::Aborted,
1307:             MutationState::RecoveryRequired => ReceiptState::RecoveryRequired,
1308:         },
1309:         files: receipt
1310:             .files
1311:             .into_iter()
1312:             .map(|file| ReceiptChange {
1313:                 path: file.path,
1314:                 before_sha256: file.before.to_string(),
1315:                 intended_after_sha256: file.after.to_string(),
1316:                 before_bytes: file.before_bytes,
1317:                 intended_after_bytes: file.after_bytes,
1318:                 effect_after_sha256: file.effect_after.map(|hash| hash.to_string()),
1319:                 effect_after_bytes: file.effect_after_bytes,
1320:             })
1321:             .collect(),
1322:     })
1323: }
1324: fn preview_diff(candidate: &MutationCandidate) -> Result<(Vec<Change>, String), MutationError> {
1325:     let mut changes = Vec::new();
1326:     let mut diff = String::new();
1327:     for before in candidate.before.files() {
1328:         let after = candidate
1329:             .after
1330:             .files()
1331:             .iter()
1332:             .find(|file| file.path() == before.path())
1333:             .ok_or(MutationError::Invalid)?;
1334:         if before.bytes() == after.bytes() {
1335:             continue;
1336:         }
1337:         if !match candidate.kind {
1338:             MutationKind::ManifestPatch => matches!(before.path(), "Cargo.toml" | "Cargo.lock"),
1339:             MutationKind::FormatApply | MutationKind::FixApply => before.path().ends_with(".rs"),
1340:             MutationKind::DependencyAdd | MutationKind::DependencyRemove => {
1341:                 matches!(before.path(), "Cargo.toml" | "Cargo.lock")
1342:                     || before.path().ends_with("/Cargo.toml")
1343:             }
1344:         } {
1345:             return Err(MutationError::PermissionDenied);
1346:         }
1347:         changes.push(Change {
1348:             path: before.path().into(),
1349:             before_sha256: mutation_bytes_digest(before.bytes())?.to_string(),
1350:             after_sha256: mutation_bytes_digest(after.bytes())?.to_string(),
1351:             before_bytes: before.bytes().len() as u64,
1352:             after_bytes: after.bytes().len() as u64,
1353:         });
1354:         use std::fmt::Write;
1355:         writeln!(diff, "--- a/{}\n+++ b/{}", before.path(), after.path())
1356:             .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1357:         let lines = |bytes: &[u8]| {
1358:             bytes.iter().filter(|b| **b == b'\n').count()
1359:                 + usize::from(!bytes.is_empty() && bytes.last() != Some(&b'\n'))
1360:         };
1361:         writeln!(
1362:             diff,
1363:             "@@ -1,{} +1,{} @@",
1364:             lines(before.bytes()),
1365:             lines(after.bytes())
1366:         )
1367:         .map_err(|_| MutationError::Io)?;
1368:         for (prefix, bytes) in [("-", before.bytes()), ("+", after.bytes())] {
1369:             let text = std::str::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| MutationError::Invalid)?;
1370:             for line in text.split_inclusive('\n') {
1371:                 diff.push_str(prefix);
1372:                 diff.push_str(line);
1373:                 if !line.ends_with('\n') {
1374:                     diff.push_str("\n\\ No newline at end of file\n");
1375:                 }
1376:                 if diff.len() > 128 * 1024 {
1377:                     return Err(MutationError::LimitExceeded);
1378:                 }
1379:             }
1380:         }
1381:     }
1382:     Ok((changes, diff))
1383: }

1394:     fn framed(fields: &[&str]) -> String {
1395:         fields
1396:             .iter()
1397:             .map(|field| format!("{}:{field}", field.len()))
1398:             .collect()
1399:     }

1406:             .expect("test bundle")
1407:         };

1410:             before: bundle(b"pub fn answer( )->u8 { 42 }\n"),

1432:     #[test]
1433:     fn nested_contract_rejects_scope_escalation_and_arbitrary_edits()
1434:     -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
1435:         let contract = Contract::<Input, Output>::new()?;
1436:         let base = json!({"project_ref":"prj_0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef", "action":{
1437:             "mode":"preview","expected_project_fingerprint":format!("sha256:{}", "a".repeat(64)),
1438:             "edit":{"operation":"lint_set","scope":"package","tool":"rust","name":"unsafe_code","level":"deny","priority":null}
1439:         }});
1440:         assert!(
1441:             contract
1442:                 .decode(Some(serde_json::from_value(base.clone())?))
1443:                 .is_ok()
1444:         );
1445:         let mut invalid = Vec::new();
1446:         let mut value = base.clone();
1447:         value["write_root"] = json!("/tmp");
1448:         invalid.push(value);
1449:         let mut value = base.clone();
1450:         value["action"]["confirm"] = json!(true);
1451:         invalid.push(value);
1452:         let mut value = base.clone();
1453:         value["action"]["edit"]["path"] = json!("package.build");
1454:         invalid.push(value);
1455:         let mut value = base.clone();
1456:         value["action"]["edit"]["operation"] = json!("json_patch");
1457:         invalid.push(value);
1458:         let mut value = base.clone();
1459:         value["action"]["edit"]["name"] = json!("unsafe_code\n[package]");
1460:         invalid.push(value);
1461:         let mut value = base;
1462:         value["action"]["edit"]["tool"] = json!("cargo");
1463:         invalid.push(value);
1464:         for value in invalid {
1465:             let error = contract
1466:                 .decode(Some(serde_json::from_value(value)?))
1467:                 .err()
1468:                 .ok_or("accepted invalid input")?;
1469:             assert_eq!(error.code, rmcp::model::ErrorCode::INVALID_PARAMS);
1470:             assert_eq!(error.message, "Invalid tool arguments");
1471:         }
1472:         Ok(())
1473:     }

1509:     #[test]
1510:     fn late_worker_cancellation_revokes_the_undelivered_plan_before_next_admission()
1511:     -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
1512:         runtime()?.block_on(async {
1513:             let workers = Workers::new();
1514:             let request = tokio_util::sync::CancellationToken::new();
1515:             let plans = Arc::new(Mutex::new(SharedPlans::default()));
1516:             let retention = PreviewRetention::default();
1517:             let token = retention.token();
1518:             let id = test_mutation_id(1);
1519:             let digest = test_fingerprint(1);
1520:             let task_workers = workers.clone();
1521:             let task_request = request.clone();
1522:             let task_plans = Arc::clone(&plans);
1523:             let task_id = id.clone();
1524:             let task_digest = digest.clone();
1525:             let (remembered_tx, remembered_rx) = tokio::sync::oneshot::channel();
1526:             let (release_tx, release_rx) = std::sync::mpsc::sync_channel(1);
1527:             let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
1528:                 task_workers
1529:                     .run_joined(
1530:                         task_request,
1531:                         Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5),
1532:                         move |_| {
1533:                             let mut shared = task_plans.lock().map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?;
1534:                             let SharedPlans { plans, clock } = &mut *shared;
1535:                             plans
1536:                                 .remember_revocable(
1537:                                     task_id,
1538:                                     task_digest,
1539:                                     "/workspace".into(),
1540:                                     test_candidate(),
1541:                                     clock,
1542:                                     token,
1543:                                 )
1544:                                 .map_err(reason)?;
1545:                             drop(shared);
1546:                             remembered_tx.send(()).map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?;
1547:                             release_rx
1548:                                 .recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5))
1549:                                 .map_err(|_| Reason::Io)?;
1550:                             Ok::<_, Reason>(())
1551:                         },
1552:                     )
1553:                     .await
1554:             });
1555: 
1556:             tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), remembered_rx).await??;
1557:             request.cancel();
1558:             release_tx.send(())?;
1559:             let joined = task.await?.map_err(|_| "worker rejected")?;
1560:             assert!(joined.result.is_ok());
1561:             assert_eq!(joined.interrupted, Some(WorkerError::Cancelled));
1562: 
1563:             // This is the observable MCP outcome for a completed preview whose
1564:             // cancellation is noticed only after the blocking closure returns.
1565:             let contract = Contract::<FormatInput, Output>::new()?;
1566:             let response =
1567:                 serde_json::to_value(contract.encode(Output::failure(Reason::Cancelled, 1))?)?;
1568:             assert_eq!(response["structuredContent"]["status"], "cancelled");
1569:             assert!(response["structuredContent"]["data"].is_null());
1570: 
1571:             // The response did not carry the preview, so dropping its delivery
1572:             // guard must make the remembered plan unreachable and reclaimable.
1573:             drop(retention);
1574:             {
1575:                 let mut shared = plans.lock().map_err(|_| "plans poisoned")?;
1576:                 let SharedPlans { plans, clock } = &mut *shared;
1577:                 assert!(matches!(
1578:                     plans.resolve(
1579:                         &id,
1580:                         &digest,
1581:                         IdempotencyKey::new("cancelled-preview".into()).expect("idempotency key"),
1582:                         clock,
1583:                     ),
1584:                     Err(MutationError::NotFound)
1585:                 ));
1586:                 for number in 2..=5 {
1587:                     plans
1588:                         .remember(
1589:                             test_mutation_id(number),
1590:                             test_fingerprint(number as u8),
1591:                             "/workspace".into(),
1592:                             test_candidate(),
1593:                             clock,
1594:                         )
1595:                         .expect("revoked plan is pruned before all four slots are admitted");
1596:                 }
1597:             }
1598:             assert!(workers.shutdown(Duration::from_secs(1)).await);
1599:             Ok(())
1600:         })
1601:     }

1603:     #[test]
1604:     fn format_provenance_requires_exact_version_and_terminal_fingerprint_frame() {
1605:         let fingerprint = format!("sha256:{}", "a".repeat(64));
1606:         let base = [
1607:             "m2-fmt-apply-v1",
1608:             "local_coordinated",
1609:             "linux/arm64",
1610:             fingerprint.as_str(),
1611:             fingerprint.as_str(),
1612:             fingerprint.as_str(),
1613:             "1.98.1",
1614:             "1.98.1",
1615:             fingerprint.as_str(),
1616:         ];
1617:         let valid = format!("{}71:{fingerprint}", framed(&base));
1618:         assert!(validation_view(&valid).is_ok());
1619: 
1620:         let mut wrong_version = base;
1621:         wrong_version[0] = "m2-fmt-apply-v2";
1622:         let malformed = [
1623:             framed(&base),
1624:             format!("{}70:{fingerprint}", framed(&base)),
1625:             format!("{}71:{}", framed(&base), &fingerprint[..70]),
1626:             format!("{}71:{fingerprint}x", framed(&base)),
1627:             format!("{}71:sha256:{}", framed(&base), "g".repeat(64)),
1628:             format!("{}71:{fingerprint}", framed(&wrong_version)),
1629:         ];
1630:         for encoded in malformed {
1631:             assert!(matches!(
1632:                 validation_view(&encoded),
1633:                 Err(MutationError::Invalid)
1634:             ));
1635:         }
1636:     }

1639:     fn resolved_provenance_requires_exact_frames_and_preserve_presence_disposition() {
1640:         let fingerprint = format!("sha256:{}", "a".repeat(64));
1641:         for version in [
1642:             "m2-manifest-resolved-v1",
1643:             "m2-dependency-add-v1",
1644:             "m2-dependency-remove-v1",
1645:         ] {
1646:             for disposition in ["updated_existing", "transient_unpublished"] {
1647:                 let fields = [
1648:                     version,
1649:                     "local_coordinated",
1650:                     "linux/arm64",
1651:                     &fingerprint,
1652:                     &fingerprint,
1653:                     &fingerprint,
1654:                     "1.98.1",
1655:                     "1.98.1",
1656:                     &fingerprint,
1657:                     &fingerprint,
1658:                     &fingerprint,
1659:                     &fingerprint,
1660:                     disposition,
1661:                     "member/Cargo.toml",
1662:                 ];
1663:                 let valid = framed(&fields);
1664:                 let view = validation_view(&valid).expect("valid resolved provenance");
1665:                 assert!(view.resolution.is_some());
1666:                 assert!(view.mutation_execution_fingerprint.is_none());
1667:                 for (index, replacement) in [
1668:                     (9, "sha256:bad"),
1669:                     (10, "sha256:bad"),
1670:                     (11, "sha256:bad"),
1671:                     (12, "create_lock"),
1672:                     (13, "../Cargo.toml"),
1673:                     (13, "/Cargo.toml"),
1674:                     (13, "src/lib.rs"),
1675:                     (0, "m2-dependency-add-v2"),
1676:                 ] {
1677:                     let mut invalid = fields;
1678:                     invalid[index] = replacement;
1679:                     assert!(matches!(
1680:                         validation_view(&framed(&invalid)),
1681:                         Err(MutationError::Invalid)
1682:                     ));
1683:                 }
1684:                 for malformed in [
1685:                     format!("{valid}0:"),
1686:                     framed(&fields[..13]),
1687:                     format!("{}99999:x", framed(&fields[..13])),
1688:                     format!("{}1:é", framed(&fields[..13])),
1689:                 ] {
1690:                     assert!(matches!(
1691:                         validation_view(&malformed),
1692:                         Err(MutationError::Invalid)
1693:                     ));
1694:                 }
1695:             }
1696:         }
1697:     }

20: fn lint_preview(opened: &Value, level: &str) -> Value {
21:     json!({
22:         "mode":"preview",
23:         "expected_project_fingerprint":opened["fingerprint"],
24:         "edit":{
25:             "operation":"lint_set",
26:             "scope":"workspace",
27:             "tool":"rust",
28:             "name":"unsafe_code",
29:             "level":level,
30:             "priority":null
31:         }
32:     })
33: }

35: fn commit(preview: &Value, key: &str) -> Value {
36:     json!({
37:         "mode":"commit",
38:         "plan_id":preview["data"]["plan_id"],
39:         "plan_digest":preview["data"]["plan_digest"],
40:         "idempotency_key":key
41:     })
42: }

44: fn reopen(server: &mut Server, fixture: &Fixture, id: i64) -> Result<Value> {
45:     server.send(call(
46:         id,
47:         "rust.project.open",
48:         json!({"path":fixture.project}),
49:     ))?;
50:     let response = server.receive(id, DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT)?;
51:     assert_eq!(
52:         response["result"]["structuredContent"]["status"], "passed",
53:         "{response}"
54:     );
55:     Ok(response["result"]["structuredContent"]["data"].clone())
56: }

58: #[test]
59: #[ignore = "explicit approved Docker runtime/socket on macOS APFS; serial runtime qualification"]
60: fn terminal_plans_free_quota_and_replay_only_from_exact_durable_identity() -> Result {
61:     let _serial = SERIAL.lock().map_err(|_| "serial lock poisoned")?;
62:     let mut fixture = Fixture::new()?;
63:     fixture.assert_clean(None)?;
64:     let original = fixture.source_bytes()?;
65:     let mut server = Server::start_with_grants(&fixture, true, true)?;
66:     let (mut opened, _) = server.bootstrap_open(&fixture)?;
67:     let patch_schema = tool_schema(&mut server, 3, "rust.manifest.patch")?;
68:     let mut request_id = 4;
69:     let mut first_commit = None;
70:     let mut first_receipt = None;
71:     let mut first_after_manifest = None;
72: 
73:     // Five delivered previews and terminal commits in one server process exceed
74:     // the pending-plan quota unless each terminal plan releases its RAM budget.
75:     for (index, level) in ["deny", "warn", "allow", "forbid", "warn"]
76:         .into_iter()
77:         .enumerate()
78:     {
79:         let before = fixture.source_bytes()?;
80:         server.send(patch_call(
81:             request_id,
82:             &opened,
83:             lint_preview(&opened, level),
84:         ))?;
85:         let preview = mutation_output(
86:             server.receive(request_id, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
87:             &patch_schema,
88:             "passed",
89:         )?;
90:         request_id += 1;
91:         assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &before)?;
92:         assert_eq!(preview["data"]["files"][0]["path"], "Cargo.toml");
93:         assert!(
94:             preview["data"]["diff"]
95:                 .as_str()
96:                 .is_some_and(|diff| diff.contains(&format!("unsafe_code = \"{level}\"")))
97:         );
98: 
99:         let action = commit(&preview, &format!("terminal_plan_{index}"));
100:         server.send(patch_call(request_id, &opened, action.clone()))?;
101:         let receipt = mutation_output(
102:             server.receive(request_id, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
103:             &patch_schema,
104:             "passed",
105:         )?;
106:         request_id += 1;
107:         assert_eq!(receipt["data"]["state"], "committed");
108:         let after = fixture.source_bytes()?;
109:         for (path, bytes) in &before {
110:             if path != "Cargo.toml" {
111:                 assert_eq!(after[path], *bytes, "unexpected change to {path}");
112:             }
113:         }
114:         assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &after)?;
115:         assert!(
116:             String::from_utf8(after["Cargo.toml"].clone())?
117:                 .contains(&format!("unsafe_code = \"{level}\""))
118:         );
119:         opened = reopen(&mut server, &fixture, request_id)?;
120:         request_id += 1;
121: 
122:         if index == 0 {
123:             // The response was consumed above, but a caller that lost delivery
124:             // may repeat the exact commit. This must resolve from the terminal
125:             // journal after the in-memory plan has been retired.
126:             server.send(patch_call(request_id, &opened, action.clone()))?;
127:             let replay = mutation_output(
128:                 server.receive(request_id, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
129:                 &patch_schema,
130:                 "passed",
131:             )?;
132:             request_id += 1;
133:             assert_eq!(replay["data"], receipt["data"]);
134:             assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &after)?;
135:             opened = reopen(&mut server, &fixture, request_id)?;
136:             request_id += 1;
137:             first_commit = Some(action);
138:             first_receipt = Some(receipt);
139:             first_after_manifest = Some(after["Cargo.toml"].clone());
140:         }
141:     }
142: 
143:     let first_commit = first_commit.ok_or("first commit absent")?;
144:     let first_receipt = first_receipt.ok_or("first receipt absent")?;
145:     let first_after_manifest = first_after_manifest.ok_or("first manifest absent")?;
146:     let final_manifest = fs::read(fixture.project.join("Cargo.toml"))?;
147:     assert_ne!(final_manifest, first_after_manifest);
148:     assert_ne!(final_manifest, original["Cargo.toml"]);
149:     server.finish()?;
150:     fixture.assert_clean(None)?;
151: 
152:     // Advance the source again after the original operation. Durable replay is
153:     // historical evidence and must not resurrect either its old manifest or any
154:     // other old source byte after a server restart.
155:     fs::write(
156:         fixture.project.join("app/src/lib.rs"),
157:         "pub fn changed_after_terminal_commit() {}\n",
158:     )?;
159:     let advanced = fixture.source_bytes()?;
160:     let mut restarted = Server::start_with_grants(&fixture, true, true)?;
161:     let (reopened, _) = restarted.bootstrap_open(&fixture)?;
162:     let restarted_patch_schema = tool_schema(&mut restarted, 3, "rust.manifest.patch")?;
163:     let restarted_format_schema = tool_schema(&mut restarted, 4, "rust.fmt.apply")?;
164: 
165:     let mut wrong_key = first_commit.clone();
166:     wrong_key["idempotency_key"] = json!("different_key");
167:     restarted.send(patch_call(5, &reopened, wrong_key))?;
168:     let rejected = mutation_output(
169:         restarted.receive(5, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
170:         &restarted_patch_schema,
171:         "blocked",
172:     )?;
173:     assert_eq!(rejected["error_code"], "conflict");
174:     assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &advanced)?;
175: 
176:     let mut wrong_digest = first_commit.clone();
177:     wrong_digest["plan_digest"] = json!(format!("sha256:{}", "0".repeat(64)));
178:     restarted.send(patch_call(6, &reopened, wrong_digest))?;
179:     let rejected = mutation_output(
180:         restarted.receive(6, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
181:         &restarted_patch_schema,
182:         "blocked",
183:     )?;
184:     assert_eq!(rejected["error_code"], "conflict");
185:     assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &advanced)?;
186: 
187:     restarted.send(format_call(7, &reopened, first_commit.clone()))?;
188:     let rejected = mutation_output(
189:         restarted.receive(7, JOIN_TIMEOUT)?,
190:         &restarted_format_schema,
191:         "blocked",
192:     )?;
193:     assert_eq!(rejected["error_code"], "permission_denied");
194:     assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &advanced)?;
195: 
196:     restarted.send(patch_call(8, &reopened, first_commit))?;

200:         "passed",
201:     )?;
202:     assert_eq!(replay["data"], first_receipt["data"]);
203:     assert_fixture_tree(&fixture, &advanced)?;
